//#endregion INPUT DECLARATIONS

//#region MODULE 2 -- UTILITY
// ============================================================================
// MODULE 2 -- UTILITY
// ============================================================================
// Owns: g_atr14
// Reads: (none -- ta.atr only)

g_atr14  = ta.atr(14)

// ATR-14 is the current-period reading; the 20-period SMA is the volatility baseline.
// SMA; delta is normalised distance from baseline.
// ta.sma() requires unconditional global scope when reused across branches;
// na-guard on baseline prevents division-by-zero on early bars before
// the 20-period SMA stabilises.
g_atrBaseline = ta.sma(g_atr14, 20)
g_atrDelta    = (na(g_atrBaseline) or g_atrBaseline == 0.0) ? 0.0 : (g_atr14 - g_atrBaseline) / g_atrBaseline

//#endregion MODULE 2

//#region MODULE 3 -- HTF DATA
// ============================================================================
// MODULE 3 -- HTF DATA
// ============================================================================
// Owns: g_ctxClose, g_ctxClose10,
//       g_msdClose (array<float>[7]), g_msdHigh (array<float>[7]),
//       g_msdLow (array<float>[7]),
//       g_contextBiasDir
// Reads: i_tfContext, i_tfValidation, i_msdTf1..7, syminfo
// All request.security() calls live at global scope per Pine v6 constraint.
// Tuple returns are used for multi-series requests; UDT values are not passed
// through request.security.

// --- Context TF close plus close-10-bars-ago for direction inference.

g_ctxClose = request.security(
     symbol         = syminfo.tickerid,
     timeframe      = i_tfContext,
     expression     = close,
     gaps           = barmerge.gaps_off,
     lookahead      = barmerge.lookahead_off)

g_ctxClose10 = request.security(
     symbol         = syminfo.tickerid,
     timeframe      = i_tfContext,
     expression     = close[10],
     gaps           = barmerge.gaps_off,
     lookahead      = barmerge.lookahead_off)

// --- MSD registry: 7 rows of (close, high, low) tuples. Each row is its
// indexed access per row.

[_msdClose1, _msdHigh1, _msdLow1] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, i_msdTf1, [close, high, low], gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
[_msdClose2, _msdHigh2, _msdLow2] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, i_msdTf2, [close, high, low], gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
[_msdClose3, _msdHigh3, _msdLow3] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, i_msdTf3, [close, high, low], gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
[_msdClose4, _msdHigh4, _msdLow4] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, i_msdTf4, [close, high, low], gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
[_msdClose5, _msdHigh5, _msdLow5] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, i_msdTf5, [close, high, low], gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
[_msdClose6, _msdHigh6, _msdLow6] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, i_msdTf6, [close, high, low], gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
[_msdClose7, _msdHigh7, _msdLow7] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, i_msdTf7, [close, high, low], gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)

// --- Promote MSD tuples to parallel arrays for indexed row access.

var array<float> g_msdClose = array.new_float(7, na)
var array<float> g_msdHigh  = array.new_float(7, na)
var array<float> g_msdLow   = array.new_float(7, na)

array.set(g_msdClose, 0, _msdClose1)
array.set(g_msdClose, 1, _msdClose2)
array.set(g_msdClose, 2, _msdClose3)
array.set(g_msdClose, 3, _msdClose4)
array.set(g_msdClose, 4, _msdClose5)
array.set(g_msdClose, 5, _msdClose6)
array.set(g_msdClose, 6, _msdClose7)

array.set(g_msdHigh, 0, _msdHigh1)
array.set(g_msdHigh, 1, _msdHigh2)
array.set(g_msdHigh, 2, _msdHigh3)
array.set(g_msdHigh, 3, _msdHigh4)
array.set(g_msdHigh, 4, _msdHigh5)
array.set(g_msdHigh, 5, _msdHigh6)
array.set(g_msdHigh, 6, _msdHigh7)

array.set(g_msdLow, 0, _msdLow1)
array.set(g_msdLow, 1, _msdLow2)
array.set(g_msdLow, 2, _msdLow3)
array.set(g_msdLow, 3, _msdLow4)
array.set(g_msdLow, 4, _msdLow5)

// --- Context TF bias direction ---
// Compares current Context TF close against the close 10 bars ago on Context
// TF. When sufficient history exists, classifies bullish (+1) or bearish
// (-1). Neutral (0) when insufficient history or flat movement. An ATR
// tolerance band prevents micro-flipping near flat trends.
// enrich with EMA slope confirmation via MODULE 10 CONTEXTUAL, but the
// simple close-vs-close-10 signal is the canonical Context TF bias proxy

var int g_contextBiasDir = 0

_ctxTolerance = g_atr14 * 0.1
_ctxDelta = na(g_ctxClose) or na(g_ctxClose10) ? 0.0 : g_ctxClose - g_ctxClose10

if not na(g_ctxClose) and not na(g_ctxClose10)
    if _ctxDelta > _ctxTolerance
        g_contextBiasDir := 1
    else if _ctxDelta < -_ctxTolerance
        g_contextBiasDir := -1

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// For each of the 7 MSD TFs, tracks the two most recent CONFIRMED HTF bars'
// (high, low) pairs. On each HTF bar close (detected via ta.change on the
// per-TF close series), the just-closed bar's (high, low) is pushed into
// g_msdSwingHigh[tf] / g_msdSwingLow[tf]. The last two entries are compared
// to produce a structure pattern per the same convention as
// f_structurePattern():
//    +1  HH + HL (bullish)
//    -1  LL + LH (bearish)
//     0  mixed / insufficient history
// This is NOT a pivot-confirmed swing -- it is bar-over-bar HTF structure,
// which is what MSD traders actually read at a glance. Uses zero new
// request.security calls (reuses the _msdHighN / _msdLowN series already
// populated in the MSD block above).

var array<float> g_msdSwingHigh1 = array.new_float(0)
var array<float> g_msdSwingLow1  = array.new_float(0)
var array<float> g_msdSwingHigh2 = array.new_float(0)
var array<float> g_msdSwingLow2  = array.new_float(0)
var array<float> g_msdSwingHigh3 = array.new_float(0)
var array<float> g_msdSwingLow3  = array.new_float(0)
var array<float> g_msdSwingHigh4 = array.new_float(0)
var array<float> g_msdSwingLow4  = array.new_float(0)
var array<float> g_msdSwingHigh5 = array.new_float(0)
var array<float> g_msdSwingLow5  = array.new_float(0)
var array<float> g_msdSwingHigh6 = array.new_float(0)
var array<float> g_msdSwingLow6  = array.new_float(0)
var array<float> g_msdSwingHigh7 = array.new_float(0)
var array<float> g_msdSwingLow7  = array.new_float(0)

// Helper: push new value, keep max 4 entries (FIFO).
f_pushSwingBuf(array<float> _buf, float _val) =>
    if not na(_val)
        array.push(_buf, _val)
        if array.size(_buf) > 4
            array.shift(_buf)
    _buf

// Takes the per-TF high + low buffers and a reference price; returns the
// percentile of reference price within the union range formed by buffer
// min/max. Mirrors the chart-TF SWING H/L formula in Module 19.19 but
// operates on the per-TF buffers populated by Module 3.
// Returns:
//   - na if insufficient data (< 2 entries or flat range)
//   - 0..100 float percentile otherwise
f_msdRangePct(array<float> _highs, array<float> _lows, float _ref) =>
    float _result = na
    if array.size(_highs) >= 2 and array.size(_lows) >= 2 and not na(_ref)
        float _rangeHigh = array.max(_highs)
        float _rangeLow  = array.min(_lows)
        if _rangeHigh > _rangeLow
            _result := (_ref - _rangeLow) / (_rangeHigh - _rangeLow) * 100.0
    _result

// Detects refPrice transitions across the per-TF swing range boundary and
// maintains two persistent flags (reclaimLow / reclaimHigh) for use by the
// SWING 9-segment bar renderer.
// State coding in _statePrev: 0 = inside range / +1 = above swingHigh /
// -1 = below swingLow.
// Transitions:
//   prev = +1, cur = 0  -> reclaimHigh := true  (came back from above)
//   prev = -1, cur = 0  -> reclaimLow := true   (came back from below)
//   cur = +1 or -1      -> clear both reclaim flags (price has left range)
// Also publishes the buffer max/min as g_msdRangeHigh[row] / g_msdRangeLow[row]
// so the render layer can read them by row index instead of dispatching on
// per-TF buffer variables.
// Pine v6 scope constraint: helper mutates only array elements via
// array.set on parameters. No scalar global mutation.
f_updateMsdReclaim(array<float> _highBuf, array<float> _lowBuf, int _row,
                   array<int> _statePrev, array<bool> _reclaimLow, array<bool> _reclaimHigh,
                   array<float> _rangeHigh, array<float> _rangeLow) =>
    float _rh = array.size(_highBuf) > 0 ? array.max(_highBuf) : na
    float _rl = array.size(_lowBuf)  > 0 ? array.min(_lowBuf)  : na
    array.set(_rangeHigh, _row, _rh)
    array.set(_rangeLow,  _row, _rl)
    int _curState = (na(_rh) or na(_rl)) ? 0 : (close > _rh ? 1 : close < _rl ? -1 : 0)
    int _prev = array.get(_statePrev, _row)
    if _prev == 1 and _curState == 0
        array.set(_reclaimHigh, _row, true)
    if _prev == -1 and _curState == 0
        array.set(_reclaimLow, _row, true)
    if _curState != 0
        array.set(_reclaimHigh, _row, false)
        array.set(_reclaimLow,  _row, false)
    array.set(_statePrev, _row, _curState)

// Pine v6 requires globals referenced inside a function body to be declared
// before the function definition -- forward-reference is NOT permitted for
// var globals inside function bodies even though it IS permitted for other
// functions. These constants are consumed by f_msdObProxy below.
// State semantics:
//   OB_STATE_NONE        = 0   no active OB detected on this TF (display: "-")
//   OB_STATE_PRISTINE    = 1   active untested zone          (display: "● Np")
//   OB_STATE_TESTED      = 2   zone has been revisited       (display: "● Np [T]")
//   OB_STATE_INVALIDATED = 3   zone has been closed through  (display: "○ Np")
var int OB_STATE_NONE        = 0
var int OB_STATE_PRISTINE    = 1
var int OB_STATE_TESTED      = 2
var int OB_STATE_INVALIDATED = 3

// Finds the nearest buffered swing high above the reference price and the
// nearest buffered swing low below the reference price. Returns a 4-tuple
// [abovePrice, aboveState, belowPrice, belowState] for parallel-array
// NONE when no buffered pivot exists on that side.
// per-TF OB detector that distinguishes pristine / tested / invalidated
// based on price revisit + close-through state tracking. Display code
// already handles all four states; only this function is swapped.
f_msdObProxy(array<float> _highs, array<float> _lows, float _ref) =>
    float _abovePrice = na
    int   _aboveState = OB_STATE_NONE
    float _belowPrice = na
    int   _belowState = OB_STATE_NONE

    if not na(_ref)
        // Nearest high above ref price.
        int _hSize = array.size(_highs)
        if _hSize > 0
            float _bestAbove = na
            for _i = 0 to _hSize - 1
                float _h = array.get(_highs, _i)
                if not na(_h) and _h > _ref
                    if na(_bestAbove) or _h < _bestAbove
                        _bestAbove := _h
            if not na(_bestAbove)
                _abovePrice := _bestAbove
                _aboveState := OB_STATE_PRISTINE

        // Nearest low below ref price.
        int _lSize = array.size(_lows)
        if _lSize > 0
            float _bestBelow = na
            for _i = 0 to _lSize - 1
                float _l = array.get(_lows, _i)
                if not na(_l) and _l < _ref
                    if na(_bestBelow) or _l > _bestBelow
                        _bestBelow := _l
            if not na(_bestBelow)
                _belowPrice := _bestBelow
                _belowState := OB_STATE_PRISTINE

    [_abovePrice, _aboveState, _belowPrice, _belowState]

// Same Pine v6 forward-reference constraint as OB_STATE_* above -- var
// globals used inside a function body must be declared before the
// function definition. FVG states mirror OB states for display-layer
var int FVG_STATE_NONE        = 0
var int FVG_STATE_PRISTINE    = 1
var int FVG_STATE_TESTED      = 2
var int FVG_STATE_INVALIDATED = 3

// Mirrors OB_STATE_* and FVG_STATE_* for vocabulary symmetry across all
// per-TF zone displays in MSD. Distinct namespace from Module 9's BB
// lifecycle constants. These states track the simplified per-TF display
// lifecycle:
//   NONE    -> em-dash (no MSD display)
//   ACTIVE  -> [UF]   PRISTINE
//   TESTED  -> [PF]   TESTED
//   EXPIRED -> FILLED INVALIDATED
var int BB_STATE_NONE        = 0
var int BB_STATE_PRISTINE    = 1
var int BB_STATE_TESTED      = 2
var int BB_STATE_INVALIDATED = 3

// semantics (body close inside zone, N=2 consecutive opposite-side close);
// FVG diverges (wick-into retest, single-bar invalidation). The enum
// (e.g. BPR) can be added by extending this enum and adding branches inside
// f_advanceObFvgState.
var int ZONETYPE_OB  = 0
var int ZONETYPE_FVG = 1
var int ZONETYPE_BB  = 2

// Detects 3-bar FVG patterns using the existing per-TF swing high/low
// buffers. An FVG exists when wicks of bar N and bar N-2 do not overlap:
//   Bullish FVG: lows[N] > highs[N-2]
//                Gap zone: highs[N-2] (bottom) .. lows[N] (top)
//                Sits BELOW current price as potential support on revisit.
//   Bearish FVG: highs[N] < lows[N-2]
//                Gap zone: highs[N] (bottom) .. lows[N-2] (top)
//                Sits ABOVE current price as potential resistance on revisit.
// Returns a 4-tuple [abovePrice, aboveState, belowPrice, belowState]
// and FVG symmetrically. The "price" is the NEAR edge of the gap -- the
// edge closest to chart close -- which is what a trader actually watches
// as the first-touch zone on revisit.
// per-TF FVG detector that tracks multiple active gaps with mitigation
// state per gap. Display code already handles all four states.
f_msdFvgProxy(array<float> _highs, array<float> _lows, float _ref) =>
    float _abovePrice = na
    int   _aboveState = FVG_STATE_NONE
    float _belowPrice = na
    int   _belowState = FVG_STATE_NONE

    int _size = math.min(array.size(_highs), array.size(_lows))
    if _size >= 3 and not na(_ref)
        float _newH = array.get(_highs, _size - 1)
        float _newL = array.get(_lows,  _size - 1)
        float _oldH = array.get(_highs, _size - 3)
        float _oldL = array.get(_lows,  _size - 3)

        // Bullish FVG (gap below current price -- potential support).
        if not na(_newL) and not na(_oldH) and _newL > _oldH
            // Near edge of the gap (top) is _newL -- closest to price.
            if _newL < _ref  // gap sits below reference price
                _belowPrice := _newL
                _belowState := FVG_STATE_PRISTINE

        // Bearish FVG (gap above current price -- potential resistance).
        if not na(_newH) and not na(_oldL) and _newH < _oldL
            // Near edge of the gap (bottom) is _newH -- closest to price.
            if _newH > _ref  // gap sits above reference price
                _abovePrice := _newH
                _aboveState := FVG_STATE_PRISTINE

    [_abovePrice, _aboveState, _belowPrice, _belowState]

// A Breaker Block is a former opposite-direction zone now traded through.
// This per-TF proxy uses swing-buffer evidence only; strict chart-TF CHoCH
// validation remains in the chart-timeframe engine. Tooltip summary:
//   Bear BB above price (resistance candidate): a former swing LOW that
//   price has broken below -- the low now sits ABOVE current close, i.e.
//   broken support flipped to resistance.
//   Bull BB below price (support candidate): a former swing HIGH that
//   price has broken above -- the high now sits BELOW current close, i.e.
//   broken resistance flipped to support.
// Returns a 4-tuple [abovePrice, aboveState, belowPrice, belowState]
// emission semantics: PRISTINE on detection, NONE otherwise; the state
// machine f_advanceObFvgState advances OB/FVG/BB zones to TESTED or
// INVALIDATED based on price revisit and close-through tracking.
// Per-TF BB proxy: this populator scans the same last-4-pivot swing buffers
// used by OB and FVG. It does not track originating OB index, sweep quality,
// or CHoCH event; those remain chart-timeframe data sources.
f_msdBbProxy(array<float> _highs, array<float> _lows, float _ref) =>
    float _abovePrice = na
    int   _aboveState = BB_STATE_NONE
    float _belowPrice = na
    int   _belowState = BB_STATE_NONE

    if not na(_ref)
        // BB above price: nearest swing LOW that sits above current close.
        // Walks _lows buffer for the smallest low > _ref (closest to price
        // from above). Polarity-swap of OB-above logic which walks _highs.
        int _lSize = array.size(_lows)
        if _lSize > 0
            float _bestLow = na
            for _i = 0 to _lSize - 1
                float _l = array.get(_lows, _i)
                if not na(_l) and _l > _ref
                    if na(_bestLow) or _l < _bestLow
                        _bestLow := _l
            if not na(_bestLow)
                _abovePrice := _bestLow
                _aboveState := BB_STATE_PRISTINE

        // BB below price: nearest swing HIGH that sits below current close.
        // Walks _highs buffer for the largest high < _ref (closest to price
        // from below). Polarity-swap of OB-below logic which walks _lows.
        int _hSize = array.size(_highs)
        if _hSize > 0
            float _bestHigh = na
            for _i = 0 to _hSize - 1
                float _h = array.get(_highs, _i)
                if not na(_h) and _h < _ref
                    if na(_bestHigh) or _h > _bestHigh
                        _bestHigh := _h
            if not na(_bestHigh)
                _belowPrice := _bestHigh
                _belowState := BB_STATE_PRISTINE

    [_abovePrice, _aboveState, _belowPrice, _belowState]

// per-TF / per-side state machine for OB, FVG, and BB zone types. Reads
// the proxy's nearest-level output for this bar and the persistent
// Inputs:
//   _row        -- MSD row index 0..6
//   _zoneType   -- ZONETYPE_OB  (0): body retest, N=2 consecutive invalidation
//                  ZONETYPE_FVG (1): wick retest, single-bar invalidation
//                  mitigation gate)
//   _isAbove    -- true: above-price zone (resistance), close > zoneTop = invalidating
//                  false: below-price zone (support), close < zoneBot = invalidating
//   _rawPrice   -- proxy output for this bar (na = no zone available)
//   _tolerance  -- half-width of zone band (g_atr14 * proximity input)
//   _trackedArr -- tracked price array (mutated): zone replacement detection
//   _stateArr   -- state array (mutated): 0=NONE / 1=PRISTINE / 2=TESTED / 3=INVALIDATED
//   _cthruArr   -- close-through counter array (mutated for OB and BB; FVG
// Zone replacement: when |rawPrice - trackedPrice| > 0.5 ATR, treat as new
// zone and reset state to PRISTINE. Prevents micro-fluctuation from
// resetting state spuriously while still catching genuine pivot rotation.
// Pine v6 scope constraint: helper mutates only array elements via
// array.set on parameters. No scalar global mutation. Multi-line ternary
f_advanceObFvgState(int _row, int _zoneType, bool _isAbove, float _rawPrice, float _tolerance,
                    array<float> _trackedArr, array<int> _stateArr, array<int> _cthruArr) =>
    // FVG diverges from OB/BB on retest detection (wick vs body) and
    // invalidation cadence (single-bar vs N=2 consecutive). OB and BB
    bool _isFvgZone = _zoneType == ZONETYPE_FVG

    if na(_rawPrice)
        // No zone available -- reset all
        array.set(_trackedArr, _row, na)
        array.set(_stateArr,   _row, 0)        // NONE
        if not _isFvgZone
            array.set(_cthruArr, _row, 0)
    else
        float _tracked = array.get(_trackedArr, _row)
        int   _state   = array.get(_stateArr,   _row)
        float _replaceTol = g_atr14 * 0.5
        bool  _isNewZone  = na(_tracked) or math.abs(_rawPrice - _tracked) > _replaceTol

        if _isNewZone
            // Zone replacement -- reset to PRISTINE
            array.set(_trackedArr, _row, _rawPrice)
            array.set(_stateArr,   _row, 1)    // PRISTINE
            if not _isFvgZone
                array.set(_cthruArr, _row, 0)
        else
            // Same zone -- advance state machine within zone bounds
            float _zoneTop = _tracked + _tolerance
            float _zoneBot = _tracked - _tolerance

            // Retest detection (PRISTINE -> TESTED).
            // OB / BB: body close inside [zoneBot, zoneTop].
            // FVG:     wick into zone (high >= zoneBot AND low <= zoneTop).
            bool _retestFired = (_isFvgZone ? (high >= _zoneBot and low <= _zoneTop) :
                                              (close >= _zoneBot and close <= _zoneTop))

            // Opposite-side body close = breaks AWAY from zone:
            //   above-zone (resistance): close above zoneTop = price broke up through
            //   below-zone (support):    close below zoneBot = price broke down through
            bool _oppositeClose = _isAbove ? close > _zoneTop : close < _zoneBot

            // PRISTINE -> TESTED on retest
            if _state == 1 and _retestFired
                _state := 2

            // INVALIDATED transition applies to PRISTINE or TESTED only
            if (_state == 1 or _state == 2) and _oppositeClose
                if _isFvgZone
                    _state := 3                // FVG: single-bar
                else
                    int _cthru = array.get(_cthruArr, _row) + 1
                    if _cthru >= 2
                        _state := 3            // OB / BB: N=2 consecutive
                        array.set(_cthruArr, _row, 0)
                    else
                        array.set(_cthruArr, _row, _cthru)
            else if not _isFvgZone
                // OB / BB: reset counter on any non-opposite close
                array.set(_cthruArr, _row, 0)

            array.set(_trackedArr, _row, _rawPrice)
            array.set(_stateArr,   _row, _state)

// Bar-close detection per TF. ta.change() must be hoisted to global scope
// and evaluated unconditionally. The change signal is cached per TF, then
// consumed by the row update gate.
_msdChange1 = ta.change(_msdClose1)
_msdChange2 = ta.change(_msdClose2)
_msdChange3 = ta.change(_msdClose3)
_msdChange4 = ta.change(_msdClose4)
_msdChange5 = ta.change(_msdClose5)
_msdChange6 = ta.change(_msdClose6)
_msdChange7 = ta.change(_msdClose7)

// When the HTF close series changes value, the prior HTF bar has just
// closed -- push its [1] high/low (the closed bar's values). Using [1]
// prevents capturing the still-forming bar.
if _msdChange1 != 0
    f_pushSwingBuf(g_msdSwingHigh1, _msdHigh1[1])
    f_pushSwingBuf(g_msdSwingLow1,  _msdLow1[1])
if _msdChange2 != 0
    f_pushSwingBuf(g_msdSwingHigh2, _msdHigh2[1])
    f_pushSwingBuf(g_msdSwingLow2,  _msdLow2[1])
if _msdChange3 != 0
    f_pushSwingBuf(g_msdSwingHigh3, _msdHigh3[1])
    f_pushSwingBuf(g_msdSwingLow3,  _msdLow3[1])
if _msdChange4 != 0
    f_pushSwingBuf(g_msdSwingHigh4, _msdHigh4[1])
    f_pushSwingBuf(g_msdSwingLow4,  _msdLow4[1])
if _msdChange5 != 0
    f_pushSwingBuf(g_msdSwingHigh5, _msdHigh5[1])
    f_pushSwingBuf(g_msdSwingLow5,  _msdLow5[1])
if _msdChange6 != 0
    f_pushSwingBuf(g_msdSwingHigh6, _msdHigh6[1])
    f_pushSwingBuf(g_msdSwingLow6,  _msdLow6[1])
if _msdChange7 != 0
    f_pushSwingBuf(g_msdSwingHigh7, _msdHigh7[1])
    f_pushSwingBuf(g_msdSwingLow7,  _msdLow7[1])

// Per-TF structure pattern global array -- populated in Module 4 after
// f_structurePattern() is defined (Pine v6 forward-reference rules require
// the function definition to precede its call site at global scope).
var array<int> g_msdStructPattern = array.new_int(7, 0)

// using the per-TF swing high/low buffers. Stores 0..100 percentile or na
// when the buffer is insufficient.
var array<float> g_msdRangePct = array.new_float(7, na)

// Populated per-bar by 7 callsites of f_updateMsdReclaim (after g_msdRangePct
// callsites). Consumed by f_msdSwingPositionBar in Module 19.1.D via
// f_renderMsdRow.
// g_msdRangeHigh / g_msdRangeLow -- buffer max/min per TF, mirror of what
// the percentile + zone-proxy helpers compute internally; shared row data
// avoids dispatching on per-TF buffer variables inside f_renderMsdRow.
// g_msdRangeStatePrev -- per-TF range state at end of prior bar (+1 above /
// -1 below / 0 inside). Drives transition detection in f_updateMsdReclaim.
// g_msdReclaimLow / g_msdReclaimHigh -- persistent reclaim flags per TF,
// set on outside-to-inside transitions, cleared when price leaves range.
// Read by f_msdSwingPositionBar to select reclaim-from-low vs reclaim-from-
// high visual state.
var array<float> g_msdRangeHigh      = array.new_float(7, na)
var array<float> g_msdRangeLow       = array.new_float(7, na)
var array<int>   g_msdRangeStatePrev = array.new_int(7, 0)
var array<bool>  g_msdReclaimLow     = array.new_bool(7, false)
var array<bool>  g_msdReclaimHigh    = array.new_bool(7, false)

// Four parallel arrays hold per-TF OB-proxy data: nearest zone above chart
// close, nearest zone below, and each zone's mitigation state. The state
// only ever produces PRISTINE or NONE, but the display layer handles all
// lands. The OB_STATE_* constants are declared above f_msdObProxy --
// Pine v6 function bodies cannot forward-reference var globals.

var array<float> g_msdObAbovePrice = array.new_float(7, na)
var array<int>   g_msdObAboveState = array.new_int(7, 0)
var array<float> g_msdObBelowPrice = array.new_float(7, na)
var array<int>   g_msdObBelowState = array.new_int(7, 0)

// Four parallel arrays mirror the OB contract above. FVG_STATE_* constants
// are declared above f_msdFvgProxy. Same forward-compatible semantics --
var array<float> g_msdFvgAbovePrice = array.new_float(7, na)
var array<int>   g_msdFvgAboveState = array.new_int(7, 0)
var array<float> g_msdFvgBelowPrice = array.new_float(7, na)
var array<int>   g_msdFvgBelowState = array.new_int(7, 0)

//   OB:  PRISTINE -> TESTED   on body close inside zone
//        any -> INVALIDATED   on N=2 consecutive opposite-side body closes
//   FVG: PRISTINE -> TESTED   on wick into zone
//        any -> INVALIDATED   on single opposite-side body close
// Tracked-price arrays detect zone replacement: when the proxy's nearest-
// the state machine treats it as a new zone and resets to PRISTINE. Within
// consecutive opposite-side closes for OB invalidation only -- FVG fires
// on a single bar so no counter is needed.
// State arrays g_msdObAboveState / g_msdFvgAboveState / etc. (declared
var array<float> g_msdObAboveTracked  = array.new_float(7, na)
var array<float> g_msdObBelowTracked  = array.new_float(7, na)
var array<int>   g_msdObAboveCthru    = array.new_int(7, 0)
var array<int>   g_msdObBelowCthru    = array.new_int(7, 0)
var array<float> g_msdFvgAboveTracked = array.new_float(7, na)
var array<float> g_msdFvgBelowTracked = array.new_float(7, na)

// Eight parallel arrays mirror the OB + FVG contract above. BB_STATE_*
// opposite-direction zone now traded through" without strict chart-TF
// CHoCH validation. State machine (f_advanceObFvgState via ZONETYPE_BB)
// advances PRISTINE -> TESTED on body retest, -> INVALIDATED on N=2
// consecutive opposite-side close. Tracked + cthru arrays mirror OB
// pattern -- BB inherits OB state-machine rules per D1 mitigation gate.
var array<float> g_msdBbAbovePrice   = array.new_float(7, na)
var array<int>   g_msdBbAboveState   = array.new_int(7, 0)
var array<float> g_msdBbBelowPrice   = array.new_float(7, na)
var array<int>   g_msdBbBelowState   = array.new_int(7, 0)
var array<float> g_msdBbAboveTracked = array.new_float(7, na)
var array<float> g_msdBbBelowTracked = array.new_float(7, na)
var array<int>   g_msdBbAboveCthru   = array.new_int(7, 0)
var array<int>   g_msdBbBelowCthru   = array.new_int(7, 0)

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

//#endregion MODULE 3

//#region MODULE 4 -- SWING DETECTION
// ============================================================================
// MODULE 4 -- SWING DETECTION
// ============================================================================
// Owns: g_swingHigh, g_swingLow, g_swingHighIdx, g_swingLowIdx,
//       g_swingHighArr, g_swingLowArr,
//       g_newSwingHighFired, g_newSwingLowFired,
//       g_swingPatternChart
// Reads: g_atr14 (UTILITY), i_pivotLookbackLeft, i_pivotLookbackRight,
//        i_atrPivotMultiplier, i_showSwingMarkers
// ta.pivothigh / ta.pivotlow -- those produce Pine v6 warnings inside
// conditionals and the clean-room rebuild standardises on manual pivot
// walking throughout.
// A bar at offset _right qualifies as a swing high when its high is the
// strict maximum across the window [_right - _left .. _right + _right].
// Confirmation therefore occurs _right bars after the pivot forms -- the
// standard delay for manual pivot detection.
// The ATR filter requires enough distance from the prior same-direction pivot.

// --- SWING state globals (var declarations each on own line per Pine v6) ---
var float g_swingHigh         = na
var float g_swingLow          = na
var int   g_swingHighIdx      = na
var int   g_swingLowIdx       = na
var array<float> g_swingHighArr    = array.new_float(0)
var array<float> g_swingLowArr     = array.new_float(0)
var array<int>   g_swingHighIdxArr = array.new_int(0)
var array<int>   g_swingLowIdxArr  = array.new_int(0)
var bool  g_newSwingHighFired = false
var bool  g_newSwingLowFired  = false
var int   g_swingPatternChart = 0

// --- Manual pivot detection helpers ---
// strict window extreme. Returns na if not a pivot, else the pivot price.

f_manualPivotHigh(int _left, int _right) =>
    _candidateIdx = _right
    _candidateVal = high[_candidateIdx]
    _isPivot = true
    if bar_index < _left + _right
        _isPivot := false
    else
        for _i = 1 to _left
            if high[_candidateIdx + _i] >= _candidateVal
                _isPivot := false
                break
        if _isPivot
            for _j = 1 to _right
                if high[_candidateIdx - _j] >= _candidateVal
                    _isPivot := false
                    break
    _isPivot ? _candidateVal : na

f_manualPivotLow(int _left, int _right) =>
    _candidateIdx = _right
    _candidateVal = low[_candidateIdx]
    _isPivot = true
    if bar_index < _left + _right
        _isPivot := false
    else
        for _i = 1 to _left
            if low[_candidateIdx + _i] <= _candidateVal
                _isPivot := false
                break
        if _isPivot
            for _j = 1 to _right
                if low[_candidateIdx - _j] <= _candidateVal
                    _isPivot := false
                    break
    _isPivot ? _candidateVal : na

// --- Swing detection on current bar ---

_pivHigh = f_manualPivotHigh(i_pivotLookbackLeft, i_pivotLookbackRight)
_pivLow  = f_manualPivotLow(i_pivotLookbackLeft,  i_pivotLookbackRight)

// ATR filter: distance from prior same-direction swing must exceed threshold.
_atrThreshold = i_atrPivotMultiplier * g_atr14

_highAccepted = not na(_pivHigh) and (na(g_swingHigh) or math.abs(_pivHigh - g_swingHigh) > _atrThreshold)
_lowAccepted  = not na(_pivLow)  and (na(g_swingLow)  or math.abs(_pivLow  - g_swingLow)  > _atrThreshold)

// Reset fired flags each bar, raise only when a new swing confirms.
g_newSwingHighFired := false
g_newSwingLowFired  := false

if _highAccepted
    g_swingHigh    := _pivHigh
    g_swingHighIdx := bar_index - i_pivotLookbackRight
    array.push(g_swingHighArr,    _pivHigh)
    array.push(g_swingHighIdxArr, g_swingHighIdx)
    if array.size(g_swingHighArr) > 50
        array.shift(g_swingHighArr)
        array.shift(g_swingHighIdxArr)
    g_newSwingHighFired := true

if _lowAccepted
    g_swingLow    := _pivLow
    g_swingLowIdx := bar_index - i_pivotLookbackRight
    array.push(g_swingLowArr,    _pivLow)
    array.push(g_swingLowIdxArr, g_swingLowIdx)
    if array.size(g_swingLowArr) > 50
        array.shift(g_swingLowArr)
        array.shift(g_swingLowIdxArr)
    g_newSwingLowFired := true

// --- Structure pattern classification (chart TF) ---
// Classifies the last two confirmed swing highs and lows into:
//   +1 = HH-HL (bullish structure)
//   -1 = LL-LH (bearish structure)
//    0 = mixed / insufficient data

f_structurePattern(array<float> _highs, array<float> _lows) =>
    _sizeH = array.size(_highs)
    _sizeL = array.size(_lows)
    _result = 0
    if _sizeH >= 2 and _sizeL >= 2
        _h1 = array.get(_highs, _sizeH - 1)
        _h2 = array.get(_highs, _sizeH - 2)
        _l1 = array.get(_lows,  _sizeL - 1)
        _l2 = array.get(_lows,  _sizeL - 2)
        if _h1 > _h2 and _l1 > _l2
            _result := 1
        else if _h1 < _h2 and _l1 < _l2
            _result := -1
    _result

g_swingPatternChart := f_structurePattern(g_swingHighArr, g_swingLowArr)

// g_msdStructPattern[0..6] produced from the bar-over-bar HTF highs/lows
// buffered in Module 3. Pushes happen only on HTF bar close (ta.change on
// the per-TF close series), so this is inexpensive on non-close bars but
// must run every bar to keep the array current.
array.set(g_msdStructPattern, 0, f_structurePattern(g_msdSwingHigh1, g_msdSwingLow1))
array.set(g_msdStructPattern, 1, f_structurePattern(g_msdSwingHigh2, g_msdSwingLow2))
array.set(g_msdStructPattern, 2, f_structurePattern(g_msdSwingHigh3, g_msdSwingLow3))
array.set(g_msdStructPattern, 3, f_structurePattern(g_msdSwingHigh4, g_msdSwingLow4))
array.set(g_msdStructPattern, 4, f_structurePattern(g_msdSwingHigh5, g_msdSwingLow5))
array.set(g_msdStructPattern, 5, f_structurePattern(g_msdSwingHigh6, g_msdSwingLow6))
array.set(g_msdStructPattern, 6, f_structurePattern(g_msdSwingHigh7, g_msdSwingLow7))

// g_msdRangePct[0..6] percentile of chart-TF close within each per-TF range
// (buffer min..buffer max). Reference price is current chart-TF close --
// "where is price sitting inside this timeframe's range right now" semantic.
array.set(g_msdRangePct, 0, f_msdRangePct(g_msdSwingHigh1, g_msdSwingLow1, close))
array.set(g_msdRangePct, 1, f_msdRangePct(g_msdSwingHigh2, g_msdSwingLow2, close))
array.set(g_msdRangePct, 2, f_msdRangePct(g_msdSwingHigh3, g_msdSwingLow3, close))
array.set(g_msdRangePct, 3, f_msdRangePct(g_msdSwingHigh4, g_msdSwingLow4, close))
array.set(g_msdRangePct, 4, f_msdRangePct(g_msdSwingHigh5, g_msdSwingLow5, close))
array.set(g_msdRangePct, 5, f_msdRangePct(g_msdSwingHigh6, g_msdSwingLow6, close))
array.set(g_msdRangePct, 6, f_msdRangePct(g_msdSwingHigh7, g_msdSwingLow7, close))

// Updates g_msdRangeHigh/Low + g_msdRangeStatePrev + g_msdReclaimLow/High
// per TF every bar. Runs unconditionally so reclaim state stays current
// when buffers are not pushed (i.e. between HTF bar closes). Helper does
// all the work; main scope just dispatches the 7 per-TF buffer pairs.
f_updateMsdReclaim(g_msdSwingHigh1, g_msdSwingLow1, 0, g_msdRangeStatePrev, g_msdReclaimLow, g_msdReclaimHigh, g_msdRangeHigh, g_msdRangeLow)
f_updateMsdReclaim(g_msdSwingHigh2, g_msdSwingLow2, 1, g_msdRangeStatePrev, g_msdReclaimLow, g_msdReclaimHigh, g_msdRangeHigh, g_msdRangeLow)
f_updateMsdReclaim(g_msdSwingHigh3, g_msdSwingLow3, 2, g_msdRangeStatePrev, g_msdReclaimLow, g_msdReclaimHigh, g_msdRangeHigh, g_msdRangeLow)
f_updateMsdReclaim(g_msdSwingHigh4, g_msdSwingLow4, 3, g_msdRangeStatePrev, g_msdReclaimLow, g_msdReclaimHigh, g_msdRangeHigh, g_msdRangeLow)
f_updateMsdReclaim(g_msdSwingHigh5, g_msdSwingLow5, 4, g_msdRangeStatePrev, g_msdReclaimLow, g_msdReclaimHigh, g_msdRangeHigh, g_msdRangeLow)
f_updateMsdReclaim(g_msdSwingHigh6, g_msdSwingLow6, 5, g_msdRangeStatePrev, g_msdReclaimLow, g_msdReclaimHigh, g_msdRangeHigh, g_msdRangeLow)
f_updateMsdReclaim(g_msdSwingHigh7, g_msdSwingLow7, 6, g_msdRangeStatePrev, g_msdReclaimLow, g_msdReclaimHigh, g_msdRangeHigh, g_msdRangeLow)

// For each TF, locate the nearest buffered swing high above chart close
// (upper zone proxy) and nearest buffered swing low below chart close
// owns Price arrays; State arrays are owned by f_advanceObFvgState which
// runs after these callsites. The proxy's _obAsN / _obBsN tuple fields
// are received but not written here -- the helper computes the real
[_obA1, _obAs1, _obB1, _obBs1] = f_msdObProxy(g_msdSwingHigh1, g_msdSwingLow1, close)
array.set(g_msdObAbovePrice, 0, _obA1)
array.set(g_msdObBelowPrice, 0, _obB1)

[_obA2, _obAs2, _obB2, _obBs2] = f_msdObProxy(g_msdSwingHigh2, g_msdSwingLow2, close)
array.set(g_msdObAbovePrice, 1, _obA2)
array.set(g_msdObBelowPrice, 1, _obB2)

[_obA3, _obAs3, _obB3, _obBs3] = f_msdObProxy(g_msdSwingHigh3, g_msdSwingLow3, close)
array.set(g_msdObAbovePrice, 2, _obA3)
array.set(g_msdObBelowPrice, 2, _obB3)

[_obA4, _obAs4, _obB4, _obBs4] = f_msdObProxy(g_msdSwingHigh4, g_msdSwingLow4, close)
array.set(g_msdObAbovePrice, 3, _obA4)
array.set(g_msdObBelowPrice, 3, _obB4)

[_obA5, _obAs5, _obB5, _obBs5] = f_msdObProxy(g_msdSwingHigh5, g_msdSwingLow5, close)
array.set(g_msdObAbovePrice, 4, _obA5)
array.set(g_msdObBelowPrice, 4, _obB5)

[_obA6, _obAs6, _obB6, _obBs6] = f_msdObProxy(g_msdSwingHigh6, g_msdSwingLow6, close)
array.set(g_msdObAbovePrice, 5, _obA6)
array.set(g_msdObBelowPrice, 5, _obB6)

[_obA7, _obAs7, _obB7, _obBs7] = f_msdObProxy(g_msdSwingHigh7, g_msdSwingLow7, close)
array.set(g_msdObAbovePrice, 6, _obA7)
array.set(g_msdObBelowPrice, 6, _obB7)

// Uses the same per-TF swing high/low buffers that feed OB detection; FVG
// detection looks for 3-bar wick-non-overlap patterns. Reference price is
// chart-TF close -- FVGs above and below are considered independently.
// State arrays are owned by f_advanceObFvgState (callsites below). The
// proxy's _fvgAsN / _fvgBsN tuple fields are received but not written.
[_fvgA1, _fvgAs1, _fvgB1, _fvgBs1] = f_msdFvgProxy(g_msdSwingHigh1, g_msdSwingLow1, close)
array.set(g_msdFvgAbovePrice, 0, _fvgA1)
array.set(g_msdFvgBelowPrice, 0, _fvgB1)

[_fvgA2, _fvgAs2, _fvgB2, _fvgBs2] = f_msdFvgProxy(g_msdSwingHigh2, g_msdSwingLow2, close)
array.set(g_msdFvgAbovePrice, 1, _fvgA2)
array.set(g_msdFvgBelowPrice, 1, _fvgB2)

[_fvgA3, _fvgAs3, _fvgB3, _fvgBs3] = f_msdFvgProxy(g_msdSwingHigh3, g_msdSwingLow3, close)
array.set(g_msdFvgAbovePrice, 2, _fvgA3)
array.set(g_msdFvgBelowPrice, 2, _fvgB3)

[_fvgA4, _fvgAs4, _fvgB4, _fvgBs4] = f_msdFvgProxy(g_msdSwingHigh4, g_msdSwingLow4, close)
array.set(g_msdFvgAbovePrice, 3, _fvgA4)
array.set(g_msdFvgBelowPrice, 3, _fvgB4)

[_fvgA5, _fvgAs5, _fvgB5, _fvgBs5] = f_msdFvgProxy(g_msdSwingHigh5, g_msdSwingLow5, close)
array.set(g_msdFvgAbovePrice, 4, _fvgA5)
array.set(g_msdFvgBelowPrice, 4, _fvgB5)

[_fvgA6, _fvgAs6, _fvgB6, _fvgBs6] = f_msdFvgProxy(g_msdSwingHigh6, g_msdSwingLow6, close)
array.set(g_msdFvgAbovePrice, 5, _fvgA6)
array.set(g_msdFvgBelowPrice, 5, _fvgB6)

[_fvgA7, _fvgAs7, _fvgB7, _fvgBs7] = f_msdFvgProxy(g_msdSwingHigh7, g_msdSwingLow7, close)
array.set(g_msdFvgAbovePrice, 6, _fvgA7)
array.set(g_msdFvgBelowPrice, 6, _fvgB7)

// Mirrors OB + FVG callsite pattern above. Reads same per-TF swing
// derives from broken swing low now sitting above price (former support
// flipped to resistance), BB below from broken swing high now sitting
// below price (former resistance flipped to support). State arrays owned
// by f_advanceObFvgState (callsites below via ZONETYPE_BB). Proxy emits
// PRISTINE on detection, NONE otherwise; transient _bbAsN / _bbBsN tuple
// fields received but not written here.
[_bbA1, _bbAs1, _bbB1, _bbBs1] = f_msdBbProxy(g_msdSwingHigh1, g_msdSwingLow1, close)
array.set(g_msdBbAbovePrice, 0, _bbA1)
array.set(g_msdBbBelowPrice, 0, _bbB1)

[_bbA2, _bbAs2, _bbB2, _bbBs2] = f_msdBbProxy(g_msdSwingHigh2, g_msdSwingLow2, close)
array.set(g_msdBbAbovePrice, 1, _bbA2)
array.set(g_msdBbBelowPrice, 1, _bbB2)

[_bbA3, _bbAs3, _bbB3, _bbBs3] = f_msdBbProxy(g_msdSwingHigh3, g_msdSwingLow3, close)
array.set(g_msdBbAbovePrice, 2, _bbA3)
array.set(g_msdBbBelowPrice, 2, _bbB3)

[_bbA4, _bbAs4, _bbB4, _bbBs4] = f_msdBbProxy(g_msdSwingHigh4, g_msdSwingLow4, close)
array.set(g_msdBbAbovePrice, 3, _bbA4)
array.set(g_msdBbBelowPrice, 3, _bbB4)

[_bbA5, _bbAs5, _bbB5, _bbBs5] = f_msdBbProxy(g_msdSwingHigh5, g_msdSwingLow5, close)
array.set(g_msdBbAbovePrice, 4, _bbA5)
array.set(g_msdBbBelowPrice, 4, _bbB5)

[_bbA6, _bbAs6, _bbB6, _bbBs6] = f_msdBbProxy(g_msdSwingHigh6, g_msdSwingLow6, close)
array.set(g_msdBbAbovePrice, 5, _bbA6)
array.set(g_msdBbBelowPrice, 5, _bbB6)

[_bbA7, _bbAs7, _bbB7, _bbBs7] = f_msdBbProxy(g_msdSwingHigh7, g_msdSwingLow7, close)
array.set(g_msdBbAbovePrice, 6, _bbA7)
array.set(g_msdBbBelowPrice, 6, _bbB7)

// Runs once per bar after the proxies have populated Price arrays. For
// each of the 7 MSD rows, advances the per-side state machine for OB,
// FVG, and BB. Helper writes to the existing display State arrays;
// tracking arrays (g_msdObAboveTracked / g_msdObAboveCthru / BB mirrors,
// declared in Module 3) hold zone-replacement + counter state across bars.
// Tolerance bands derived from input proximity multipliers
// (i_obProximityPct for OB and BB, i_fvgProximityPct for FVG) scaled by
// 42 helper calls (7 rows x 6 sides). FVG counter param uses OB counter
// FVG paths (ZONETYPE_FVG branch). BB uses dedicated cthru arrays.
float _obStateTol  = g_atr14 * i_obProximityPct
float _fvgStateTol = g_atr14 * i_fvgProximityPct
float _bbStateTol  = g_atr14 * i_obProximityPct
for _row = 0 to 6
    f_advanceObFvgState(_row, ZONETYPE_OB,  true,  array.get(g_msdObAbovePrice, _row),  _obStateTol,  g_msdObAboveTracked,  g_msdObAboveState,  g_msdObAboveCthru)
    f_advanceObFvgState(_row, ZONETYPE_OB,  false, array.get(g_msdObBelowPrice, _row),  _obStateTol,  g_msdObBelowTracked,  g_msdObBelowState,  g_msdObBelowCthru)
    f_advanceObFvgState(_row, ZONETYPE_FVG, true,  array.get(g_msdFvgAbovePrice, _row), _fvgStateTol, g_msdFvgAboveTracked, g_msdFvgAboveState, g_msdObAboveCthru)
    f_advanceObFvgState(_row, ZONETYPE_FVG, false, array.get(g_msdFvgBelowPrice, _row), _fvgStateTol, g_msdFvgBelowTracked, g_msdFvgBelowState, g_msdObBelowCthru)
    f_advanceObFvgState(_row, ZONETYPE_BB,  true,  array.get(g_msdBbAbovePrice, _row),  _bbStateTol,  g_msdBbAboveTracked,  g_msdBbAboveState,  g_msdBbAboveCthru)
    f_advanceObFvgState(_row, ZONETYPE_BB,  false, array.get(g_msdBbBelowPrice, _row),  _bbStateTol,  g_msdBbBelowTracked,  g_msdBbBelowState,  g_msdBbBelowCthru)

// --- Chart markers (gated by display toggles) ---
// Plotted at the confirmed pivot bar (i_pivotLookbackRight bars back).

plotshape(i_showSwingMarkers and i_showChartOverlays and g_newSwingHighFired,
     title    = "Swing High",
     style    = shape.triangledown,
     location = location.abovebar,
     color    = color.new(color.red, 30),
     size     = size.tiny,
     offset   = -i_pivotLookbackRight, display = display.pane)

plotshape(i_showSwingMarkers and i_showChartOverlays and g_newSwingLowFired,
     title    = "Swing Low",
     style    = shape.triangleup,
     location = location.belowbar,
     color    = color.new(color.green, 30),
     size     = size.tiny,
     offset   = -i_pivotLookbackRight, display = display.pane)

//#endregion MODULE 4

//#region MODULE 5 PRELUDE -- EVENT ROUTING CONSTANTS
// ============================================================================
// MODULE 5 PRELUDE -- EVENT ROUTING CONSTANTS
// ============================================================================
// EVT_TYPE_* names internal eventType string identifiers
// used by Module 5 STRUCTURE for switch/comparison logic (g_validation
// .eventType assignments + downstream consumer comparisons). Distinct from
// the inline display literals at structure-event chart-label sites (e.g.
// "BOS DISP" with space rendered for chart labels; "BOS_DISP" with
// underscore for internal routing). Routing and display labels intentionally differ.
// This prelude remains the single source of truth for routing identifiers.

string EVT_TYPE_BOS_DISP  = "BOS_DISP"
string EVT_TYPE_BOS_PLAIN = "BOS_PLAIN"
string EVT_TYPE_BB_RETEST = "BB_RETEST"
string EVT_TYPE_CHOCH     = "CHOCH"
string EVT_TYPE_MSS       = "MSS"

var array<int> g_eventLabelAboveCount = array.new_int(1, 0)
var array<int> g_eventLabelBelowCount = array.new_int(1, 0)

f_eventLabelY(float _base, bool _above) =>
    _step = na(g_atr14) or g_atr14 <= 0 ? syminfo.mintick * 12.0 : math.max(syminfo.mintick * 12.0, g_atr14 * 0.16)
    _count = _above ? array.get(g_eventLabelAboveCount, 0) : array.get(g_eventLabelBelowCount, 0)
    if _above
        array.set(g_eventLabelAboveCount, 0, _count + 1)
        _base + (_step * (_count + 1))
    else
        array.set(g_eventLabelBelowCount, 0, _count + 1)
        _base - (_step * (_count + 1))

//#endregion MODULE 5 PRELUDE

//#region MODULE 5 -- STRUCTURE DETECTION
// ============================================================================
// MODULE 5 -- STRUCTURE DETECTION
// ============================================================================
// Owns: g_validation, g_bosFiredBar, g_chochFiredBar, g_mssFiredBar,
//       g_trendDirChart
// Reads: g_swingHigh, g_swingLow, g_swingHighArr, g_swingLowArr,
//        g_newSwingHighFired, g_newSwingLowFired, g_swingPatternChart,
//        g_atr14 (from UTILITY/SWING),
//        i_validationFreshnessBars,
//        i_showBosLabels, i_showChochLabels, i_showMssLabels
// Produces the Validation Event Detector output g_validation (UDT) consumed
// downstream by DELIVERY_STATE, VERDICT_CONT, VERDICT_REV, CONFLUENCE,
//   BOS_DISP -- close beyond prior swing in trend direction, body >= 1.5 ATR
//   BOS_PLAIN -- close beyond prior swing in trend direction, body < 1.5 ATR
//   CHOCH    -- close beyond minor opposite swing (early reversal signal)
//   MSS      -- close beyond major opposite swing with displacement
// Priority when multiple fire same bar (validation event winner):
//   MSS > CHoCH > BOS_DISP > BOS_PLAIN
// indeterminate (pattern = 0), CHoCH/MSS suppress (no baseline to break).

// --- Validation event UDT (declared at global scope before use) ---

type ValidationState
    bool   fired
    int    dir
    string eventType
    bool   stale
    bool   directionConflict
    int    ageBars
    int    anchorBarIdx
    float  anchorPrice

// --- STRUCTURE state globals ---

var ValidationState g_validation = ValidationState.new(false, 0, "NONE", true, false, 999, na, na)
var int g_bosFiredBar   = na
var int g_chochFiredBar = na
var int g_mssFiredBar   = na
var int g_trendDirChart = 0

// Track the major swing levels -- the last confirmed opposite-direction
// swing becomes the reference that CHoCH/MSS break against. In a bullish
// trend, the reference is the most recent swing LOW (breaking it = CHoCH
// of up-trend). In a bearish trend, the reference is the most recent
// swing HIGH.
var float g_minorRefHigh = na
var float g_minorRefLow  = na
var float g_majorRefHigh = na
var float g_majorRefLow  = na

// available (not 0), it is the authoritative trend baseline. When Context
// TF returns 0 (insufficient history on bar 0 or first few bars), fall back
// to the chart-TF swing pattern proxy so BOS/CHoCH/MSS detection still has
// a trend baseline to work against.

g_trendDirChart := g_contextBiasDir != 0 ? g_contextBiasDir : g_swingPatternChart

// --- Maintain structural reference levels ---
// On a new swing high, refresh minor/major high references.
// On a new swing low, refresh minor/major low references.
// Minor = most recent; Major = second-most-recent (sampled when array has >= 2).

if g_newSwingHighFired
    g_minorRefHigh := g_swingHigh
    _sizeH = array.size(g_swingHighArr)
    if _sizeH >= 2
        g_majorRefHigh := array.get(g_swingHighArr, _sizeH - 2)

if g_newSwingLowFired
    g_minorRefLow := g_swingLow
    _sizeL = array.size(g_swingLowArr)
    if _sizeL >= 2
        g_majorRefLow := array.get(g_swingLowArr, _sizeL - 2)

// --- Displacement helper: current bar body as multiple of ATR ---

_bodySize   = math.abs(close - open)
_isDisplacement = g_atr14 > 0 and _bodySize >= (1.5 * g_atr14)

// --- BOS detection (B02) ---
// BOS = decisive close beyond the prior SAME-DIRECTION swing in trend direction.
// Requires established trend (g_trendDirChart != 0).

_bosFired = false
_bosDir   = 0
_bosType  = "NONE"
_bosAnchorPrice = float(na)

if g_trendDirChart == 1 and not na(g_minorRefHigh) and close > g_minorRefHigh and close[1] <= g_minorRefHigh
    _bosFired := true
    _bosDir   := 1
    _bosType  := _isDisplacement ? EVT_TYPE_BOS_DISP : EVT_TYPE_BOS_PLAIN
    _bosAnchorPrice := g_minorRefHigh

if g_trendDirChart == -1 and not na(g_minorRefLow) and close < g_minorRefLow and close[1] >= g_minorRefLow
    _bosFired := true
    _bosDir   := -1
    _bosType  := _isDisplacement ? EVT_TYPE_BOS_DISP : EVT_TYPE_BOS_PLAIN
    _bosAnchorPrice := g_minorRefLow

// --- CHoCH detection (B03) ---
// CHoCH = close beyond the most recent opposite-direction minor swing,
// against prevailing trend. Early reversal signal. No displacement required.

_chochFired = false
_chochDir   = 0
_chochAnchorPrice = float(na)

if g_trendDirChart == 1 and not na(g_minorRefLow) and close < g_minorRefLow and close[1] >= g_minorRefLow
    _chochFired := true
    _chochDir   := -1
    _chochAnchorPrice := g_minorRefLow

if g_trendDirChart == -1 and not na(g_minorRefHigh) and close > g_minorRefHigh and close[1] <= g_minorRefHigh
    _chochFired := true
    _chochDir   := 1
    _chochAnchorPrice := g_minorRefHigh

// --- MSS detection (B04) ---
// MSS = close beyond the MAJOR opposite-direction swing, against trend,
// with displacement required. Stronger than CHoCH.
// The major swing is the second-most-recent opposite-direction swing;
// breaking it means the larger structural frame has shifted.

_mssFired = false
_mssDir   = 0
_mssAnchorPrice = float(na)
_mssDisplacementOk = _isDisplacement

if g_trendDirChart == 1 and not na(g_majorRefLow) and close < g_majorRefLow and close[1] >= g_majorRefLow and _mssDisplacementOk
    _mssFired := true
    _mssDir   := -1
    _mssAnchorPrice := g_majorRefLow

if g_trendDirChart == -1 and not na(g_majorRefHigh) and close > g_majorRefHigh and close[1] <= g_majorRefHigh and _mssDisplacementOk
    _mssFired := true
    _mssDir   := 1
    _mssAnchorPrice := g_majorRefHigh

// --- Validation Event Detector (priority selection) ---
// Priority MSS > CHoCH > BOS_DISP > BOS_PLAIN.
// When multiple fire same bar, the winner populates g_validation.
// When none fire, age the prior event and update stale flag.

_eventFiredThisBar = _mssFired or _chochFired or _bosFired

if _eventFiredThisBar
    _winType   = _mssFired ? EVT_TYPE_MSS : _chochFired ? EVT_TYPE_CHOCH : _bosType
    _winDir    = _mssFired ? _mssDir : _chochFired ? _chochDir : _bosDir
    _winAnchor = _mssFired ? _mssAnchorPrice : _chochFired ? _chochAnchorPrice : _bosAnchorPrice

    g_validation.fired     := true
    g_validation.dir       := _winDir
    g_validation.eventType := _winType
    g_validation.stale     := false
    // when the validation event direction disagrees with Context TF direction.
    // When Context TF direction is 0 (insufficient history), the conflict
    // flag defaults to false (no conflict can be asserted without a baseline).
    g_validation.directionConflict := g_contextBiasDir != 0 and _winDir != g_contextBiasDir
    g_validation.ageBars   := 0
    g_validation.anchorBarIdx := bar_index
    g_validation.anchorPrice  := _winAnchor

    if _mssFired
        g_mssFiredBar := bar_index
    else if _chochFired
        g_chochFiredBar := bar_index
    else if _bosFired
        g_bosFiredBar := bar_index
else
    // No new event -- age the prior, update stale flag. fired resets to
    // false so downstream modules can test "new event this bar".
    g_validation.fired := false
    if not na(g_validation.anchorBarIdx)
        g_validation.ageBars := bar_index - g_validation.anchorBarIdx
        g_validation.stale := g_validation.ageBars > i_validationFreshnessBars

// --- Chart labels for structure events ---
// Drawn at the current bar for immediate visual feedback. Colour-coded by
// direction: bullish events above price, bearish events below. Individual
// toggles per event type.

// eventType identifier stays as compile-stable "BOS_DISP" string. Chart
// labels render user-facing display strings inline.
_bosLabelText   = _bosFired ? (_bosType == EVT_TYPE_BOS_DISP ? "BOS DISP" : "BOS") : ""
_chochLabelText = _chochFired ? "CHoCH" : ""
_mssLabelText   = _mssFired ? "MSS" : ""

array.set(g_eventLabelAboveCount, 0, 0)
array.set(g_eventLabelBelowCount, 0, 0)

if i_showBosLabels and i_showChartOverlays and _bosFired
    label.new(
         x        = bar_index,
         y        = f_eventLabelY(_bosDir == 1 ? high : low, _bosDir == 1),
         text     = _bosLabelText,
         style    = _bosDir == 1 ? label.style_label_down : label.style_label_up,
         yloc     = yloc.price,
         color    = _bosDir == 1 ? color.new(color.teal, 20) : color.new(color.maroon, 20),
         textcolor = color.white,
         size     = size.small)

if i_showChochLabels and i_showChartOverlays and _chochFired
    label.new(
         x        = bar_index,
         y        = f_eventLabelY(_chochDir == 1 ? high : low, _chochDir == 1),
         text     = _chochLabelText,
         style    = _chochDir == 1 ? label.style_label_down : label.style_label_up,
         yloc     = yloc.price,
         color    = color.new(color.orange, 20),
         textcolor = color.white,
         size     = size.small)

if i_showMssLabels and i_showChartOverlays and _mssFired
    label.new(
         x        = bar_index,
         y        = f_eventLabelY(_mssDir == 1 ? high : low, _mssDir == 1),
         text     = _mssLabelText,
         style    = _mssDir == 1 ? label.style_label_down : label.style_label_up,
         yloc     = yloc.price,
         color    = color.new(color.purple, 10),
         textcolor = color.white,
         size     = size.normal)

//#endregion MODULE 5

//#region MODULE 7 -- LIQUIDITY
// ============================================================================
// MODULE 7 -- LIQUIDITY
// ============================================================================
// Owns: g_pools (array<LiquidityPool>), g_sweepState, g_sweepDir,
//       g_sweepQuality, g_sweepBarIdx, g_liqStatus,
//       g_cwState, g_cwStartBar, g_cwDir,
//       g_priorSweepPresent, g_priorSweepBarIdx, g_priorSweepDir,
//       g_nearestBSL, g_nearestSSL
// Reads: g_swingHighArr, g_swingLowArr (SWING), g_atr14 (UTILITY),
//        g_newSwingHighFired, g_newSwingLowFired (SWING),
//        i_lpEqhEqlTolerancePct, i_lpMinDistanceAtrMult,
//        i_lpShowMarkers, i_confirmationWindowBars, i_pivotLookbackRight,
//        i_showChartOverlays
// File-order note: MODULE 7 sits before MODULE 6 because Pine v6 requires
// declarations to appear before downstream consumers.
//   - Pool formation: detect EQH/EQL pools by comparing the most recent
//      swing high/low against existing pools within ATR-tolerance.
//   - Sweep detection: wick penetrates pool level AND close rejects.
//      Distance from swing origin must qualify as meaningful.
//   - Sweep quality grading: depth + speed + rejection-body composite,
//      used by the confirmed-sweep signal for verdict engines.
//   - Prior-sweep tracking: maintains g_priorSweepPresent (opposite-
//      direction sweep within freshness window) for DELIVERY_STATE
//      REVERSAL clause and VERDICT_REV DIR condition.

// --- Pool UDT (declared at global scope before use) ---

type LiquidityPool
    float  price
    int    side                  // +1 = buy-side liquidity (above price), -1 = sell-side (below)
    int    formedBarIdx
    int    ageBars
    int    touchCount
    float  qualityScore          // 0-100 grading at last sweep (0 if never swept)
    bool   swept
    bool   merged

// --- Confirmation Window state constants ---

var int CW_IDLE       = 0
var int CW_ARMED      = 1
var int CW_CONFIRMING = 2
var int CW_CONFIRMED  = 3

// --- LIQUIDITY globals ---

var array<LiquidityPool> g_pools = array.new<LiquidityPool>(0)

var int   g_sweepState      = 0
var int   g_sweepDir        = 0
var float g_sweepQuality    = 0.0
var int   g_sweepBarIdx     = na
var float g_sweepPoolPrice  = na

var int   g_cwState     = 0
var int   g_cwStartBar  = na
var int   g_cwDir       = 0

var bool  g_priorSweepPresent = false
var int   g_priorSweepBarIdx  = na
var int   g_priorSweepDir     = 0
var float g_priorSweepQuality = 0.0

var float g_nearestBSL = na
var float g_nearestSSL = na
var int   g_liqStatus  = 0

// --- Pool formation helpers ---

f_poolAddOrMerge(float _price, int _side, int _barIdx, float _tolerance, bool _mergeEnabled) =>
    _merged = false
    if _mergeEnabled and array.size(g_pools) > 0
        for _i = 0 to array.size(g_pools) - 1
            _p = array.get(g_pools, _i)
            if _p.side == _side and not _p.swept and math.abs(_p.price - _price) <= _tolerance
                _newPrice = (_p.price + _price) / 2.0
                _p.price := _newPrice
                _p.touchCount := _p.touchCount + 1
                _p.merged := true
                array.set(g_pools, _i, _p)
                _merged := true
                break
    if not _merged
        _pool = LiquidityPool.new(_price, _side, _barIdx, 0, 1, 0.0, false, false)
        array.push(g_pools, _pool)
    _merged

// --- Pool formation: check most recent swings against pool tolerance ---

_poolTolerance = g_atr14 * i_lpEqhEqlTolerancePct

if g_newSwingHighFired and not na(g_swingHigh)
    f_poolAddOrMerge(g_swingHigh, 1, g_swingHighIdx, _poolTolerance, true)

if g_newSwingLowFired and not na(g_swingLow)
    f_poolAddOrMerge(g_swingLow, -1, g_swingLowIdx, _poolTolerance, true)

// --- Age pools and prune stale/swept ones ---

if array.size(g_pools) > 0
    _toRemove = array.new_int(0)
    for _i = 0 to array.size(g_pools) - 1
        _p = array.get(g_pools, _i)
        _p.ageBars := bar_index - _p.formedBarIdx
        array.set(g_pools, _i, _p)
        if (_p.swept and _p.ageBars > 50) or _p.ageBars > 200
            array.push(_toRemove, _i)
    if array.size(_toRemove) > 0
        for _j = array.size(_toRemove) - 1 to 0
            array.remove(g_pools, array.get(_toRemove, _j))
    while array.size(g_pools) > 30
        array.shift(g_pools)

// --- Sweep detection ---

_sweepFiredThisBar = false
_sweepFiredDir = 0
_sweepFiredPool = float(na)
_sweepFiredQuality = 0.0

if array.size(g_pools) > 0
    for _i = 0 to array.size(g_pools) - 1
        _p = array.get(g_pools, _i)
        if _p.swept
            continue
        // BSL pool (above price): wick high above pool, close below pool
        if _p.side == 1 and high > _p.price and close < _p.price
            _distOk = (_p.price - low) >= (i_lpMinDistanceAtrMult * g_atr14)
            if _distOk
                _depth = high - _p.price
                _rejectionBody = math.abs(close - open)
                _qual = math.min(100.0, (_depth / g_atr14 * 40.0) + (_rejectionBody / g_atr14 * 60.0))
                _p.swept := true
                _p.qualityScore := _qual
                array.set(g_pools, _i, _p)
                _sweepFiredThisBar := true
                _sweepFiredDir := -1
                _sweepFiredPool := _p.price
                _sweepFiredQuality := _qual
                break
        // SSL pool (below price): wick low below pool, close above pool
        if _p.side == -1 and low < _p.price and close > _p.price
            _distOk = (high - _p.price) >= (i_lpMinDistanceAtrMult * g_atr14)
            if _distOk
                _depth = _p.price - low
                _rejectionBody = math.abs(close - open)
                _qual = math.min(100.0, (_depth / g_atr14 * 40.0) + (_rejectionBody / g_atr14 * 60.0))
                _p.swept := true
                _p.qualityScore := _qual
                array.set(g_pools, _i, _p)
                _sweepFiredThisBar := true
                _sweepFiredDir := 1
                _sweepFiredPool := _p.price
                _sweepFiredQuality := _qual
                break

// --- Maintain sweep state globals ---

if _sweepFiredThisBar
    g_sweepState     := 1
    g_sweepDir       := _sweepFiredDir
    g_sweepQuality   := _sweepFiredQuality
    g_sweepBarIdx    := bar_index
    g_sweepPoolPrice := _sweepFiredPool
else if not na(g_sweepBarIdx) and (bar_index - g_sweepBarIdx) <= i_confirmationWindowBars
    g_sweepState := 2
else
    g_sweepState := 0


if _sweepFiredThisBar
    g_cwState    := CW_ARMED
    g_cwStartBar := bar_index
    g_cwDir      := _sweepFiredDir
else if g_cwState == CW_ARMED
    _ageBarsArmed = bar_index - g_cwStartBar
    if _ageBarsArmed >= i_confirmationWindowBars
        g_cwState    := CW_IDLE
        g_cwStartBar := na
        g_cwDir      := 0
    else
        _confirmedBull = g_cwDir == 1 and close > open and close > g_sweepPoolPrice
        _confirmedBear = g_cwDir == -1 and close < open and close < g_sweepPoolPrice
        if _confirmedBull or _confirmedBear
            g_cwState := CW_CONFIRMING
else if g_cwState == CW_CONFIRMING
    _ageBars = bar_index - g_cwStartBar
    if _ageBars >= i_confirmationWindowBars
        g_cwState    := CW_IDLE
        g_cwStartBar := na
        g_cwDir      := 0
    else
        _strongFollowBull = g_cwDir == 1 and close > g_sweepPoolPrice and close > close[1]
        _strongFollowBear = g_cwDir == -1 and close < g_sweepPoolPrice and close < close[1]
        if _strongFollowBull or _strongFollowBear
            g_cwState := CW_CONFIRMED
else if g_cwState == CW_CONFIRMED
    if not na(g_cwStartBar) and (bar_index - g_cwStartBar) > (i_confirmationWindowBars * 2)
        g_cwState    := CW_IDLE
        g_cwStartBar := na
        g_cwDir      := 0

// --- Prior-sweep tracking for REVERSAL clause / VERDICT_REV DIR ---
// so Reversal DIR clause 3 can enforce the WICK/PIERCE quality filter per

if _sweepFiredThisBar
    g_priorSweepBarIdx  := bar_index
    g_priorSweepDir     := _sweepFiredDir
    g_priorSweepQuality := _sweepFiredQuality

_priorFreshnessBars = i_confirmationWindowBars * 3
g_priorSweepPresent := not na(g_priorSweepBarIdx) and (bar_index - g_priorSweepBarIdx) <= _priorFreshnessBars and g_contextBiasDir != 0 and g_priorSweepDir != 0 and g_priorSweepDir == -g_contextBiasDir

// --- Nearest pool price globals for VERDICT engines and display ---

g_nearestBSL := na
g_nearestSSL := na
if array.size(g_pools) > 0
    _minBslDist = 1e20
    _minSslDist = 1e20
    for _i = 0 to array.size(g_pools) - 1
        _p = array.get(g_pools, _i)
        if _p.swept
            continue
        if _p.side == 1 and _p.price > close
            _d = _p.price - close
            if _d < _minBslDist
                _minBslDist := _d
                g_nearestBSL := _p.price
        if _p.side == -1 and _p.price < close
            _d = close - _p.price
            if _d < _minSslDist
                _minSslDist := _d
                g_nearestSSL := _p.price

g_liqStatus := 0
if not na(g_nearestBSL) and not na(g_nearestSSL)
    _bslDist = g_nearestBSL - close
    _sslDist = close - g_nearestSSL
    g_liqStatus := _bslDist < _sslDist ? 1 : -1
else if not na(g_nearestBSL)
    g_liqStatus := 1
else if not na(g_nearestSSL)
    g_liqStatus := -1

// --- Chart markers: small label pins at pool formation and sweep points ---
// label.new() is the canonical Pine v6 idiom for plotting at absolute
// price levels. Labels are placed only at pool FORMATION events (new swing
// fires) to stay within Pine's drawing budget.

if i_lpShowMarkers and i_showChartOverlays and g_newSwingHighFired and not na(g_swingHigh)
    label.new(
         x        = bar_index - i_pivotLookbackRight,
         y        = g_swingHigh,
         text     = "BSL",
         style    = label.style_circle,
         color    = color.new(color.red, 70),
         textcolor = color.new(color.red, 30),
         size     = size.tiny)

if i_lpShowMarkers and i_showChartOverlays and g_newSwingLowFired and not na(g_swingLow)
    label.new(
         x        = bar_index - i_pivotLookbackRight,
         y        = g_swingLow,
         text     = "SSL",
         style    = label.style_circle,
         color    = color.new(color.green, 70),
         textcolor = color.new(color.green, 30),
         size     = size.tiny)

// Sweep-event markers: larger cross at bar where sweep fires (visible cue
// that the FSM transitioned into ARMED).
if i_lpShowMarkers and i_showChartOverlays and _sweepFiredThisBar
    label.new(
         x        = bar_index,
         y        = f_eventLabelY(_sweepFiredDir == 1 ? low : high, _sweepFiredDir != 1),
         text     = "SWEEP",
         style    = _sweepFiredDir == 1 ? label.style_label_up : label.style_label_down,
         yloc     = yloc.price,
         color    = color.new(color.yellow, 20),
         textcolor = color.black,
         size     = size.tiny)

//#endregion MODULE 7

//#region MODULE 8 -- ZONES
// ============================================================================
// MODULE 8 -- ZONES
// ============================================================================
// Owns: Zone UDT, ZS_FORMED/TESTED/MITIGATED/EXPIRED/INVERTED enum,
//       g_obs (array<Zone>), g_fvgs (array<Zone>), g_bprs (array<Zone>),
//       g_zoneRejectionConfirmed, g_zoneRejectionDir,
//       g_nearestBullZone, g_nearestBearZone
// Reads: g_swingHigh, g_swingLow, g_swingHighIdx, g_swingLowIdx,
//        g_newSwingHighFired, g_newSwingLowFired (SWING),
//        g_atr14 (UTILITY), g_contextBiasDir (HTF_DATA),
//        i_obMaxZones, i_obProximityPct,
//        i_fvgMaxZones, i_fvgMinSizePct,
//        i_fvgProximityPct,
//        i_bprMaxZones,
//        i_showChartOverlays
// Chart-TF detection avoids additional request.security calls and keeps zone
// events aligned with the chart execution context.

// --- Zone UDT (declared at global scope before use) ---

type Zone
    float  priceTop
    float  priceBottom
    int    side                  // +1 bullish (demand), -1 bearish (supply)
    int    formedBarIdx
    int    state                 // ZS_FORMED/TESTED/MITIGATED/EXPIRED/INVERTED
    int    ageBars
    int    touchCount
    string zoneType              // "OB", "FVG", "iFVG", "BPR"
    float  atrAtFormation
    box    boxId
    line   midlineId
    label  labelId
    bool   inBpr                 // True when zone is parent of a BPR pair
                                 // (both parent FVG records flagged at BPR
                                 // formation, any active state). Drives MSD FVG-column
                                 // is NOT cleared when the BPR mitigates/
                                 // expires (historical persistence per
                                 // (a BPR is not "in a BPR" -- it IS the
                                 // BPR).

// --- Zone state enum ---

var int ZS_FORMED    = 0
var int ZS_TESTED    = 1
var int ZS_MITIGATED = 2
var int ZS_EXPIRED   = 3
var int ZS_INVERTED  = 4

// --- ZONES globals ---

var array<Zone> g_obs  = array.new<Zone>(0)
var array<Zone> g_fvgs = array.new<Zone>(0)
var array<Zone> g_bprs = array.new<Zone>(0)

var array<label> g_zoneLabelCollisionIds = array.new<label>(0)
var array<float> g_zoneLabelCollisionYs  = array.new_float(0)

var bool  g_zoneRejectionConfirmed = false
var int   g_zoneRejectionDir       = 0
var float g_nearestBullZone        = na
var float g_nearestBearZone        = na

// --- OB colour constants ---

var color OB_BULL_FORMED    = #26a69a
var color OB_BEAR_FORMED    = #ef5350
var color OB_BULL_TESTED    = #00695c
var color OB_BEAR_TESTED    = #b71c1c
var color OB_MITIGATED      = #616161
var color FVG_BULL_FORMED   = #26a69a
var color FVG_BEAR_FORMED   = #ef5350
var color FVG_INVERTED      = #ff9800
var color BPR_COLOR         = #9c27b0
var color ZONE_LABEL_BG     = #0b1220
var color ZONE_LABEL_TEXT   = #f8fafc
var int   ZONE_PROJECT_BARS = 10

f_zoneBaseColor(string _zoneType, int _side) =>
    _zoneType == "OB" ? (_side == 1 ? OB_BULL_FORMED : OB_BEAR_FORMED) :
     _zoneType == "FVG" ? (_side == 1 ? FVG_BULL_FORMED : FVG_BEAR_FORMED) :
     _zoneType == "iFVG" ? FVG_INVERTED :
     BPR_COLOR

f_zoneStateColor(string _zoneType, int _side, int _state) =>
    _state == ZS_MITIGATED or _state == ZS_EXPIRED ? OB_MITIGATED : f_zoneBaseColor(_zoneType, _side)

f_zoneLabel(string _zoneType, int _side) =>
    _sideText = _side == 1 ? "BULL " : "BEAR "
    _zoneType == "iFVG" ? "iFVG" : _sideText + _zoneType

f_zoneStatusLabel(string _zoneType, string _status) =>
    _zoneType + " | " + _status

f_applyZoneBoxStyle(box _box, string _zoneType, int _side, int _state) =>
    if not na(_box)
        _col = f_zoneStateColor(_zoneType, _side, _state)
        _retired = _state == ZS_MITIGATED or _state == ZS_EXPIRED
        _fillTransp = _retired ? 88 : _state == ZS_FORMED ? 86 : 76
        _borderTransp = _retired ? 50 : _state == ZS_FORMED ? 34 : 14
        box.set_bgcolor(_box, color.new(_col, _fillTransp))
        box.set_border_color(_box, color.new(_col, _borderTransp))
        box.set_border_width(_box, _zoneType == "BPR" ? 2 : 1)
        if _zoneType == "FVG" or _zoneType == "BPR"
            box.set_border_style(_box, line.style_dashed)
        else if _zoneType == "iFVG"
            box.set_border_style(_box, line.style_dotted)
        else
            box.set_border_style(_box, line.style_solid)

f_zoneLabelCollisionY(label _label, float _top, float _bottom) =>
    _base = (_top + _bottom) / 2.0
    _step = na(g_atr14) or g_atr14 <= 0 ? syminfo.mintick * 8.0 : math.max(syminfo.mintick * 8.0, g_atr14 * 0.04)
    _idx = array.indexof(g_zoneLabelCollisionIds, _label)
    if _idx >= 0
        array.remove(g_zoneLabelCollisionIds, _idx)
        array.remove(g_zoneLabelCollisionYs, _idx)
    _span = math.abs(_top - _bottom)
    _preferInside = _span >= _step * 2.0
    _resolved = _base
    _found = false
    for _try = 0 to 6
        _tier = int(math.ceil(_try / 2.0))
        _candidate = _try == 0 ? _base : _try % 2 == 1 ? _base + (_step * _tier) : _base - (_step * _tier)
        _ok = not _preferInside or (_candidate <= _top and _candidate >= _bottom)
        if _ok and array.size(g_zoneLabelCollisionYs) > 0
            for _j = 0 to array.size(g_zoneLabelCollisionYs) - 1
                if math.abs(_candidate - array.get(g_zoneLabelCollisionYs, _j)) < _step
                    _ok := false
                    break
        if _ok
            _resolved := _candidate
            _found := true
            break
    if not _found
        _resolved := _base
    array.push(g_zoneLabelCollisionIds, _label)
    array.push(g_zoneLabelCollisionYs, _resolved)
    _resolved

f_createZoneLabel(int _x, float _top, float _bottom, string _zoneType, int _side, int _state) =>
    _txt = f_zoneLabel(_zoneType, _side)
    _bg = _state == ZS_MITIGATED or _state == ZS_EXPIRED ? ZONE_LABEL_BG : f_zoneBaseColor(_zoneType, _side)
    _tx = _state == ZS_MITIGATED or _state == ZS_EXPIRED ? color.new(ZONE_LABEL_TEXT, 35) : ZONE_LABEL_TEXT
    _mid = (_top + _bottom) / 2.0
    _label = label.new(x = _x, y = _mid, text = _txt, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_label_left, color = color.new(_bg, 0), textcolor = _tx, size = size.small)
    label.set_y(_label, f_zoneLabelCollisionY(_label, _top, _bottom))
    _label

f_applyZoneLabelStyle(label _label, string _zoneType, int _side, int _state, float _top, float _bottom) =>
    if not na(_label)
        _mid = f_zoneLabelCollisionY(_label, _top, _bottom)
        label.set_x(_label, bar_index + ZONE_PROJECT_BARS)
        label.set_y(_label, _mid)
        label.set_text(_label, f_zoneLabel(_zoneType, _side))
        _bg = _state == ZS_MITIGATED or _state == ZS_EXPIRED ? ZONE_LABEL_BG : f_zoneBaseColor(_zoneType, _side)
        label.set_color(_label, color.new(_bg, 0))
        label.set_textcolor(_label, _state == ZS_MITIGATED or _state == ZS_EXPIRED ? color.new(ZONE_LABEL_TEXT, 35) : ZONE_LABEL_TEXT)
        label.set_size(_label, size.small)

f_freezeMitigatedZone(Zone _z) =>
    _mid = (_z.priceTop + _z.priceBottom) / 2.0
    if not na(_z.boxId)
        box.set_right(_z.boxId, bar_index)
        f_applyZoneBoxStyle(_z.boxId, _z.zoneType, _z.side, ZS_MITIGATED)
    if not na(_z.midlineId)
        line.set_x2(_z.midlineId, bar_index)
        line.set_color(_z.midlineId, color.new(OB_MITIGATED, 55))
    if not na(_z.labelId)
        label.set_x(_z.labelId, bar_index)
        label.set_y(_z.labelId, _mid)
        label.set_text(_z.labelId, f_zoneStatusLabel(_z.zoneType, "Mitigated"))
        label.set_color(_z.labelId, color.new(ZONE_LABEL_BG, 15))
        label.set_textcolor(_z.labelId, color.new(ZONE_LABEL_TEXT, 30))
        label.set_size(_z.labelId, size.tiny)
    _z

f_createDashedZoneMidline(int _left, float _top, int _right, float _bottom, color _col) =>
    _mid = (_top + _bottom) / 2.0
    line.new(x1 = _left, y1 = _mid, x2 = _right, y2 = _mid, extend = extend.none, color = color.new(_col, 35), style = line.style_dashed, width = 1)

f_createDottedZoneMidline(int _left, float _top, int _right, float _bottom, color _col) =>
    _mid = (_top + _bottom) / 2.0
    line.new(x1 = _left, y1 = _mid, x2 = _right, y2 = _mid, extend = extend.none, color = color.new(_col, 30), style = line.style_dotted, width = 1)

// --- OB detection (chart TF) ---
// An OB is the last opposite-color candle before a structure
// event (BOS, CHoCH, or MSS). Reuses g_validation.fired + g_validation.dir
// set by the structure detector. When a structure event fires, this
// block searches the preceding 10 bars for the last opposite-color candle
// and anchors the OB there.
// Structure-confirmed triggers ensure each event provides explicit
// close-beyond-reference structure confirmation (BOS = close beyond
// same-direction swing; CHoCH = close beyond opposite-direction swing,
// no displacement required; MSS = close beyond major opposite swing
// with displacement required at >= 1.5x ATR body).

if g_validation.fired
    // structure event. _obDir = 1 for bullish event (needs bearish anchor)
    // and -1 for bearish event (needs bullish anchor).
    _obDir = g_validation.dir
    _searchBars = 10
    int _foundOffset = na
    for _offset = 1 to _searchBars
        _cOpen  = open[_offset]
        _cClose = close[_offset]
        _isOppositeBearish = _obDir ==  1 and _cClose < _cOpen
        _isOppositeBullish = _obDir == -1 and _cClose > _cOpen
        if _isOppositeBearish or _isOppositeBullish
            _foundOffset := _offset
            break
    if not na(_foundOffset)
        _anchorOffset = _foundOffset
        _anchorHigh = high[_anchorOffset]
        _anchorLow  = low[_anchorOffset]
        _obColor = _obDir == 1 ? OB_BULL_FORMED : OB_BEAR_FORMED
        _box = i_showChartOverlays and i_obShowBoxes ? box.new(left = bar_index - _anchorOffset, top = _anchorHigh, right = bar_index + 5, bottom = _anchorLow, border_color = color.new(_obColor, 30), bgcolor = color.new(_obColor, 85), extend = extend.none) : na
        if not na(_box)
            f_applyZoneBoxStyle(_box, "OB", _obDir, ZS_FORMED)
        _midline = not na(_box) ? f_createDashedZoneMidline(bar_index - _anchorOffset, _anchorHigh, bar_index + 5, _anchorLow, _obColor) : na
        _chip = not na(_box) ? f_createZoneLabel(bar_index + 1, _anchorHigh, _anchorLow, "OB", _obDir, ZS_FORMED) : na
        _ob = Zone.new(_anchorHigh, _anchorLow, _obDir, bar_index - _anchorOffset, ZS_FORMED, 0, 0, "OB", g_atr14, _box, _midline, _chip, false)
        array.push(g_obs, _ob)

// --- FVG detection (chart TF) ---
// A bullish FVG is low[0] > high[2] (current low above 2-bar-back high).
// A bearish FVG is high[0] < low[2] (current high below 2-bar-back low).

_fvgBullGap = not na(low) and not na(high[2]) and low > high[2]
_fvgBearGap = not na(high) and not na(low[2]) and high < low[2]
_fvgMinSize = g_atr14 * i_fvgMinSizePct

if _fvgBullGap
    _gapTop = low
    _gapBottom = high[2]
    _gapSize = _gapTop - _gapBottom
    if _gapSize >= _fvgMinSize
        _box = i_showChartOverlays and i_fvgShowBoxes ? box.new(
             left        = bar_index - 2,
             top         = _gapTop,
             right       = bar_index + 5,
             bottom      = _gapBottom,
             border_color = color.new(FVG_BULL_FORMED, 40),
             bgcolor      = color.new(FVG_BULL_FORMED, 88),
             extend       = extend.none) : na
        if not na(_box)
            f_applyZoneBoxStyle(_box, "FVG", 1, ZS_FORMED)
        _chip = not na(_box) ? f_createZoneLabel(bar_index + 1, _gapTop, _gapBottom, "FVG", 1, ZS_FORMED) : na
        _fvg = Zone.new(_gapTop, _gapBottom, 1, bar_index, ZS_FORMED, 0, 0, "FVG", g_atr14, _box, na, _chip, false)
        array.push(g_fvgs, _fvg)

if _fvgBearGap
    _gapTop = low[2]
    _gapBottom = high
    _gapSize = _gapTop - _gapBottom
    if _gapSize >= _fvgMinSize
        _box = i_showChartOverlays and i_fvgShowBoxes ? box.new(
             left        = bar_index - 2,
             top         = _gapTop,
             right       = bar_index + 5,
             bottom      = _gapBottom,
             border_color = color.new(FVG_BEAR_FORMED, 40),
             bgcolor      = color.new(FVG_BEAR_FORMED, 88),
             extend       = extend.none) : na
        if not na(_box)
            f_applyZoneBoxStyle(_box, "FVG", -1, ZS_FORMED)
        _chip = not na(_box) ? f_createZoneLabel(bar_index + 1, _gapTop, _gapBottom, "FVG", -1, ZS_FORMED) : na
        _fvg = Zone.new(_gapTop, _gapBottom, -1, bar_index, ZS_FORMED, 0, 0, "FVG", g_atr14, _box, na, _chip, false)
        array.push(g_fvgs, _fvg)

// --- Zone lifecycle maintenance ---
// Walks both OB and FVG arrays and updates state based on current price
// action. FORMED -> TESTED on first touch. TESTED -> MITIGATED if price
// fully penetrates opposite side. FVGs additionally invert: TESTED -> INVERTED
// when fully filled (opposite side breached), then track touches with
// effective-side rejection logic in f_checkZoneRejection.

f_updateZones(array<Zone> _zones, bool _fvgInversionOn) =>
    if array.size(_zones) > 0
        for _i = 0 to array.size(_zones) - 1
            _z = array.get(_zones, _i)
            _z.ageBars := bar_index - _z.formedBarIdx
