            _projectsRight = _z.state != ZS_MITIGATED and _z.state != ZS_EXPIRED
            if _projectsRight
                if not na(_z.boxId)
                    box.set_right(_z.boxId, bar_index + ZONE_PROJECT_BARS)
                if not na(_z.midlineId)
                    line.set_x2(_z.midlineId, bar_index + ZONE_PROJECT_BARS)
                if not na(_z.labelId)
                    f_applyZoneLabelStyle(_z.labelId, _z.zoneType, _z.side, _z.state, _z.priceTop, _z.priceBottom)

            // Lifecycle transitions
            if _z.state == ZS_FORMED
                // TESTED: current bar touched zone (skip same-bar test for newly formed zones)
                _touched = _z.ageBars > 0 and low <= _z.priceTop and high >= _z.priceBottom
                if _touched
                    _z.state := ZS_TESTED
                    _z.touchCount := _z.touchCount + 1
                    if not na(_z.boxId)
                        f_applyZoneBoxStyle(_z.boxId, _z.zoneType, _z.side, _z.state)
                    if not na(_z.midlineId)
                        line.set_color(_z.midlineId, color.new(f_zoneStateColor(_z.zoneType, _z.side, _z.state), 25))
                    if not na(_z.labelId)
                        f_applyZoneLabelStyle(_z.labelId, _z.zoneType, _z.side, _z.state, _z.priceTop, _z.priceBottom)
            else if _z.state == ZS_TESTED
                // MITIGATED: close fully through zone in opposite direction
                _mitigated = (_z.side == 1 and close < _z.priceBottom) or (_z.side == -1 and close > _z.priceTop)
                if _mitigated
                    // FVG with inversion enabled -> INVERTED instead of MITIGATED
                    if _fvgInversionOn and _z.zoneType == "FVG"
                        _z.state := ZS_INVERTED
                        _z.zoneType := "iFVG"
                        if not na(_z.boxId)
                            f_applyZoneBoxStyle(_z.boxId, _z.zoneType, _z.side, _z.state)
                        if na(_z.midlineId)
                            _z.midlineId := f_createDottedZoneMidline(_z.formedBarIdx - 2, _z.priceTop, bar_index + 5, _z.priceBottom, FVG_INVERTED)
                        if not na(_z.labelId)
                            f_applyZoneLabelStyle(_z.labelId, _z.zoneType, _z.side, _z.state, _z.priceTop, _z.priceBottom)
                    else
                        _z.state := ZS_MITIGATED
                        _z := f_freezeMitigatedZone(_z)
            else if _z.state == ZS_INVERTED
                // iFVG: tracks retouches for analytics. Functional rejection
                // (effective side flipped) fires via f_checkZoneRejection on
                // subsequent bars (age-gated to prevent same-bar inversion-
                // and-rejection double-firing).
                _touchedInverted = low <= _z.priceTop and high >= _z.priceBottom
                if _touchedInverted
                    _z.touchCount := _z.touchCount + 1

            // EXPIRED: age > 100 bars regardless of state (except INVERTED which extends)
            _expiryLimit = _z.state == ZS_INVERTED ? 150 : 100
            if _z.ageBars > _expiryLimit
                _z.state := ZS_EXPIRED
                if not na(_z.boxId)
                    box.delete(_z.boxId)
                    _z.boxId := na
                if not na(_z.midlineId)
                    line.delete(_z.midlineId)
                    _z.midlineId := na
                if not na(_z.labelId)
                    label.delete(_z.labelId)
                    _z.labelId := na

            array.set(_zones, _i, _z)

array.clear(g_zoneLabelCollisionIds)
array.clear(g_zoneLabelCollisionYs)

f_updateZones(g_obs, false)
f_updateZones(g_fvgs, true)
f_updateZones(g_bprs, false)

// --- Zone array pruning ---

f_pruneZones(array<Zone> _zones, int _maxCount) =>
    // Remove expired
    if array.size(_zones) > 0
        _toRemove = array.new_int(0)
        for _i = 0 to array.size(_zones) - 1
            _z = array.get(_zones, _i)
            if _z.state == ZS_EXPIRED
                array.push(_toRemove, _i)
        if array.size(_toRemove) > 0
            for _j = array.size(_toRemove) - 1 to 0
                array.remove(_zones, array.get(_toRemove, _j))
    // Enforce cap
    while array.size(_zones) > _maxCount
        _oldest = array.shift(_zones)
        if not na(_oldest.boxId)
            box.delete(_oldest.boxId)
        if not na(_oldest.midlineId)
            line.delete(_oldest.midlineId)
        if not na(_oldest.labelId)
            label.delete(_oldest.labelId)

f_pruneZones(g_obs, i_obMaxZones)
f_pruneZones(g_fvgs, i_fvgMaxZones)
f_pruneZones(g_bprs, i_bprMaxZones)

// BPR = new FVG that overlaps an existing active (FORMED/TESTED/INVERTED)
// FVG of opposite original direction by >= 50%. Walks g_fvgs looking for
// any active opposite-side pairing. Per mainstream ICT methodology, BPR
// is the overlap of two opposing FVGs (one bullish, one bearish); both
// halves contribute unfilled imbalance to the resulting zone.
// variant) to active-state (mainstream Definition 1). Increases BPR
// signal count, aligns with community ICT indicators.

if (_fvgBullGap or _fvgBearGap) and array.size(g_fvgs) > 0
    _newSize = array.size(g_fvgs)
    if _newSize >= 2
        _newest = array.get(g_fvgs, _newSize - 1)
        if _newest.formedBarIdx == bar_index and _newest.zoneType == "FVG"
            for _i = 0 to _newSize - 2
                _prior = array.get(g_fvgs, _i)
                if (_prior.state == ZS_FORMED or _prior.state == ZS_TESTED or _prior.state == ZS_INVERTED) and _prior.side != _newest.side
                    // Compute overlap
                    _overlapTop = math.min(_newest.priceTop, _prior.priceTop)
                    _overlapBot = math.max(_newest.priceBottom, _prior.priceBottom)
                    if _overlapTop > _overlapBot
                        _overlapSize = _overlapTop - _overlapBot
                        _newestSize = _newest.priceTop - _newest.priceBottom
                        _priorSize = _prior.priceTop - _prior.priceBottom
                        _minSize = math.min(_newestSize, _priorSize)
                        _overlapPct = _minSize > 0 ? (_overlapSize / _minSize) : 0.0
                        if _overlapPct >= 0.5
                            // BPR no longer renders as a standalone chart object.
                            // (chart-TF row only). On-chart parent FVG/iFVG
                            // zone styling integration). The Zone record is
                            // retained for confluence factor firing, delivery
                            // state feed, and array lifecycle (g_bprs lifecycle
                            // now properly updated by Edit Group C).
                            _bpr = Zone.new(_overlapTop, _overlapBot, _newest.side, bar_index, ZS_FORMED, 0, 0, "BPR", g_atr14, na, na, na, false)
                            array.push(g_bprs, _bpr)

                            // Propagate inBpr flag to both parent FVG records
                            // (any active state). Both halves of the BPR pair are visually
                            // elevated. Pine v6 UDT mutation requires
                            // array.set after .field := mutation to ensure
                            // the change is reflected in subsequent reads.
                            _newest.inBpr := true
                            _prior.inBpr := true
                            array.set(g_fvgs, _newSize - 1, _newest)
                            array.set(g_fvgs, _i, _prior)
                            break

// --- Zone rejection detection (resolves _zoneRejectionConfirmed) ---
// Fires when current bar touches an active bull zone AND closes higher
// (bullish rejection), OR touches a bear zone AND closes lower (bearish
// rejection), in the direction of Context TF bias.

f_checkZoneRejection(array<Zone> _zones, int _biasDir) =>
    _fired = false
    _dir = 0
    if _biasDir != 0 and array.size(_zones) > 0
        for _i = 0 to array.size(_zones) - 1
            _z = array.get(_zones, _i)
            if _z.state == ZS_EXPIRED or _z.state == ZS_MITIGATED
                continue
            // Effective direction: inverted FVGs function opposite their stored side.
            // Stored side remains original FVG direction for BPR detection.
            _effectiveSide = _z.state == ZS_INVERTED ? -_z.side : _z.side
            // Bullish rejection: bias=bull, touched bull zone, close above open.
            // not just ZS_INVERTED. Reason: ICT/SMC methodology is form-first-
            // retest-later -- FVG/BPR cannot validly fire same-bar rejection on
            // their own formation bar (touch condition trivially satisfied by
            // the forming zone boundary already equals the current wick). OB unaffected
            // because formedBarIdx = bar_index - _anchorOffset already produces
            // ageBars > 0. iFVG age-gate intent (1 bar after inversion) preserved
            // mechanically; age-since-original-formation semantic earmarked for
            if _biasDir == 1 and _effectiveSide == 1 and _z.ageBars > 0 and low <= _z.priceTop and close > open and close > _z.priceBottom
                _fired := true
                _dir := 1
                break
            // Bearish rejection: bias=bear, touched bear zone, close below open.
            if _biasDir == -1 and _effectiveSide == -1 and _z.ageBars > 0 and high >= _z.priceBottom and close < open and close < _z.priceTop
                _fired := true
                _dir := -1
                break
    [_fired, _dir]

[_obRejFired, _obRejDir] = f_checkZoneRejection(g_obs, g_contextBiasDir)
[_fvgRejFired, _fvgRejDir] = f_checkZoneRejection(g_fvgs, g_contextBiasDir)
[_bprRejFired, _bprRejDir] = f_checkZoneRejection(g_bprs, g_contextBiasDir)

g_zoneRejectionConfirmed := _obRejFired or _fvgRejFired or _bprRejFired
g_zoneRejectionDir := _obRejFired ? _obRejDir : _fvgRejFired ? _fvgRejDir : _bprRejFired ? _bprRejDir : 0

// --- Nearest-zone globals for VERDICT engines (proximity scoring) ---

g_nearestBullZone := na
g_nearestBearZone := na
_minBullDist = 1e20
_minBearDist = 1e20

if array.size(g_obs) > 0
    for _i = 0 to array.size(g_obs) - 1
        _z = array.get(g_obs, _i)
        if _z.state == ZS_EXPIRED or _z.state == ZS_MITIGATED
            continue
        _mid = (_z.priceTop + _z.priceBottom) / 2.0
        _d = math.abs(close - _mid)
        if _z.side == 1 and _d < _minBullDist
            _minBullDist := _d
            g_nearestBullZone := _mid
        if _z.side == -1 and _d < _minBearDist
            _minBearDist := _d
            g_nearestBearZone := _mid

if array.size(g_fvgs) > 0
    for _i = 0 to array.size(g_fvgs) - 1
        _z = array.get(g_fvgs, _i)
        if _z.state == ZS_EXPIRED or _z.state == ZS_MITIGATED
            continue
        _mid = (_z.priceTop + _z.priceBottom) / 2.0
        _d = math.abs(close - _mid)
        if _z.side == 1 and _d < _minBullDist
            _minBullDist := _d
            g_nearestBullZone := _mid
        if _z.side == -1 and _d < _minBearDist
            _minBearDist := _d
            g_nearestBearZone := _mid

//#endregion MODULE 8

//#region MODULE 9 -- BREAKER BLOCKS
// ============================================================================
// MODULE 9 -- BREAKER BLOCKS
// ============================================================================
// Owns: BbRecord UDT, BB state enum, g_bbs (array<BbRecord>),
//       g_bbRetestFired, g_bbRetestDir, g_bbRetestPrice
// Extends: g_validation.eventType to include "BB_RETEST",
//          g_zoneRejectionConfirmed to include BB zone rejections
// Reads: g_obs (ZONES), g_validation (STRUCTURE), g_sweepDir, g_sweepBarIdx,
//        g_sweepPoolPrice, g_sweepQuality (LIQUIDITY), g_atr14 (UTILITY),
//        g_contextBiasDir (HTF_DATA), i_bbRequireChoch,
//        i_bbMaxZones, i_bbShowLabels, i_obProximityPct,
//        i_showChartOverlays
// Lifecycle:
//   CANDIDATE -- OB that was swept (LiqScan sweep at OB swing level)
//   CONFIRMED_BB -- CHoCH in opposite direction within 5 bars of sweep
//   ACTIVE    -- persistence state post-formation, awaiting retest
//   TESTED    -- price retested zone with qualifying rejection
//   EXPIRED   -- full penetration, second close through, 100-bar age,
//                or 5x ATR invalidation distance exceeded
// OB/BB mutex: when OB enters BB lifecycle (transitions through SWEPT),
// the originating OB's state is force-set to MITIGATED so it no longer
// contributes to OB confluence / zone-rejection / nearest-zone. Once

// --- BbRecord UDT ---

type BbRecord
    int    obIdx             // index snapshot of originating OB in g_obs (at candidacy time)
    int    state             // BB_CANDIDATE etc.
    int    side              // +1 bull BB (originated from bear OB), -1 bear BB (from bull OB)
    float  priceTop
    float  priceBottom
    int    candidateBarIdx
    int    sweepBarIdx
    int    chochBarIdx
    int    formationBarIdx   // bar where state -> CONFIRMED_BB
    int    lastEventBar
    int    touchCount
    box    boxId
    label  labelId

// --- BB state enum ---

var int BB_CANDIDATE    = 0
var int BB_SWEPT        = 1
var int BB_CONFIRMED_BB = 2
var int BB_ACTIVE       = 3
var int BB_TESTED       = 4
var int BB_EXPIRED      = 5


var int   BB_CHOCH_WINDOW   = 5
var int   BB_FAIL_WINDOW    = 10
var int   BB_MAX_AGE        = 100
var float BB_INVAL_ATR_MULT = 5.0


var color BB_BULL_COLOR = #0F4F3E     // rich teal (bull BB)
var color BB_BEAR_COLOR = #7F1D1D     // rich coral (bear BB)

f_applyBbBoxStyle(box _box, int _side, int _state) =>
    if not na(_box)
        _col = _side == 1 ? BB_BULL_COLOR : BB_BEAR_COLOR
        _fillTransp = _state == BB_TESTED ? 48 : 60
        box.set_bgcolor(_box, color.new(_col, _fillTransp))
        box.set_border_color(_box, color.new(_col, 0))
        box.set_border_width(_box, 2)
        box.set_border_style(_box, line.style_solid)

f_bbLabel(int _side) =>
    (_side == 1 ? "BULL " : "BEAR ") + "BB"

f_createBbLabel(int _x, float _top, float _bottom, int _side, int _state) =>
    _bg = _side == 1 ? BB_BULL_COLOR : BB_BEAR_COLOR
    _mid = (_top + _bottom) / 2.0
    _label = label.new(x = _x, y = _mid, text = f_bbLabel(_side), yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_label_left, color = color.new(_bg, 0), textcolor = ZONE_LABEL_TEXT, size = size.small)
    label.set_y(_label, f_zoneLabelCollisionY(_label, _top, _bottom))
    _label

f_applyBbLabelStyle(label _label, int _side, int _state, float _top, float _bottom) =>
    if not na(_label)
        _mid = f_zoneLabelCollisionY(_label, _top, _bottom)
        label.set_x(_label, bar_index + ZONE_PROJECT_BARS)
        label.set_y(_label, _mid)
        label.set_text(_label, f_bbLabel(_side))
        _bg = _side == 1 ? BB_BULL_COLOR : BB_BEAR_COLOR
        label.set_color(_label, color.new(_bg, 0))
        label.set_textcolor(_label, ZONE_LABEL_TEXT)
        label.set_size(_label, size.small)

// --- BB globals ---

var array<BbRecord> g_bbs = array.new<BbRecord>(0)

var bool  g_bbRetestFired = false
var int   g_bbRetestDir   = 0
var float g_bbRetestPrice = na

// --- BB candidacy: a sweep at an OB's price level promotes OB to CANDIDATE ---
// This fires from the LIQUIDITY sweep event. When the swept pool price
// matches an existing OB's price range (within ATR tolerance), that OB
// enters the BB lifecycle as CANDIDATE.

if _sweepFiredThisBar and array.size(g_obs) > 0
    _sweepTolerance = g_atr14 * 0.3
    for _i = 0 to array.size(g_obs) - 1
        _ob = array.get(g_obs, _i)
        if _ob.state == ZS_EXPIRED or _ob.state == ZS_MITIGATED
            continue
        // Bullish sweep (SSL swept): check bull OBs near the sweep price
        _bullMatch = _sweepFiredDir == 1 and _ob.side == 1 and _sweepFiredPool >= _ob.priceBottom - _sweepTolerance and _sweepFiredPool <= _ob.priceTop + _sweepTolerance
        // Bearish sweep (BSL swept): check bear OBs near the sweep price
        _bearMatch = _sweepFiredDir == -1 and _ob.side == -1 and _sweepFiredPool >= _ob.priceBottom - _sweepTolerance and _sweepFiredPool <= _ob.priceTop + _sweepTolerance
        if _bullMatch or _bearMatch
            // Check the OB isn't already in a BB lifecycle
            _alreadyTracked = false
            if array.size(g_bbs) > 0
                for _j = 0 to array.size(g_bbs) - 1
                    _bb = array.get(g_bbs, _j)
                    if _bb.obIdx == _i and _bb.state != BB_EXPIRED
                        _alreadyTracked := true
                        break
            if not _alreadyTracked
                // (0-100). BREAK-class sweeps tend to have very high depth and
                // weak rejection; WICK/PIERCE have balanced depth+rejection.
                _qualityOk = g_sweepQuality >= 30.0
                if _qualityOk
                    // BB side is OPPOSITE to the OB side (character flip)
                    _bbSide = -_ob.side
                    _newBb = BbRecord.new(_i, BB_CANDIDATE, _bbSide,
                         _ob.priceTop, _ob.priceBottom,
                         bar_index, bar_index, na, na, bar_index, 0, na, na)
                    array.push(g_bbs, _newBb)

// --- BB state machine ---

f_advanceBbState() =>
    if array.size(g_bbs) > 0
        for _i = 0 to array.size(g_bbs) - 1
            _bb = array.get(g_bbs, _i)
            if _bb.state == BB_EXPIRED
                continue

            _sinceCandidate = bar_index - _bb.candidateBarIdx
            _sinceSweep = na(_bb.sweepBarIdx) ? 999 : (bar_index - _bb.sweepBarIdx)
            if not na(_bb.boxId)
                box.set_right(_bb.boxId, bar_index + ZONE_PROJECT_BARS)
            if not na(_bb.labelId)
                f_applyBbLabelStyle(_bb.labelId, _bb.side, _bb.state, _bb.priceTop, _bb.priceBottom)

            // CANDIDATE -> SWEPT: same bar as sweep (candidacy already created
            // with sweepBarIdx=candidateBarIdx). Immediate transition on next bar.
            if _bb.state == BB_CANDIDATE and _sinceCandidate >= 1
                _bb.state := BB_SWEPT
                _bb.lastEventBar := bar_index

            // SWEPT -> CONFIRMED_BB: CHoCH in opposite direction to original OB
            // within BB_CHOCH_WINDOW bars of sweep. When i_bbRequireChoch is
            // off, displacement BOS_DISP substitutes for CHoCH.
            if _bb.state == BB_SWEPT and _sinceSweep <= BB_CHOCH_WINDOW
                _chochMatch = g_validation.fired and g_validation.eventType == EVT_TYPE_CHOCH and g_validation.dir == _bb.side
                _altMatch = not i_bbRequireChoch and g_validation.fired and g_validation.eventType == EVT_TYPE_BOS_DISP and g_validation.dir == _bb.side
                if _chochMatch or _altMatch
                    _bb.state := BB_CONFIRMED_BB
                    _bb.chochBarIdx := bar_index
                    _bb.formationBarIdx := bar_index
                    _bb.lastEventBar := bar_index
                    // OB/BB mutex: retire the originating OB
                    if _bb.obIdx >= 0 and _bb.obIdx < array.size(g_obs)
                        _origOb = array.get(g_obs, _bb.obIdx)
                        _origOb.state := ZS_MITIGATED
                        _origOb := f_freezeMitigatedZone(_origOb)
                        array.set(g_obs, _bb.obIdx, _origOb)
                    // Draw BB box in richer colour
                    if i_showChartOverlays and i_bbShowBoxes
                        _col = _bb.side == 1 ? BB_BULL_COLOR : BB_BEAR_COLOR
                        _bb.boxId := box.new(
                             left        = _bb.candidateBarIdx,
                             top         = _bb.priceTop,
                             right       = bar_index + 10,
                             bottom      = _bb.priceBottom,
                             border_color = _col,
                             border_width = 2,
                              bgcolor      = color.new(_col, 60),
                              extend       = extend.none)
                        f_applyBbBoxStyle(_bb.boxId, _bb.side, _bb.state)
                    if i_bbShowLabels and i_showChartOverlays
                        _bb.labelId := f_createBbLabel(bar_index + 1, _bb.priceTop, _bb.priceBottom, _bb.side, _bb.state)

            // SWEPT -> EXPIRED: no CHoCH within BB_FAIL_WINDOW
            if _bb.state == BB_SWEPT and _sinceSweep > BB_FAIL_WINDOW
                _bb.state := BB_EXPIRED
                _bb.lastEventBar := bar_index
                if not na(_bb.boxId)
                    box.delete(_bb.boxId)
                    _bb.boxId := na
                if not na(_bb.labelId)
                    label.delete(_bb.labelId)
                    _bb.labelId := na

            // CONFIRMED_BB -> ACTIVE: automatic on next bar after CHoCH
            if _bb.state == BB_CONFIRMED_BB and (bar_index - _bb.formationBarIdx) >= 1
                _bb.state := BB_ACTIVE
                _bb.lastEventBar := bar_index

            // ACTIVE -> TESTED: price enters zone with qualifying close
            if _bb.state == BB_ACTIVE
                _inZone = low <= _bb.priceTop and high >= _bb.priceBottom
                if _inZone
                    // Qualifying reaction: close in bias direction (same as BB side)
                    _bullReject = _bb.side == 1 and close > open and close > _bb.priceBottom
                    _bearReject = _bb.side == -1 and close < open and close < _bb.priceTop
                    if _bullReject or _bearReject
                        _bb.state := BB_TESTED
                        _bb.touchCount := _bb.touchCount + 1
                        _bb.lastEventBar := bar_index
                        // BB_RETEST validation event emission happens at MAIN
                        // SCRIPT SCOPE post-call (see below f_advanceBbState).
                        // Pine v6 does NOT permit scalar global mutation from
                        // inside functions, so UDT-reference field
                        // mutation is also avoided here; the
                        // post-call block handles both g_validation emission
                        // and the g_bbRetestX bar-level flags.
                        if not na(_bb.boxId)
                            // Brighten the tested zone
                            f_applyBbBoxStyle(_bb.boxId, _bb.side, _bb.state)
                        if not na(_bb.labelId)
                            f_applyBbLabelStyle(_bb.labelId, _bb.side, _bb.state, _bb.priceTop, _bb.priceBottom)

            if _bb.state == BB_ACTIVE or _bb.state == BB_TESTED
                _ageBars = bar_index - _bb.formationBarIdx
                // Full penetration: close fully through zone in opposite direction
                _fullPenetration = (_bb.side == 1 and close < _bb.priceBottom) or (_bb.side == -1 and close > _bb.priceTop)
                // Invalidation distance: 5x ATR movement in opposite direction
                _invalidationDist = g_atr14 * BB_INVAL_ATR_MULT
                _invalidated = (_bb.side == 1 and close < (_bb.priceBottom - _invalidationDist)) or (_bb.side == -1 and close > (_bb.priceTop + _invalidationDist))
                // Second close through after TESTED: exhaustion
                _secondClose = _bb.state == BB_TESTED and _fullPenetration
                if _fullPenetration or _invalidated or _ageBars > BB_MAX_AGE or _secondClose
                    _bb.state := BB_EXPIRED
                    _bb.lastEventBar := bar_index
                    if not na(_bb.boxId)
                        box.delete(_bb.boxId)
                        _bb.boxId := na
                    if not na(_bb.labelId)
                        label.delete(_bb.labelId)
                        _bb.labelId := na

            array.set(g_bbs, _i, _bb)

// --- Advance the BB state machine, then derive bar-level flags ---
// Pine v6 does not permit mutation of scalar global `var` variables from
// inside a function. State advances inside f_advanceBbState
// (where UDT field mutation on g_bbs ARRAY ELEMENTS is legal because it
// mutates locally-retrieved objects), then main script scope:
//   (a) derive the three g_bbRetestX globals
//   (b) emit the BB_RETEST validation event on g_validation
// Both steps scan g_bbs[] for BB_TESTED transitions at bar_index.

f_advanceBbState()

// Reset and derive BB_RETEST bar-level flags from post-function state
g_bbRetestFired := false
g_bbRetestDir   := 0
g_bbRetestPrice := na

if array.size(g_bbs) > 0
    for _i = 0 to array.size(g_bbs) - 1
        _bb = array.get(g_bbs, _i)
        // BB_TESTED transition this bar = fresh retest
        if _bb.state == BB_TESTED and _bb.lastEventBar == bar_index
            g_bbRetestFired := true
            g_bbRetestDir   := _bb.side
            g_bbRetestPrice := (_bb.priceTop + _bb.priceBottom) / 2.0
            // 4.3. Priority cascade: MSS > CHoCH > BOS_DISP > BB_RETEST >
            // BOS_PLAIN. Only overwrite when either no event fired this
            // bar or only a plain BOS fired.
            if not g_validation.fired or g_validation.eventType == EVT_TYPE_BOS_PLAIN
                g_validation.fired     := true
                g_validation.dir       := _bb.side
                g_validation.eventType := EVT_TYPE_BB_RETEST
                g_validation.stale     := false
                g_validation.directionConflict := g_contextBiasDir != 0 and _bb.side != g_contextBiasDir
                g_validation.ageBars   := 0
                g_validation.anchorBarIdx := bar_index
                g_validation.anchorPrice  := (_bb.priceTop + _bb.priceBottom) / 2.0
            break

// --- BB array pruning ---

if array.size(g_bbs) > 0
    _toRemoveBbs = array.new_int(0)
    for _i = 0 to array.size(g_bbs) - 1
        _bb = array.get(g_bbs, _i)
        // Remove EXPIRED BBs older than 50 bars while retaining recent context.
        if _bb.state == BB_EXPIRED and (bar_index - _bb.lastEventBar) > 50
            array.push(_toRemoveBbs, _i)
    if array.size(_toRemoveBbs) > 0
        for _j = array.size(_toRemoveBbs) - 1 to 0
            array.remove(g_bbs, array.get(_toRemoveBbs, _j))
    while array.size(g_bbs) > i_bbMaxZones
        _oldest = array.shift(g_bbs)
        if not na(_oldest.boxId)
            box.delete(_oldest.boxId)
        if not na(_oldest.labelId)
            label.delete(_oldest.labelId)

// --- Extend g_zoneRejectionConfirmed to include BB zones ---
// ZONES module already set g_zoneRejectionConfirmed based on OB/FVG/BPR.
// BB rejections count too.

f_checkBbRejection(int _biasDir) =>
    _fired = false
    _dir = 0
    if _biasDir != 0 and array.size(g_bbs) > 0
        for _i = 0 to array.size(g_bbs) - 1
            _bb = array.get(g_bbs, _i)
            if _bb.state != BB_ACTIVE and _bb.state != BB_TESTED
                continue
            if _biasDir == 1 and _bb.side == 1 and low <= _bb.priceTop and close > open and close > _bb.priceBottom
                _fired := true
                _dir := 1
                break
            if _biasDir == -1 and _bb.side == -1 and high >= _bb.priceBottom and close < open and close < _bb.priceTop
                _fired := true
                _dir := -1
                break
    [_fired, _dir]

[_bbRejFired, _bbRejDir] = f_checkBbRejection(g_contextBiasDir)

if _bbRejFired
    g_zoneRejectionConfirmed := true
    if g_zoneRejectionDir == 0
        g_zoneRejectionDir := _bbRejDir

//#endregion MODULE 9

//#region MODULE 10 -- IMPULSE
// ============================================================================
// MODULE 10 -- IMPULSE
// ============================================================================
// Owns: IMP_STRONG/SOLID/HOLLOW/ABSORBED/WEAK enum,
//       g_impState, g_impDirection, g_bodyRatio, g_volRatio,
//       g_displacement, g_displacementDir,
//       g_consolidation,
//       g_patternEvent, g_patternType, g_patternDir
// Reads: g_atr14 (UTILITY), i_traderType, close, open, high, low, volume
//   E01 -- 5-state candle classification (STRONG/SOLID/HOLLOW/ABSORBED/WEAK)
//   E02 -- Consolidation detection (N candles within tight range)
//   E03 -- Confirmed Continuation pattern (STRONG -> HOLLOW -> STRONG same dir)
//   E04 -- Absorption Reversal pattern (STRONG -> ABSORBED opposite dir)
//   E05 -- Compression Breakout pattern (compression -> strong directional break)
//   E06 -- Displacement detection (body > 1.5 x ATR, feeds BOS_DISP)
//   Body ratio threshold for STRONG:  Scalper 55%, Intraday 65%, Swing 70%
//   Volume ratio threshold for STRONG: Scalper 110%, Intraday 120%, Swing 130%

// --- IMP state enum ---

var int IMP_STRONG   = 0
var int IMP_SOLID    = 1
var int IMP_HOLLOW   = 2
var int IMP_ABSORBED = 3
var int IMP_WEAK     = 4

// --- IMPULSE globals ---

var int    g_impState      = IMP_WEAK
var int    g_impDirection  = 0
var float  g_bodyRatio     = 0.0
var float  g_volRatio      = 1.0
var bool   g_displacement     = false
var int    g_displacementDir  = 0
var bool   g_consolidation    = false
var bool   g_patternEvent  = false
var string g_patternType   = ""
var int    g_patternDir    = 0

// --- Trader-type-scaled thresholds ---

_bodyThresholdStrong = i_traderType == "Scalper" ? 0.55 : i_traderType == "Swing" ? 0.70 : 0.65
_bodyThresholdSolid  = i_traderType == "Scalper" ? 0.40 : i_traderType == "Swing" ? 0.55 : 0.50
_bodyThresholdHollow = i_traderType == "Scalper" ? 0.15 : i_traderType == "Swing" ? 0.25 : 0.20
_volThresholdStrong  = i_traderType == "Scalper" ? 1.10 : i_traderType == "Swing" ? 1.30 : 1.20

// --- Per-bar body and volume ratios ---

_barRange    = high - low
_barBody     = math.abs(close - open)
_bodyRatioThis = _barRange > 0 ? (_barBody / _barRange) : 0.0

// 20-bar average volume baseline
_volAvg = ta.sma(volume, 20)
_volRatioThis = _volAvg > 0 ? (volume / _volAvg) : 1.0

g_bodyRatio := _bodyRatioThis
g_volRatio  := _volRatioThis

// --- Direction ---

_dirThis = close > open ? 1 : close < open ? -1 : 0

// --- Candle classification (priority cascade: WEAK > ABSORBED > STRONG > SOLID > HOLLOW) ---

// WEAK: doji or near-doji (tiny body)
_isWeak = _bodyRatioThis < _bodyThresholdHollow

// ABSORBED: significant directional attempt reversed within the bar
// Proxy: body is moderate but top wick OR bottom wick is >= 2x the body
// and close is in the opposite half of the candle range
_topWick = high - math.max(open, close)
_botWick = math.min(open, close) - low
_absorptionBullish = _botWick > (_barBody * 2.0) and close > open and (close - low) < (high - low) * 0.5
_absorptionBearish = _topWick > (_barBody * 2.0) and close < open and (high - close) < (high - low) * 0.5
_isAbsorbed = not _isWeak and (_absorptionBullish or _absorptionBearish)

// STRONG: body above STRONG threshold AND volume above volume threshold
_isStrong = not _isWeak and not _isAbsorbed and _bodyRatioThis >= _bodyThresholdStrong and _volRatioThis >= _volThresholdStrong

// SOLID: body above SOLID threshold (volume not required at this tier)
_isSolid = not _isWeak and not _isAbsorbed and not _isStrong and _bodyRatioThis >= _bodyThresholdSolid

// HOLLOW: directional but small body
_isHollow = not _isWeak and not _isAbsorbed and not _isStrong and not _isSolid

if _isWeak
    g_impState := IMP_WEAK
    g_impDirection := 0
else if _isAbsorbed
    g_impState := IMP_ABSORBED
    g_impDirection := _absorptionBullish ? 1 : -1
else if _isStrong
    g_impState := IMP_STRONG
    g_impDirection := _dirThis
else if _isSolid
    g_impState := IMP_SOLID
    g_impDirection := _dirThis
else
    g_impState := IMP_HOLLOW
    g_impDirection := _dirThis

// --- Displacement (E06): body > 1.5x ATR in a STRONG candle ---
// Named _impDisplaced to avoid collision with STRUCTURE module's
// _isDisplacement (identifier already defined).

_impDisplaced = g_impState == IMP_STRONG and _barBody > (g_atr14 * 1.5)
g_displacement := _impDisplaced
g_displacementDir := _impDisplaced ? g_impDirection : 0

// --- E02 consolidation detector ---
// Consolidation uses N consecutive candles within a tight range. N scales with
// trader type (same as DELIVERY_STATE's lookback). Tight range defined
// as range <= 0.75 x ATR sustained over the window. This is the canonical
// replacement for the earlier range-within-ATR proxy in DELIVERY_STATE.

_consolLookback = i_traderType == "Scalper" ? 5 : i_traderType == "Swing" ? 12 : 8
_consolWinHigh = ta.highest(high, _consolLookback)
_consolWinLow  = ta.lowest(low, _consolLookback)
_consolWinRange = _consolWinHigh - _consolWinLow
_consolTight = _consolWinRange <= (g_atr14 * 0.75)
// Additional criterion: at least 60% of recent candles must be HOLLOW or WEAK
// This distinguishes genuine consolidation from post-displacement drift.
// Pine v6 has no compound assignment operators -- use := always. Division
// between ints is integer division; cast denominator via * 1.0 for float.
_lowEnergyCount = 0
for _k = 0 to _consolLookback - 1
    _pastBody = math.abs(close[_k] - open[_k])
    _pastRange = high[_k] - low[_k]
    _pastBodyRatio = _pastRange > 0 ? (_pastBody / _pastRange) : 0.0
    if _pastBodyRatio < _bodyThresholdSolid
        _lowEnergyCount := _lowEnergyCount + 1
_lowEnergyPct = _lowEnergyCount / (_consolLookback * 1.0)
_consolEnergy = _lowEnergyPct >= 0.6

g_consolidation := _consolTight and _consolEnergy

// --- E03/E04/E05 pattern detection (simplified single-bar-back lookback) ---

g_patternEvent := false
g_patternType  := ""
g_patternDir   := 0

// E03 Confirmed Continuation: current STRONG in same dir as STRONG 2 bars ago,
// with a HOLLOW or SOLID bar sandwiched in between.
if g_impState == IMP_STRONG and bar_index >= 2
    _twoBackBody  = math.abs(close[2] - open[2])
    _twoBackRange = high[2] - low[2]
    _twoBackBodyR = _twoBackRange > 0 ? (_twoBackBody / _twoBackRange) : 0.0
    _twoBackVolR  = not na(_volAvg[2]) and _volAvg[2] > 0 ? (volume[2] / _volAvg[2]) : 1.0
    _twoBackStrong = _twoBackBodyR >= _bodyThresholdStrong and _twoBackVolR >= _volThresholdStrong
    _twoBackDir = close[2] > open[2] ? 1 : close[2] < open[2] ? -1 : 0
    _oneBackBody  = math.abs(close[1] - open[1])
    _oneBackRange = high[1] - low[1]
    _oneBackBodyR = _oneBackRange > 0 ? (_oneBackBody / _oneBackRange) : 0.0
    _oneBackIsPause = _oneBackBodyR < _bodyThresholdStrong
    if _twoBackStrong and _twoBackDir == g_impDirection and _oneBackIsPause
        g_patternEvent := true
        g_patternType  := "ConfirmedContinuation"
        g_patternDir   := g_impDirection

// E04 Absorption Reversal: STRONG candle immediately followed by ABSORBED candle
// in opposite direction
if not g_patternEvent and g_impState == IMP_ABSORBED and bar_index >= 1
    _oneBackBody  = math.abs(close[1] - open[1])
    _oneBackRange = high[1] - low[1]
    _oneBackBodyR = _oneBackRange > 0 ? (_oneBackBody / _oneBackRange) : 0.0
    _oneBackVolR  = not na(_volAvg[1]) and _volAvg[1] > 0 ? (volume[1] / _volAvg[1]) : 1.0
    _oneBackStrong = _oneBackBodyR >= _bodyThresholdStrong and _oneBackVolR >= _volThresholdStrong
    _oneBackDir = close[1] > open[1] ? 1 : close[1] < open[1] ? -1 : 0
    if _oneBackStrong and g_impDirection != 0 and _oneBackDir != 0 and g_impDirection != _oneBackDir
        g_patternEvent := true
        g_patternType  := "AbsorptionReversal"
        g_patternDir   := g_impDirection

// E05 Compression Breakout: STRONG directional candle emerging from consolidation
// (current bar STRONG, prior N bars met consolidation criteria)
if not g_patternEvent and g_impState == IMP_STRONG
    // Uses the prior-bar consolidation state as the activation latch
    _wasConsolidating = g_consolidation[1]
    if _wasConsolidating
        g_patternEvent := true
        g_patternType  := "CompressionBreakout"
        g_patternDir   := g_impDirection

//#endregion MODULE 10

//#region MODULE 11 -- CONTEXTUAL FILTERS
// ============================================================================
// MODULE 11 -- CONTEXTUAL FILTERS
// ============================================================================
// Owns: KZ_ASIAN/LONDON/NY_AM/NY_PM/LUNCH enum,
//       g_kzActive, g_kzName, g_kzCountdown, g_lunchActive,
//       g_vwap, g_vwapPrev,
//       g_ema1, g_ema2, g_ema3,
//       g_midnightOpen, g_pdh, g_pdl
// Reads: i_kzAsian/London/NyAm/NyPm,
//        i_kzLunchDeadZone,
//        i_vwapAnchor,
//        i_ema1Length, i_ema2Length, i_ema3Length,
//        i_showSessionReferenceLevels,
//        syminfo.type, hlc3, volume

// --- Killzone enum ---

var int KZ_NONE   = -1
var int KZ_ASIAN  = 0
var int KZ_LONDON = 1
var int KZ_NY_AM  = 2
var int KZ_NY_PM  = 3
var int KZ_LUNCH  = 4

// --- CONTEXTUAL globals ---

var int    g_kzActive     = KZ_NONE
var string g_kzName       = ""
var int    g_kzCountdown  = 0
var bool   g_lunchActive  = false
var float  g_vwap         = na
var float  g_vwapPrev     = na
var float  g_ema1         = na
var float  g_ema2         = na
var float  g_ema3         = na
var float  g_midnightOpen = na
var float  g_pdh          = na
var float  g_pdl          = na

// Crypto: 24/7 model, killzones still fire but no session boxes.
// Forex: standard London/NY dominant.
// Equities: exchange hours dominant; Asian/NY PM deprioritised.

_isForex      = syminfo.type == "forex"
_isCrypto     = syminfo.type == "crypto"
_isEquity     = syminfo.type == "stock" or syminfo.type == "fund" or syminfo.type == "ETF"
_isIndex      = syminfo.type == "index"
_isFutures    = syminfo.type == "futures"
_isCfd        = syminfo.type == "cfd"
_isCommodity  = syminfo.type == "commodity"
_isDr         = syminfo.type == "dr"
_isBond       = syminfo.type == "bond"
_isWarrant    = syminfo.type == "warrant"
_isStructured = syminfo.type == "structured"
_isRight      = syminfo.type == "right"

// Equity-like grouping: stocks/funds/ETFs plus equity-adjacent instruments
// (depositary receipts, warrants, structured products, rights). Used to gate
// Asian and NY-PM killzones consistently across equity-class instruments.
_isEquityLike = _isEquity or _isDr or _isWarrant or _isStructured or _isRight

// London and NY-AM fire for all 12 official syminfo.type values. Asian and NY-PM
// exclude equity-like instruments (stock/fund/ETF/dr/warrant/structured/right)
// per ICT methodology - those instruments transact during their primary exchange
// hours rather than during Asian or NY-PM windows.
_kzAsianActive  = i_kzAsian  and not _isEquityLike
_kzLondonActive = i_kzLondon and (_isForex or _isCrypto or _isIndex or _isEquity or _isFutures or _isCfd or _isCommodity or _isDr or _isBond or _isWarrant or _isStructured or _isRight)
_kzNyAmActive   = i_kzNyAm   and (_isForex or _isCrypto or _isIndex or _isEquity or _isFutures or _isCfd or _isCommodity or _isDr or _isBond or _isWarrant or _isStructured or _isRight)
_kzNyPmActive   = i_kzNyPm   and not _isEquityLike

// --- Hardcoded ICT killzone windows (GMT) ---
// These are institutional conventions, not user preferences.
// Asian KZ 00:00-03:00, London KZ 07:00-10:00, NY AM KZ 13:00-16:00, NY PM KZ 19:00-21:00
// Lunch dead-zone 16:00-19:00 GMT (between NY AM and NY PM).

_inAsianKz  = time(timeframe.period, "0000-0300:1234567", "GMT") != 0
_inLondonKz = time(timeframe.period, "0700-1000:1234567", "GMT") != 0
_inNyAmKz   = time(timeframe.period, "1300-1600:1234567", "GMT") != 0
_inNyPmKz   = time(timeframe.period, "1900-2100:1234567", "GMT") != 0
_inLunchKz  = time(timeframe.period, "1600-1900:1234567", "GMT") != 0

// Apply priority: NY PM > NY AM > London > Asian > Lunch (each overrides prior)
// Priority matches the temporal ordering a trader encounters during a day.

g_kzActive := KZ_NONE
g_kzName   := ""

if _kzAsianActive and _inAsianKz
    g_kzActive := KZ_ASIAN
    g_kzName   := "ASIAN"
if _kzLondonActive and _inLondonKz
    g_kzActive := KZ_LONDON
    g_kzName   := "LONDON"
if _kzNyAmActive and _inNyAmKz
    g_kzActive := KZ_NY_AM
    g_kzName   := "NY AM"
if _kzNyPmActive and _inNyPmKz
    g_kzActive := KZ_NY_PM
    g_kzName   := "NY PM"

g_lunchActive := i_kzLunchDeadZone and _inLunchKz
if g_lunchActive and g_kzActive == KZ_NONE
    g_kzActive := KZ_LUNCH
    g_kzName   := "LUNCH"

// Minutes remaining in current killzone. Computes by scanning forward
// on chart TF seconds-per-bar to find session end. Session boundary model:
// track when the KZ flag first became true and subtract from known KZ length.
// KZ durations: Asian 180min, London 180min, NY AM 180min, NY PM 120min, Lunch 180min

var int _kzStartTime = na
var int _kzPriorActive = KZ_NONE

if g_kzActive != _kzPriorActive and g_kzActive != KZ_NONE
    _kzStartTime := time
_kzPriorActive := g_kzActive

_kzDurationMin = g_kzActive == KZ_NY_PM ? 120 : g_kzActive == KZ_NONE ? 0 : 180

if g_kzActive != KZ_NONE and not na(_kzStartTime)
    _elapsedMin = (time - _kzStartTime) / 60000
    g_kzCountdown := math.max(0, _kzDurationMin - _elapsedMin)
else
    g_kzCountdown := 0

// --- VWAP ---
// Pine v6 constraint: ta.* calls must be unconditional at global scope
// (even conditional expressions containing ta.* can produce consistency
// warnings). All ta.change() calls are hoisted to global scope, pre-computing
// every possible reset boundary, and then select among them based on the
// anchor input. g_vwap is computed by ta.vwap with a selected reset.
// session.isfirstbar_regular is already a series bool that fires once per
// session; ta.change() on a bool is nonsensical. Use it directly as the
// session-anchor reset trigger.

_resetSession = session.isfirstbar_regular
_resetDaily   = ta.change(dayofmonth) != 0
_resetWeekly  = ta.change(weekofyear) != 0
_resetDayOfWk = ta.change(dayofweek) != 0
_resetMonday  = dayofweek == dayofweek.monday and _resetDayOfWk

_vwapReset = i_vwapAnchor == "Session" ? _resetSession : i_vwapAnchor == "Daily" ? _resetDaily : i_vwapAnchor == "Weekly" ? _resetWeekly : i_vwapAnchor == "Monday" ? _resetMonday : false

_vwapRaw = ta.vwap(hlc3, _vwapReset)
g_vwap := _vwapRaw

// Note: _vwapReset already encodes the anchor selection, so it is reused.
var float _prevVwapSnapshot = na
if _vwapReset
    _prevVwapSnapshot := g_vwap[1]
g_vwapPrev := _prevVwapSnapshot

// --- 3 EMAs ---

g_ema1 := ta.ema(close, i_ema1Length)
g_ema2 := ta.ema(close, i_ema2Length)
g_ema3 := ta.ema(close, i_ema3Length)


[_dayHighPrev, _dayLowPrev] = request.security(
     symbol     = syminfo.tickerid,
     timeframe  = "D",
     expression = [high[1], low[1]],
     gaps       = barmerge.gaps_off,
     lookahead  = barmerge.lookahead_off)

g_pdh := _dayHighPrev
g_pdl := _dayLowPrev

// Midnight Open: open of bar at 00:00 GMT (captured on day change)
var float _midnightOpenSnapshot = na
_newDay = ta.change(dayofmonth) != 0
if _newDay
    _midnightOpenSnapshot := open
g_midnightOpen := _midnightOpenSnapshot

// --- Chart overlays for EMAs and VWAP ---

plot(i_showEma1 and not na(g_ema1) ? g_ema1 : na, title = "EMA 1", color = color.new(color.blue,   30), linewidth = 1, display = display.pane - display.status_line - display.data_window - display.price_scale)
plot(i_showEma2 and not na(g_ema2) ? g_ema2 : na, title = "EMA 2", color = color.new(color.orange, 30), linewidth = 1, display = display.pane - display.status_line - display.data_window - display.price_scale)
plot(i_showEma3 and not na(g_ema3) ? g_ema3 : na, title = "EMA 3", color = color.new(color.red,    30), linewidth = 1, display = display.pane - display.status_line - display.data_window - display.price_scale)

plot(i_showVwapLine and not na(g_vwap) ? g_vwap : na, title = "VWAP", color = color.new(color.purple, 20), linewidth = 2, display = display.pane - display.status_line - display.data_window - display.price_scale)

// --- Chart overlays for session reference levels (dashed horizontal lines) ---

plot(i_showSessionReferenceLevels and not na(g_pdh) ? g_pdh : na, title = "PDH", color = color.new(color.gray, 30), style = plot.style_linebr, linewidth = 1, display = display.pane - display.status_line - display.data_window - display.price_scale)
plot(i_showSessionReferenceLevels and not na(g_pdl) ? g_pdl : na, title = "PDL", color = color.new(color.gray, 30), style = plot.style_linebr, linewidth = 1, display = display.pane - display.status_line - display.data_window - display.price_scale)
plot(i_showSessionReferenceLevels and not na(g_midnightOpen) ? g_midnightOpen : na, title = "Midnight Open", color = color.new(color.yellow, 30), style = plot.style_linebr, linewidth = 1, display = display.pane - display.status_line - display.data_window - display.price_scale)

//#endregion MODULE 11

//#region MODULE 12 -- PHASE INDICATOR
// ============================================================================
// MODULE 12 -- PHASE INDICATOR
// ============================================================================
// Reads: g_msdClose[7], g_msdHigh[7], g_msdLow[7] (HTF_DATA),
//        g_swingPatternChart (SWING),
//        g_bosFiredBar, g_mssFiredBar, g_chochFiredBar (STRUCTURE),
//        g_atr14 (UTILITY), i_traderType
// across all rows (chart-TF g_swingPatternChart). BOS/MSS recent-fire
// from each TF's price position relative to its current HTF bar's
// equilibrium (mid of g_msdHigh[i] + g_msdLow[i]), with ATR tolerance.


var int PHASE_CONSOLIDATING = 0
var int PHASE_TRENDING      = 1
var int PHASE_PULLING       = 2


var array<int>    g_msdPhase          = array.new_int(7, PHASE_CONSOLIDATING)
var array<int>    g_msdPhaseDirection = array.new_int(7, 0)
var array<string> g_msdPhaseStr       = array.new_string(7, "CON")


_trnLookback = i_traderType == "Scalper" ? 10 : i_traderType == "Swing" ? 20 : 15
_pulLookback = i_traderType == "Scalper" ?  5 : i_traderType == "Swing" ? 12 :  8
_eqTolerance = g_atr14 * 0.3  // Equilibrium displacement threshold (0.3 x ATR)


_chartBosAge = na(g_bosFiredBar)   ? 9999 : (bar_index - g_bosFiredBar)
_chartMssAge = na(g_mssFiredBar)   ? 9999 : (bar_index - g_mssFiredBar)
_chartChochAge = na(g_chochFiredBar) ? 9999 : (bar_index - g_chochFiredBar)

_recentBosOrMss = math.min(_chartBosAge, _chartMssAge) <= _trnLookback

// Structure pattern: +1 HH-HL (bull), -1 LL-LH (bear), 0 mixed
_structBull = g_swingPatternChart ==  1
_structBear = g_swingPatternChart == -1
_structMixed = g_swingPatternChart == 0

// --- Per-row classifier function ---
// Priority: TRENDING > PULLING > CONSOLIDATING (default).

f_classifyTfPhase(int _row) =>
    _closeR = array.get(g_msdClose, _row)
    _highR  = array.get(g_msdHigh,  _row)
    _lowR   = array.get(g_msdLow,   _row)

    _phase = PHASE_CONSOLIDATING
    _dir   = 0
    _str   = "CON"

    if na(_closeR) or na(_highR) or na(_lowR)
        // Insufficient HTF data -- default CONSOLIDATING
        [PHASE_CONSOLIDATING, 0, "CON"]
    else
        // Per-row equilibrium: midpoint of this HTF bar's high/low
        _eqR = (_highR + _lowR) / 2.0
        _rowRange = _highR - _lowR

        // Distance from equilibrium, expressed as fraction of row's own range
        _distFromEq = _closeR - _eqR

        // Premium/discount side of this row's range
        // (positive = upper half, negative = lower half)
        _inPremium = _distFromEq > 0
        _inDiscount = _distFromEq < 0

        // Displacement check: at least _eqTolerance (chart ATR * 0.3) away
        // from equilibrium. Because different TFs have different scale, chart
        // ATR is used as the common tolerance measure -- consistent with
        // the rest of the codebase's ATR scaling.
        _displacedFromEq = math.abs(_distFromEq) >= _eqTolerance

        // TRENDING: structure pattern + recent BOS/MSS + price displaced
        // on the bias side
        _trendingBull = _structBull and _recentBosOrMss and _inPremium and _displacedFromEq
        _trendingBear = _structBear and _recentBosOrMss and _inDiscount and _displacedFromEq

        // PULLING: structure has direction + price moving opposite (i.e.
        // in the non-bias half of the range) or price in opposite half
        // of the row's range. Using shorter _pulLookback for pull check.
        _recentMove = math.min(_chartBosAge, _chartChochAge) <= _pulLookback
        _pullingBull = _structBull and _recentMove and _inDiscount
        _pullingBear = _structBear and _recentMove and _inPremium

        // Priority cascade
        if _trendingBull
            _phase := PHASE_TRENDING
            _dir   := 1
            _str   := "TRN"
        else if _trendingBear
            _phase := PHASE_TRENDING
            _dir   := -1
            _str   := "TRN"
        else if _pullingBull
            _phase := PHASE_PULLING
            _dir   := 1
            _str   := "PUL"
        else if _pullingBear
            _phase := PHASE_PULLING
            _dir   := -1
            _str   := "PUL"
        else
            _phase := PHASE_CONSOLIDATING
            _dir   := 0
            _str   := "CON"

        [_phase, _dir, _str]

// --- Evaluate all 7 MSD rows per bar ---
// Captured here at main script scope since arrays are globals; array.set
// mutations from main scope are valid Pine v6.

for _row = 0 to 6
    [_p, _d, _s] = f_classifyTfPhase(_row)
    array.set(g_msdPhase,          _row, _p)
    array.set(g_msdPhaseDirection, _row, _d)
    array.set(g_msdPhaseStr,       _row, _s)

//#endregion MODULE 12

//#region MODULE 13 -- ORDER FLOW
// ============================================================================
// MODULE 13 -- ORDER FLOW
// ============================================================================
// Owns: g_flowBias (float -1..+1), g_flowBiasDir (int),
//       g_flowBiasDivergence (bool), g_flowBiasDivergenceDir (int),
//       g_climaxFired (bool), g_climaxDir (int), g_climaxBarIdx (int)
// Reads: open, high, low, close, volume,
//        g_swingHigh, g_swingLow, g_newSwingHighFired, g_newSwingLowFired,
//        g_swingHighIdx, g_swingLowIdx (SWING),
//        g_atr14 (UTILITY), g_kzActive, g_kzName (CONTEXTUAL),
//        i_ofEnabled,
//        i_climaxEnabled, i_climaxVolumeMult,
//        i_ofShowClimaxMarkers, i_showChartOverlays
// (Order Flow Layer).
// computation is a close-position PROXY for buy/sell volume decomposition.
// It is NOT real tick-level bid/ask data. Every user-facing label must
// use "Flow Bias" and never "Delta". This module's globals and comments
// honour that discipline.

// --- ORDER_FLOW globals ---

var float g_flowBias              = 0.0   // -1 (full sell) .. +1 (full buy)
var int   g_flowBiasDir           = 0     // +1 buy / -1 sell / 0 neutral
var bool  g_flowBiasDivergence    = false
var int   g_flowBiasDivergenceDir = 0

var bool  g_climaxFired  = false
var int   g_climaxDir    = 0
var int   g_climaxBarIdx = na

// --- Flow Bias computation ---
// Close-position proxy: where did the bar close within its range?
//   closeFrac = (close - low) / (high - low)   -> 0..1
//   flowBias  = (closeFrac - 0.5) * 2           -> -1..+1
// Weight by relative volume so a high-volume bias carries more weight in
// aggregation but the per-bar flowBias remains in [-1, +1].

_barRangeOf = high - low
_closeFrac = _barRangeOf > 0 ? (close - low) / _barRangeOf : 0.5
_flowBiasThis = (_closeFrac - 0.5) * 2.0

g_flowBias := i_ofEnabled ? _flowBiasThis : 0.0
g_flowBiasDir := _flowBiasThis > 0.1 ? 1 : _flowBiasThis < -0.1 ? -1 : 0

// --- Flow Bias divergence at swing pivots ---
// A bearish divergence fires when a NEW swing high is made (g_newSwingHighFired)
// AND the flow bias at that swing is WEAKER than the flow bias at the
// prior swing high (lower positive or negative). Bullish divergence is
// the symmetric condition at swing lows.
// The last qualifying swing's flow bias is tracked in persistent vars.

var float _priorSwingHighFlowBias = na
var float _priorSwingLowFlowBias  = na

g_flowBiasDivergence := false
g_flowBiasDivergenceDir := 0

if i_ofEnabled
    if g_newSwingHighFired and not na(g_swingHigh)
        _thisSwingFlowBias = g_flowBias
        if not na(_priorSwingHighFlowBias)
            // Bearish divergence: this high's flow bias is weaker than prior
            if _thisSwingFlowBias < _priorSwingHighFlowBias - 0.15
                g_flowBiasDivergence := true
                g_flowBiasDivergenceDir := -1
        _priorSwingHighFlowBias := _thisSwingFlowBias
    if g_newSwingLowFired and not na(g_swingLow)
        _thisSwingFlowBias = g_flowBias
        if not na(_priorSwingLowFlowBias)
            // Bullish divergence: this low's flow bias is higher (less negative) than prior
            if _thisSwingFlowBias > _priorSwingLowFlowBias + 0.15
                g_flowBiasDivergence := true
                g_flowBiasDivergenceDir := 1
        _priorSwingLowFlowBias := _thisSwingFlowBias

// --- Volume Climax Exhaustion ---
//   1. Killzone active (_kzActive) -- prevents noise during thin sessions
//   2. At trend extreme (new swing high or new swing low on this bar or
//      within last 2 bars)
// Climax detection:
//   Bearish exhaustion: volume > SMA*mult AND close < open AND upper wick > body
//   Bullish exhaustion: volume > SMA*mult AND close > open AND lower wick > body

_volSmaOf = ta.sma(volume, 20)
_isClimaxVolume = not na(_volSmaOf) and _volSmaOf > 0 and volume > (_volSmaOf * i_climaxVolumeMult)

_bodyOf    = math.abs(close - open)
_upperWick = high - math.max(open, close)
_lowerWick = math.min(open, close) - low

_bearExhaustion = _isClimaxVolume and close < open and _upperWick > _bodyOf
_bullExhaustion = _isClimaxVolume and close > open and _lowerWick > _bodyOf

// Trend extreme filter: current bar or last 2 bars had a new swing
_atExtreme = g_newSwingHighFired or g_newSwingLowFired or g_newSwingHighFired[1] or g_newSwingLowFired[1] or g_newSwingHighFired[2] or g_newSwingLowFired[2]

// Killzone filter: active killzone (not KZ_NONE=-1, not KZ_LUNCH=4)
_inActiveKz = g_kzActive >= 0 and g_kzActive <= 3

_climaxFilterOk = _inActiveKz and _atExtreme

g_climaxFired := false
g_climaxDir   := 0

if i_ofEnabled and i_climaxEnabled and _climaxFilterOk
    if _bearExhaustion
        g_climaxFired  := true
        g_climaxDir    := -1
        g_climaxBarIdx := bar_index
    else if _bullExhaustion
        g_climaxFired  := true
        g_climaxDir    := 1
        g_climaxBarIdx := bar_index

// --- Optional chart marker for climax events ---

if i_ofShowClimaxMarkers and i_showChartOverlays and g_climaxFired
    label.new(
         x        = bar_index,
         y        = f_eventLabelY(g_climaxDir == 1 ? low : high, g_climaxDir != 1),
         text     = "CLIMAX",
         style    = g_climaxDir == 1 ? label.style_label_up : label.style_label_down,
         yloc     = yloc.price,
         color    = color.new(color.fuchsia, 30),
         textcolor = color.white,
         size     = size.tiny)

//#endregion MODULE 13

//#region MODULE 14 -- SMT DIVERGENCE
// ============================================================================
// MODULE 14 -- SMT DIVERGENCE
// ============================================================================
// Owns: g_smt1Close, g_smt1Atr, g_smt2Close, g_smt2Atr,
//       g_smt1PriorSwingHigh/Low, g_smt2PriorSwingHigh/Low,
//       g_smtDivergenceFired, g_smtDivergenceDir,
//       g_smtDivergenceStrength, g_smtDivergenceSource
// Reads: g_swingHigh, g_swingLow, g_newSwingHighFired, g_newSwingLowFired (SWING),
//        g_atr14 (UTILITY),
//        i_smtTicker1, i_smtTicker2,
//        i_smtSignificanceAtrMult,
//        i_smt1InverseCorrelated, i_smt2InverseCorrelated,
//        i_showChartOverlays
// wired from day one).
// Detection logic:
//   At each newly confirmed chart swing pivot, capture the correlated
//   ticker's close at that bar. Compare to the correlated ticker's close
//   at the PRIOR qualifying pivot of the same type. Expected behaviour
//   for a directly-correlated ticker: chart new high = correlated new
//   high; chart new low = correlated new low. Expected behaviour for an
//   inversely-correlated ticker: chart new high = correlated new low;
//   chart new low = correlated new high.
//   A divergence fires when the CORRELATED ticker's movement does NOT
//   confirm the chart's movement, with the gap being ATR-significant.
// Ticker 2 empty-string handling:
//   A fallback symbol keeps the request path stable when ticker 2 is blank;
//   downstream logic gates consumption via the blank check.

// --- SMT globals ---

var float  g_smt1Close = na
var float  g_smt1Atr   = na
var float  g_smt2Close = na
var float  g_smt2Atr   = na

var float  g_smt1PriorSwingHigh = na
var float  g_smt1PriorSwingLow  = na
var float  g_smt2PriorSwingHigh = na
var float  g_smt2PriorSwingLow  = na

var bool   g_smtDivergenceFired    = false
var int    g_smtDivergenceDir      = 0
var float  g_smtDivergenceStrength = 0.0
var string g_smtDivergenceSource   = ""

// g_smtDivergenceFired is a per-event flag (resets each bar). The Reversal
// Score engine needs "fired within last 10 bars" semantic per Reversal
// divergence event across bars; main-scope mutation below extends the
// per-bar fired signal into a persistent window.
var int    g_smtDivergenceLastBarIdx = na
var int    g_smtDivergenceLastDir    = 0

// --- Correlated ticker data fetch ---
// Both calls are at global scope unconditionally per Pine v6 constraint.
// Ticker 2 falls back to ticker 1 when disabled -- the result is ignored
// downstream via the empty-string gate.

_smt2Symbol = i_smtTicker2 == "" ? i_smtTicker1 : i_smtTicker2

[_smt1CloseRaw, _smt1AtrRaw] = request.security(
     symbol     = i_smtTicker1,
     timeframe  = timeframe.period,
     expression = [close, ta.atr(14)],
     gaps       = barmerge.gaps_off,
     lookahead  = barmerge.lookahead_off)

[_smt2CloseRaw, _smt2AtrRaw] = request.security(
     symbol     = _smt2Symbol,
     timeframe  = timeframe.period,
     expression = [close, ta.atr(14)],
     gaps       = barmerge.gaps_off,
     lookahead  = barmerge.lookahead_off)

g_smt1Close := _smt1CloseRaw
g_smt1Atr   := _smt1AtrRaw
g_smt2Close := _smt2CloseRaw
g_smt2Atr   := _smt2AtrRaw

// --- Per-ticker divergence helper ---
// Returns [fired, dir, strength] for the given ticker's divergence check
// against the current swing event. Called once per ticker per bar.
// Pine v6 scope constraint: this function reads globals but does not
// mutate them. State updates (capturing the current pivot's price as the
// new "prior") happen at main scope.

f_checkSmtDivergence(bool _newHigh, bool _newLow,
                     float _corrClose, float _corrAtr,
                     float _priorHighPrice, float _priorLowPrice,
                     bool _inverse, float _atrMult, bool _useFilter) =>
    _fired = false
    _dir = 0
    _strength = 0.0
    if not na(_corrClose) and not na(_corrAtr) and _corrAtr > 0
        _sigThreshold = _corrAtr * _atrMult
        if _newHigh and not na(_priorHighPrice)
            // Direct correlation: expect correlated to also make new high
            //   (current _corrClose > _priorHighPrice)
            // Inverse correlation: expect correlated to make a NEW LOW
            //   (current _corrClose < _priorHighPrice). The correlated value
            //   was captured at the chart's
            //   prior high, so the test is whether correlated has moved
            //   in the opposite direction since.
            _corrDelta = _corrClose - _priorHighPrice
            // For direct correlation: divergence if correlated did NOT
            // make new high (corrDelta <= 0) with ATR-significant gap.
            // For inverse correlation: divergence if correlated did NOT
            // make new low (corrDelta >= 0) with ATR-significant gap.
            _divergesDirect  = _corrDelta <= 0
            _divergesInverse = _corrDelta >= 0
            _diverges = _inverse ? _divergesInverse : _divergesDirect
            _significant = not _useFilter or math.abs(_corrDelta) >= _sigThreshold
            if _diverges and _significant
                _fired := true
                _dir := -1  // bearish SMT divergence at high
                // Strength: ratio of gap magnitude to ATR, capped at 1.0
                _strength := math.min(1.0, math.abs(_corrDelta) / (_corrAtr * 2.0))
        if _newLow and not na(_priorLowPrice)
            _corrDelta = _corrClose - _priorLowPrice
            // Direct correlation: divergence if correlated did NOT make new low
            // Inverse correlation: divergence if correlated did NOT make new high
            _divergesDirect  = _corrDelta >= 0
            _divergesInverse = _corrDelta <= 0
            _diverges = _inverse ? _divergesInverse : _divergesDirect
            _significant = not _useFilter or math.abs(_corrDelta) >= _sigThreshold
            if _diverges and _significant
                _fired := true
                _dir := 1  // bullish SMT divergence at low
                _strength := math.min(1.0, math.abs(_corrDelta) / (_corrAtr * 2.0))
    [_fired, _dir, _strength]

// --- Per-bar SMT divergence evaluation ---
// Reset fired flag each bar (divergences are per-event signals).

g_smtDivergenceFired    := false
g_smtDivergenceDir      := 0
g_smtDivergenceStrength := 0.0
g_smtDivergenceSource   := ""

if g_newSwingHighFired or g_newSwingLowFired
    // Ticker 1 check
    [_t1Fired, _t1Dir, _t1Strength] = f_checkSmtDivergence(
         g_newSwingHighFired, g_newSwingLowFired,
         g_smt1Close, g_smt1Atr,
         g_smt1PriorSwingHigh, g_smt1PriorSwingLow,
         i_smt1InverseCorrelated, i_smtSignificanceAtrMult, true)

    if _t1Fired
        g_smtDivergenceFired    := true
        g_smtDivergenceDir      := _t1Dir
        g_smtDivergenceStrength := _t1Strength
        g_smtDivergenceSource   := i_smtTicker1

    // Ticker 2 check (only if configured)
    if i_smtTicker2 != ""
        [_t2Fired, _t2Dir, _t2Strength] = f_checkSmtDivergence(
             g_newSwingHighFired, g_newSwingLowFired,
             g_smt2Close, g_smt2Atr,
             g_smt2PriorSwingHigh, g_smt2PriorSwingLow,
             i_smt2InverseCorrelated, i_smtSignificanceAtrMult, true)

        if _t2Fired
            // If t1 already fired in same dir, strengthen; if opposite, flag t2 too
            if g_smtDivergenceFired and _t2Dir == g_smtDivergenceDir
                g_smtDivergenceStrength := math.min(1.0, g_smtDivergenceStrength + _t2Strength * 0.5)
                g_smtDivergenceSource   := g_smtDivergenceSource + " + " + i_smtTicker2
            else if not g_smtDivergenceFired
                g_smtDivergenceFired    := true
                g_smtDivergenceDir      := _t2Dir
                g_smtDivergenceStrength := _t2Strength
                g_smtDivergenceSource   := i_smtTicker2

    // Capture current pivot prices as new "prior" for next divergence check.
    // Main-scope mutation of globals is legal (these are array-analogous
    // persistent state). Pine v6 restricts scalar global mutation inside helpers.
    if g_newSwingHighFired
        g_smt1PriorSwingHigh := g_smt1Close
        g_smt2PriorSwingHigh := g_smt2Close
    if g_newSwingLowFired
        g_smt1PriorSwingLow := g_smt1Close
        g_smt2PriorSwingLow := g_smt2Close

// Persists the most recent divergence event across bars. Reversal Score
// component 9 reads g_smtDivergenceLastBarIdx + g_smtDivergenceLastDir
// instead of the per-bar g_smtDivergenceFired flag.
if g_smtDivergenceFired
    g_smtDivergenceLastBarIdx := bar_index
    g_smtDivergenceLastDir    := g_smtDivergenceDir

// --- Chart marker for SMT divergence fires ---

if i_showChartOverlays and i_smtShowLabels and g_smtDivergenceFired
    _smtLabel = "SMT " + (g_smtDivergenceDir == 1 ? "BULL" : "BEAR")
    label.new(
         x        = bar_index,
         y        = f_eventLabelY(g_smtDivergenceDir == 1 ? low : high, g_smtDivergenceDir != 1),
         text     = _smtLabel,
         style    = g_smtDivergenceDir == 1 ? label.style_label_up : label.style_label_down,
         yloc     = yloc.price,
         color    = color.new(color.aqua, 20),
         textcolor = color.black,
         size     = size.tiny)

//#endregion MODULE 14

//#region MODULE 15 -- CONFLUENCE ENGINE
// ============================================================================
// MODULE 15 -- CONFLUENCE ENGINE
// ============================================================================
// Owns: CONF_TIER_WEAK/STANDARD/STRONG enum,
//       g_confPercent, g_confMet, g_confEnabled, g_confTier, g_confTierStr,
//       g_confFactors[15] (array<bool>), g_confFactorNames[15] (array<string>)
// Reads: ALL prior module outputs (g_obs, g_fvgs, g_bprs, g_bbs,
//        g_vwap, g_vwapPrev, g_kzActive, g_bosFiredBar, g_mssFiredBar,
//        g_ema1, g_swingPatternChart, g_contextBiasDir, g_pdh, g_pdl),
//        proximity inputs (i_obProximityPct, i_fvgProximityPct,
//        i_vwapProximityPct, i_swingProxPct, i_oteEnabled),
//        tier thresholds (i_confStrongThresholdPct, i_confStandardThresholdPct)

// --- CONF tier enum ---

var int CONF_TIER_WEAK     = 0
var int CONF_TIER_STANDARD = 1
var int CONF_TIER_STRONG   = 2

// --- CONFLUENCE globals ---

// Factor count is not user-configurable; use IID Matrix presets for
// trading-style-aware confluence customisation.
const int CONF_FACTOR_COUNT = 15

var float  g_confPercent  = 0.0
var int    g_confMet      = 0
var int    g_confEnabled  = 0
var int    g_confTier     = CONF_TIER_WEAK
var string g_confTierStr  = "WEAK"

var array<bool>   g_confFactors      = array.new_bool(15, false)
var array<string> g_confFactorNames  = array.new_string(15, "")

// Factor name initialisation (runs once on bar 0)
if barstate.isfirst
    array.set(g_confFactorNames,  0, "OB")
    array.set(g_confFactorNames,  1, "FVG")
    array.set(g_confFactorNames,  2, "VWAP")
    array.set(g_confFactorNames,  3, "KZ")
    array.set(g_confFactorNames,  4, "BOS")
    array.set(g_confFactorNames,  5, "EMA")
    array.set(g_confFactorNames,  6, "SW")
    array.set(g_confFactorNames,  7, "VOL")
    array.set(g_confFactorNames,  8, "PDH")
    array.set(g_confFactorNames,  9, "PVW")
    array.set(g_confFactorNames, 10, "BB")
    array.set(g_confFactorNames, 11, "BPR")
    array.set(g_confFactorNames, 12, "SWP")
    array.set(g_confFactorNames, 13, "OTE")
    array.set(g_confFactorNames, 14, "P/D")

// Helper: render factor bracket if met, empty string otherwise
f_factorBracket(int _idx) =>
    array.get(g_confFactors, _idx) ? "[" + array.get(g_confFactorNames, _idx) + "] " : ""

// --- Per-factor evaluation ---
// Each factor produces a bool. Computed unconditionally every bar.

// Factor 1: OB proximity -- nearest ACTIVE non-mitigated OB within i_obProximityPct*ATR
// OB/BB mutex is already handled: OBs that entered BB lifecycle were marked

_obTolerance = g_atr14 * i_obProximityPct
_factorOb = false
if array.size(g_obs) > 0
    for _i = 0 to array.size(g_obs) - 1
        _z = array.get(g_obs, _i)
        if _z.state == ZS_EXPIRED or _z.state == ZS_MITIGATED
            continue
        if close >= (_z.priceBottom - _obTolerance) and close <= (_z.priceTop + _obTolerance)
            _factorOb := true
            break

// Factor 2: FVG proximity

_fvgTolerance = g_atr14 * i_fvgProximityPct
_factorFvg = false
if array.size(g_fvgs) > 0
    for _i = 0 to array.size(g_fvgs) - 1
        _z = array.get(g_fvgs, _i)
        if _z.state == ZS_EXPIRED or _z.state == ZS_MITIGATED
            continue
        if close >= (_z.priceBottom - _fvgTolerance) and close <= (_z.priceTop + _fvgTolerance)
            _factorFvg := true
            break

// Factor 3: VWAP proximity

_vwapTolerance = g_atr14 * i_vwapProximityPct
_factorVwap = not na(g_vwap) and math.abs(close - g_vwap) <= _vwapTolerance

// Factor 4: Killzone active (not NONE, not LUNCH)

_factorKz = g_kzActive >= 0 and g_kzActive <= 3

// Factor 5: Recent BOS/MSS within validation freshness window

_bosAge = na(g_bosFiredBar) ? 9999 : (bar_index - g_bosFiredBar)
_mssAge = na(g_mssFiredBar) ? 9999 : (bar_index - g_mssFiredBar)
_freshnessLimit = i_validationFreshnessBars
_factorBos = math.min(_bosAge, _mssAge) <= _freshnessLimit

// Factor 6: EMA 1 bias alignment

_factorEma = false
if not na(g_ema1) and g_contextBiasDir != 0
    // Bull bias + price above EMA 1 = aligned
    // Bear bias + price below EMA 1 = aligned
    _factorEma := (g_contextBiasDir == 1 and close > g_ema1) or (g_contextBiasDir == -1 and close < g_ema1)

// Factor 7: Swing pattern aligned with Context TF bias

_factorSw = g_contextBiasDir != 0 and g_swingPatternChart == g_contextBiasDir

// Factor 8: Volume elevated (above 20-bar SMA)

_volSmaConf = ta.sma(volume, 20)
_factorVol = not na(_volSmaConf) and _volSmaConf > 0 and volume > _volSmaConf

// Factor 9: PDH/PDL respect (price within OB-proximity ATR of either)

_factorPdh = false
_pdhlTolerance = g_atr14 * i_obProximityPct
if not na(g_pdh) and math.abs(close - g_pdh) <= _pdhlTolerance
    _factorPdh := true
if not na(g_pdl) and math.abs(close - g_pdl) <= _pdhlTolerance
    _factorPdh := true


_factorPvw = not na(g_vwapPrev) and math.abs(close - g_vwapPrev) <= _vwapTolerance

// Factor 11: BB proximity (ACTIVE or TESTED)

_bbTolerance = g_atr14 * i_obProximityPct
_factorBb = false
if array.size(g_bbs) > 0
    for _i = 0 to array.size(g_bbs) - 1
        _bb = array.get(g_bbs, _i)
        if _bb.state != BB_ACTIVE and _bb.state != BB_TESTED
            continue
        if close >= (_bb.priceBottom - _bbTolerance) and close <= (_bb.priceTop + _bbTolerance)
            _factorBb := true
            break

// Factor 12: BPR proximity

_bprTolerance = g_atr14 * i_fvgProximityPct
_factorBpr = false
if array.size(g_bprs) > 0
    for _i = 0 to array.size(g_bprs) - 1
        _z = array.get(g_bprs, _i)
        if _z.state == ZS_EXPIRED or _z.state == ZS_MITIGATED
            continue
        if close >= (_z.priceBottom - _bprTolerance) and close <= (_z.priceTop + _bprTolerance)
            _factorBpr := true
            break

// Factor 13: Swing proximity (SWP)

float _confSwingPct = na
if not na(g_swingHigh) and not na(g_swingLow) and g_swingHigh > g_swingLow
    _confSwingPct := (close - g_swingLow) / (g_swingHigh - g_swingLow) * 100.0

_factorSwp = not na(_confSwingPct) and (_confSwingPct >= (100 - i_swingProxPct) or _confSwingPct <= i_swingProxPct)

// Factor 14: OTE zone

_factorOte = i_oteEnabled and not na(_confSwingPct) and ((_confSwingPct >= 62.0 and _confSwingPct <= 79.0) or (_confSwingPct >= 21.0 and _confSwingPct <= 38.0))

// Factor 15: Premium/Discount (P/D)
// Outside the 40-60 equilibrium band to avoid midpoint flicker.

_factorPd = not na(_confSwingPct) and (_confSwingPct > 60.0 or _confSwingPct < 40.0)

// --- Populate factor array for display ---

array.set(g_confFactors,  0, _factorOb)
array.set(g_confFactors,  1, _factorFvg)
array.set(g_confFactors,  2, _factorVwap)
array.set(g_confFactors,  3, _factorKz)
array.set(g_confFactors,  4, _factorBos)
array.set(g_confFactors,  5, _factorEma)
array.set(g_confFactors,  6, _factorSw)
array.set(g_confFactors,  7, _factorVol)
array.set(g_confFactors,  8, _factorPdh)
array.set(g_confFactors,  9, _factorPvw)
array.set(g_confFactors, 10, _factorBb)
array.set(g_confFactors, 11, _factorBpr)
array.set(g_confFactors, 12, _factorSwp)
array.set(g_confFactors, 13, _factorOte)
array.set(g_confFactors, 14, _factorPd)

// --- Factor aggregation ---

_metCount = 0

if _factorOb
    _metCount := _metCount + 1
if _factorFvg
    _metCount := _metCount + 1
if _factorVwap
    _metCount := _metCount + 1
if _factorKz
    _metCount := _metCount + 1
if _factorBos
    _metCount := _metCount + 1
if _factorEma
    _metCount := _metCount + 1
if _factorSw
    _metCount := _metCount + 1
if _factorVol
    _metCount := _metCount + 1
if _factorPdh
    _metCount := _metCount + 1
if _factorPvw
    _metCount := _metCount + 1
if _factorBb
    _metCount := _metCount + 1
if _factorBpr
    _metCount := _metCount + 1
if _factorSwp
    _metCount := _metCount + 1
if _factorOte
    _metCount := _metCount + 1
if _factorPd
    _metCount := _metCount + 1

g_confMet     := _metCount
g_confEnabled := CONF_FACTOR_COUNT
g_confPercent := _metCount * 100.0 / CONF_FACTOR_COUNT

// --- Tier classification ---

if g_confPercent >= i_confStrongThresholdPct
    g_confTier    := CONF_TIER_STRONG
    g_confTierStr := "STRONG"
else if g_confPercent >= i_confStandardThresholdPct
    g_confTier    := CONF_TIER_STANDARD
    g_confTierStr := "STANDARD"
else
    g_confTier    := CONF_TIER_WEAK
    g_confTierStr := "WEAK"

//#endregion MODULE 15

//#region MODULE 6 -- DELIVERY STATE
// ============================================================================
// MODULE 6 -- DELIVERY STATE
// ============================================================================
// Reads: g_validation (STRUCTURE), g_swingPatternChart (SWING),
//        g_atr14 (UTILITY), i_traderType, i_validationTF,
//        i_showDashboard, i_dashPosition
// Implements the 5-state Delivery State classifier.
// filter on verdict display priority -- they do NOT control whether either
// verdict engine runs.
// States (priority order REVERSAL > CONSOLIDATION > EXPANSION >
//                       CONTINUATION > PULLBACK):
//   DS_REVERSAL     (4) -- CHoCH/MSS against prior trend with prior sweep
//   DS_CONSOLIDATION (2) -- range-bound with no BOS/CHoCH/MSS in window
//   DS_EXPANSION    (0) -- BOS in bias direction, aligned impulse, no retrace
//   DS_CONTINUATION (3) -- zone rejection + new BOS after PULLBACK
//   DS_PULLBACK     (1) -- retracing against bias, no CHoCH/MSS fired
//     Context TF direction
//     IMPULSE module delivers the formal E02 consolidation detector
//     OB/FVG/BB zone-rejection logic
//     prior-trend sweep detection

// --- State enum constants ---

var int DS_EXPANSION     = 0
var int DS_PULLBACK      = 1
var int DS_CONSOLIDATION = 2
var int DS_CONTINUATION  = 3
var int DS_REVERSAL      = 4

// --- DELIVERY_STATE globals ---

var int    g_deliveryState    = 1
var int    g_chartTfPhase     = DS_PULLBACK

// --- Trader-type-scaled consolidation lookback ---
// type preset scaling.

CFG_DS_CONSOL_LOOKBACK = i_traderType == "Scalper" ? 5 : i_traderType == "Swing" ? 12 : 8

// Range of last N bars on current TF within 1.0 x ATR, plus gate on absence
// of BOS/CHoCH/MSS within the lookback window.

_consolHigh = ta.highest(high, CFG_DS_CONSOL_LOOKBACK)
_consolLow  = ta.lowest(low,  CFG_DS_CONSOL_LOOKBACK)
_consolRange = _consolHigh - _consolLow
_rangeWithinAtr = g_atr14 > 0 and _consolRange <= (1.0 * g_atr14)

// "No structural event within lookback" -- check bar-age of last event vs
// the consolidation window. If g_validation.anchorBarIdx is na (no event
// ever), treat as no recent event.
_lastEventAge = na(g_validation.anchorBarIdx) ? 9999 : (bar_index - g_validation.anchorBarIdx)
_noRecentStructural = _lastEventAge > CFG_DS_CONSOL_LOOKBACK

// detector from MODULE 10 IMPULSE owns g_consolidation as the canonical
// signal. The range-within-ATR proxy computed above is retained
// as a fallback and OR'd with the formal detector so a transient window
// where both systems disagree biases toward "consolidation present" --
// which is the conservative (more stable) classifier outcome.
_e02ConsolFired = g_consolidation or (_rangeWithinAtr and _noRecentStructural)

// --- Classifier function (priority cascade) ---
// Returns the new state given the prior state and current triggers.
// Pine v6 requires UDT na checks to be explicit (nz() rejects UDTs).

f_classifyDeliveryState(int _priorState, int _biasDir,
                         bool _valFired, int _valDir, string _valType,
                         bool _e02Fired,
                         bool _zoneRejection, bool _priorSweep,
                         bool _displacementAligned, bool _priceStillAdvancing) =>
    _result = _priorState

    // Clause 1 -- REVERSAL (highest priority)
    // CHoCH or MSS fired on Validation TF AGAINST prior trend AND prior
    // sweep of prior-trend liquidity detected within recency window.
    // CHoCH/MSS still fire and flow through to the state machine via
    // f_advanceDeliveryState for transition-based reversal detection below.
    _reversalFired = _valFired and (_valType == EVT_TYPE_CHOCH or _valType == EVT_TYPE_MSS) and _biasDir != 0 and _valDir != _biasDir and _priorSweep

    // Clause 2 -- CONSOLIDATION
    _consolFired = _e02Fired

    // Clause 3 -- EXPANSION
    // Most recent validation event is a BOS in bias direction on Validation
    // TF AND current bar shows aligned displacement AND price has not yet
    // started retracing.
    _expansionFired = _valFired and (_valType == EVT_TYPE_BOS_PLAIN or _valType == EVT_TYPE_BOS_DISP) and _biasDir != 0 and _valDir == _biasDir and _displacementAligned and _priceStillAdvancing

    // Clause 4 -- CONTINUATION
    // Prior state was PULLBACK AND price has rejected from a zone AND a new
    // BOS has fired in bias direction.
    // the state machine falls through to PULLBACK or persists prior state.
    _continuationFired = _priorState == 1 and _zoneRejection and _valFired and (_valType == EVT_TYPE_BOS_PLAIN or _valType == EVT_TYPE_BOS_DISP) and _biasDir != 0 and _valDir == _biasDir

    if _reversalFired
        _result := 4
    else if _consolFired
        _result := 2
    else if _expansionFired
        _result := 0
    else if _continuationFired
        _result := 3

    _result

// Layered on top of the priority classifier for cases where the classifier
// returns the prior state but a transition-specific trigger should fire
// anyway. Primary example: CHoCH/MSS against bias from PULLBACK should

f_advanceDeliveryState(int _priorState, int _classifierState,
                        bool _valFired, int _valDir, string _valType,
                        int _biasDir) =>
    _result = _classifierState

    // will enforce via the classifier clause 1).
    if _priorState == 1 and _valFired and (_valType == EVT_TYPE_CHOCH or _valType == EVT_TYPE_MSS) and _biasDir != 0 and _valDir != _biasDir
        _result := 4

    // REVERSAL -> EXPANSION on first BOS in new direction.
    if _priorState == 4 and _valFired and (_valType == EVT_TYPE_BOS_PLAIN or _valType == EVT_TYPE_BOS_DISP)
        _result := 0

    // EXPANSION -> PULLBACK on first validation-TF close against bias
