// retain MARKET STATE label at this row; spacer applies to cols 0-2 (cont)
// and cols 5-7 (rev) only. Helper consumes g_marketState full-name strings
// (active engine), REVERSAL EMERGING keys off _revDir; other states ignore
// directions. Signature matches f_centreTint / f_stateColor pattern. Pine
// v6 transparency convention: second arg to color.new() is transparency
// 0-100 (0=opaque, 100=invisible).
f_spacerColor(string _state, int _contDir, int _revDir) =>
    color _sp_r = color.new(COL_BG_BRAND, 15)  // DUAL STAND-DOWN default (matches centre hinge)
    if _state == VOCAB_STATE_CONT_PHASE
        _sp_r := _contDir == DIR_BULL ? color.new(COL_VERDICT_BUY, 35) : color.new(COL_VERDICT_SELL, 35)
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_REV_EMERG
        _sp_r := _revDir == DIR_BULL ? color.new(COL_VERDICT_BUY, 35) : color.new(COL_VERDICT_SELL, 35)
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_DUAL_ACT
        _sp_r := color.new(COL_VERDICT_BUY, 40)
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_DIVERGENT
        _sp_r := color.new(COL_VERDICT_CONFLICT, 35)
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_COND_RIPE
        _sp_r := color.new(COL_STATE_RIPENING, 50)
    // DUAL STAND-DOWN falls through to default (matches centre hinge tint)
    _sp_r

// is the cell BG wash. Both apply to centre cells.
f_stateColor(string _state, int _contDir, int _revDir) =>
    color _stcol_r = COL_TX_DIM
    if _state == VOCAB_STATE_CONT_PHASE
        _stcol_r := _contDir == DIR_BULL ? COL_BULL : COL_BEAR
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_DUAL_ACT
        _stcol_r := COL_AMBER
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_REV_EMERG
        _stcol_r := _revDir == DIR_BULL ? COL_BULL : COL_BEAR
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_DIVERGENT
        _stcol_r := COL_AMBER
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_COND_RIPE
        _stcol_r := COL_NEUTRAL
    // DUAL STAND-DOWN: COL_TX_DIM default
    _stcol_r

// (space-dot-space) for separator instead of middot U+00B7 (not whitelisted).
f_subLine(string _state, int _contDir, int _revDir) =>
    string _sub_dir = "FLAT"
    string _sub_dom = "DORMANT"
    if _state == VOCAB_STATE_CONT_PHASE
        _sub_dir := _contDir == DIR_BULL ? "BULL" : "BEAR"
        _sub_dom := "CONT DOMINANT"
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_REV_EMERG
        _sub_dir := _revDir == DIR_BULL ? "BULL" : "BEAR"
        _sub_dom := "REV DOMINANT"
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_DUAL_ACT
        _sub_dir := "MIXED"
        _sub_dom := "BOTH ACTIVE"
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_DIVERGENT
        _sub_dir := "OPPOSED"
        _sub_dom := "ENGINES SPLIT"
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_COND_RIPE
        _sub_dir := "FORMING"
        _sub_dom := "PRE-PHASE"
    _sub_dir + " . " + _sub_dom

// dominant-engine condition tally to a both-engine percentage dominance line
//   Line 1: literal "STATE" label
//   Line 2: state name + direction-aware glyph (suppressed for ripening / standdown)
//   Line 3: dominance descriptor:
//     DIVERGENT SIGNALS -> "Conflict" (title case; distinct from [!! CONFLICT]
//     all other states  -> "Cont {pct}% . Rev {pct}%"
// Separator " . " (space-dot-space, ASCII) matches f_subLine and f_bannerVoice.
f_stateCardDominance(string _state, int _contDir, int _revDir, float _contPct, float _revPct) =>
    string _sc_glyph = ""
    if _state == VOCAB_STATE_CONT_PHASE
        _sc_glyph := _contDir == DIR_BULL ? GLYPH_DIR_BULL : GLYPH_DIR_BEAR
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_REV_EMERG
        _sc_glyph := _revDir == DIR_BULL ? GLYPH_DIR_BULL : GLYPH_DIR_BEAR
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_DUAL_ACT
        _sc_glyph := GLYPH_DIR_MIX
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_DIVERGENT
        _sc_glyph := GLYPH_STATUS_WARNING
    string _sc_dom = "Cont " + str.tostring(_contPct, "0") + "% . Rev " + str.tostring(_revPct, "0") + "%"
    if _state == VOCAB_STATE_DUAL_DOWN
        _sc_dom := "Both inactive"
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_DIVERGENT
        _sc_dom := "Conflict"
    string _sc_glyphSeg = str.length(_sc_glyph) > 0 ? " " + _sc_glyph : ""
    "STATE\n" + _state + _sc_glyphSeg + "\n" + _sc_dom

// Single-line observational commentary keyed by market state.
f_bannerVoice(string _state, int _contDir, int _revDir) =>
    string _vox_r = "neither engine active . awaiting catalyst"
    if _state == VOCAB_STATE_CONT_PHASE
        _vox_r := _contDir == DIR_BULL ? "trend extending . institutions adding" : "trend extending lower . sellers in control"
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_DUAL_ACT
        _vox_r := "both engines firing . regime engagement"
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_REV_EMERG
        _vox_r := "counter-trend signal forming . watch low timeframe confirmation"
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_DIVERGENT
        _vox_r := "engines disagree . wait for resolution before acting"
    else if _state == VOCAB_STATE_COND_RIPE
        _vox_r := "setup forming . threshold approach in progress"
    _vox_r

// point contribution from the active engine's VerdictState UDT, applies
// the >=10 point material threshold, and returns one of:
//   "driven by [comp1] + [comp2]"   (both >= 10 points)
//   "driven by [comp1]"             (only top-1 >= 10 points)
//   ""                              (no component >= 10 points -- caller
//                                    falls back to f_bannerVoice)
// Component map (8 fields from VerdictState extension at Module 16):
//   biasComp / conComp / kzComp / impComp / liqComp / volComp /
//   plus VOL / BONUS / IDM (no VOCAB_COMP_* constants for the latter
//   three -- inline literals chosen to match component-breakdown
//   string format already used in the engine helpers).
// State-conditional phrasing variations ("leaning on" for transitional
// for all states. Format consistent with f_bannerVoice ASCII discipline
// (no em dash / smart quotes).
f_whyState(VerdictState _v) =>
    array<float>  _why_vals   = array.from(_v.biasComp, _v.conComp, _v.kzComp, _v.impComp, _v.liqComp, _v.volComp, _v.bonusComp, _v.bonusInducement)
    array<string> _why_labels = array.from(VOCAB_COMP_BIAS, VOCAB_COMP_CON, VOCAB_COMP_KZ, VOCAB_COMP_IMP, VOCAB_COMP_LIQ, "VOL", "BONUS", "IDM")
    float  _why_max1 = 0.0
    float  _why_max2 = 0.0
    string _why_lbl1 = ""
    string _why_lbl2 = ""
    for _why_i = 0 to array.size(_why_vals) - 1
        float _why_v = array.get(_why_vals, _why_i)
        if _why_v >= 10.0
            if _why_v > _why_max1
                _why_max2 := _why_max1
                _why_lbl2 := _why_lbl1
                _why_max1 := _why_v
                _why_lbl1 := array.get(_why_labels, _why_i)
            else if _why_v > _why_max2
                _why_max2 := _why_v
                _why_lbl2 := array.get(_why_labels, _why_i)
    string _why_r = ""
    if _why_max1 >= 10.0 and _why_max2 >= 10.0
        _why_r := "driven by " + _why_lbl1 + " + " + _why_lbl2
    else if _why_max1 >= 10.0
        _why_r := "driven by " + _why_lbl1
    _why_r

//   structural: ob / fvg / bb / bpr / swp / ote / p/d
//               (idx 0, 1, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14)
//   level-based: vwap / ema / pdh / pvw          (idx 2, 5, 8, 9)
//   timing: kz / bos / sw / vol                  (idx 3, 4, 6, 7)
// Of FIRED factors only, computes percentage in each category. Returns:
//   "structural-led"  -- structural >= 60% of fired factors
//   "level-based"     -- level-based >= 60% of fired factors
//   "mixed"           -- neither category dominates (default)
// confluence percentage) are not currently emitted by this helper --
// the existing CONF tier (STRONG/STANDARD/WEAK) already conveys
// strength via i_confStrongThresholdPct + i_confStandardThresholdPct.
// Reads g_confFactors[15] (Module 15) directly. Pine v6 scope constraint:
// reads var globals only (no mutation).
f_confluenceCategory() =>
    int _cc_struct = (array.get(g_confFactors, 0)  ? 1 : 0) +
                     (array.get(g_confFactors, 1)  ? 1 : 0) +
                     (array.get(g_confFactors, 10) ? 1 : 0) +
                     (array.get(g_confFactors, 11) ? 1 : 0) +
                     (array.get(g_confFactors, 12) ? 1 : 0) +
                     (array.get(g_confFactors, 13) ? 1 : 0) +
                     (array.get(g_confFactors, 14) ? 1 : 0)
    int _cc_level  = (array.get(g_confFactors, 2) ? 1 : 0) +
                     (array.get(g_confFactors, 5) ? 1 : 0) +
                     (array.get(g_confFactors, 8) ? 1 : 0) +
                     (array.get(g_confFactors, 9) ? 1 : 0)
    int _cc_timing = (array.get(g_confFactors, 3) ? 1 : 0) +
                     (array.get(g_confFactors, 4) ? 1 : 0) +
                     (array.get(g_confFactors, 6) ? 1 : 0) +
                     (array.get(g_confFactors, 7) ? 1 : 0)
    int _cc_total = _cc_struct + _cc_level + _cc_timing
    string _cc_r = "mixed"
    if _cc_total > 0
        // Integer-math comparison avoids float division drift:
        // structural-led if (_cc_struct / _cc_total) >= 0.60
        if _cc_struct * 100 >= 60 * _cc_total
            _cc_r := "structural-led"
        else if _cc_level * 100 >= 60 * _cc_total
            _cc_r := "level-based"
    _cc_r

// DORMANT / NEUTRAL.
// SHIFTING is applied as a transition detector when alignment differs from
// the state 3 bars ago. That logic lives at main scope where the 3-bar history
// ring buffer (g_alignRawHist) is maintained.
// Function returns 4 raw states; main-scope wrapper applies the SHIFTING
// override via 3-bar history check before assigning to g_alignLast.
// Bar-count tracking happens at main scope (Pine v6 function bodies
// cannot mutate scalar globals).
f_computeAlignment(VerdictState _cont, VerdictState _rev) =>
    string _al_r = "NEUTRAL"
    bool _al_bothBuilding = _cont.level >= VL_READY and _rev.level >= VL_READY
    bool _al_bothInactive = _cont.level == VL_NO_TRADE and _rev.level == VL_NO_TRADE
    bool _al_opposing = _cont.dir != 0 and _rev.dir != 0 and _cont.dir != _rev.dir
    if _al_bothInactive
        _al_r := "DORMANT"
    else if _al_bothBuilding and _al_opposing
        _al_r := "DIVERGENT"
    else if _al_bothBuilding
        _al_r := "STRONG"
    _al_r

// _isContSide selects col range (true = cols 0-2 left, false = cols 5-7
// right). _v carries the engine's VerdictState UDT. _dim suppresses full
// _showConflict appends the CONFLICT modifier glyph next to direction label.
f_renderBannerSide(table _tbl, bool _isContSide, VerdictState _v, bool _dim, bool _showConflict) =>
    int _sd_c0 = _isContSide ? 0 : 5
    int _sd_c1 = _isContSide ? 2 : 7
    string _sd_label = _isContSide ? "CONTINUATION" : "REVERSAL"
    color _sd_bg = _v.dir == DIR_BULL ? COL_VERDICT_BUY : _v.dir == DIR_BEAR ? COL_VERDICT_SELL : COL_VERDICT_FLAT
    if _showConflict
        _sd_bg := COL_VERDICT_CONFLICT
    color _sd_txSecondary = _dim ? COL_TX_MUTED : COL_TX_SECONDARY
    color _sd_txDir = _dim ? COL_TX_DIM : (_v.dir == DIR_BULL ? COL_SUB_BULL : _v.dir == DIR_BEAR ? COL_SUB_BEAR : COL_SUB_READY)
    string _sd_arrow = _v.dir == DIR_BULL ? GLYPH_DIR_BULL : _v.dir == DIR_BEAR ? GLYPH_DIR_BEAR : "-"
    string _sd_dirLabel = _v.dir == DIR_BULL ? "BULL" : _v.dir == DIR_BEAR ? "BEAR" : "FLAT"
    string _sd_conflictTag = _showConflict ? "  " + GLYPH_STATUS_WARNING + " CONFLICT" : ""

    // Bgcolor sourced from f_spacerColor consuming g_marketState + cont/rev
    // directions (read via g_verdictCont / g_verdictRev globals declared
    // rev sides render IDENTICAL spacer colour for unified band reading
    // across the banner top; centre cols 3-4 retain MARKET STATE label at
    // this row (rendered by f_renderBannerCentre). Empty text content keeps
    // the row a pure colour stripe; size.tiny minimises vertical footprint.
    color _sd_spacerBg = f_spacerColor(g_marketState, g_verdictCont.dir, g_verdictRev.dir)
    table.cell(_tbl, _sd_c0, 1, "",
         bgcolor = _sd_spacerBg, text_size = g_dashTiny)
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, _sd_c0, 1, _sd_c1, 1)

    table.cell(_tbl, _sd_c0, 2, _sd_label,
         bgcolor = _sd_bg, text_color = _sd_txSecondary, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, _sd_c0, 2, _sd_c1, 2)

    // Row 3: Verdict line "[tier] [arrow] [direction] [score%]"
    // pairs awkwardly with directional language; INACTIVE-state engines
    // render "INACTIVE  21%" with score retained for context. CONFIRMED /
    // BUILDING / FORMING / EARLY tiers keep the direction caps unchanged.
    string _sd_verdict = _v.level == VL_NO_TRADE ? _v.levelStr + "  " + str.tostring(_v.pct, "0") + "%" : _v.levelStr + "  " + _sd_arrow + "  " + _sd_dirLabel + "  " + str.tostring(_v.pct, "0") + "%" + _sd_conflictTag
    table.cell(_tbl, _sd_c0, 3, _sd_verdict,
         bgcolor = _sd_bg, text_color = _sd_txDir, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = _isContSide ? f_ttBannerCont(_v) : f_ttBannerRev(_v)
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, _sd_c0, 3, _sd_c1, 3)

    table.cell(_tbl, _sd_c0, 4, f_magnitudeBar(_v.pct),
         bgcolor = _sd_bg, text_color = _sd_txDir, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, _sd_c0, 4, _sd_c1, 4)

    // Row 5: Component breakdown. Empty states render compact zero formulas
    // matching the active-engine breakdown format.
    string _sd_break = na(_v.breakdownStr) or _v.breakdownStr == "" ? (_isContSide ? "BIAS 0 + CON 0 + KZ 0 + IMP 0 + LIQ 0 = 0%" : "VAL 0 + SWP 0 + CON 0 + IMP 0 + KZ 0 = 0% (--)") : _v.breakdownStr
    table.cell(_tbl, _sd_c0, 5, _sd_break,
         bgcolor = _sd_bg, text_color = _sd_txSecondary, text_size = g_dashTiny, text_halign = text.align_center
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, _sd_c0, 5, _sd_c1, 5)

    // Row 6: Drift indicator
    table.cell(_tbl, _sd_c0, 6, f_driftStr(_v.drift3),
         bgcolor = _sd_bg, text_color = _dim ? COL_TX_MUTED : COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashTiny, text_halign = text.align_center
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, _sd_c0, 6, _sd_c1, 6)

// Function bodies read these var globals after declaration; mutation happens
// at main scope below the verdict cascade.
var int    g_alignBars = 0
var string g_alignLast = "NEUTRAL"

// Holds the prior 3 RAW classifications (output of f_computeAlignment).
// Main-scope SHIFTING-detection wrapper reads element 0 (oldest = 3 bars
// ago) and compares to the current bar's raw classification; mismatch
// shift on push.
var array<string> g_alignRawHist = array.new_string(0)

// Drives f_driftStr via VerdictState.drift3 field. Each buffer holds
// the prior 3 score percentages; main-scope mutation block reads
// element 0 BEFORE pushing the current score, computes drift = current
// - oldest, and assigns to the engine UDT's drift3 field via main-scope
// UDT field mutation (legal pattern -- Pine v6 restricts scalar function
var array<float> g_contPctHist = array.new_float(0)
var array<float> g_revPctHist  = array.new_float(0)

// Drives f_renderBannerCentre row 6 sparkline render. Parallel arrays track
// continuation/reversal activity and direction per bar. Main-scope mutation
// pushes current-bar state and FIFO-shifts when over cap. Below 8 entries
// (warmup window), the sparkline renders progressively from the right -- no
// to match dashboard activation semantic -- engines below BUILDING are not
// "active" enough to count as a sparkline event; sparkline thus reads as
// "this engine had a meaningful presence on this bar" rather than "this
// engine had any heartbeat".
// Pine v6 lexical precedence: declared BEFORE f_renderBannerCentre per
// mutate scalar globals; array push/shift on var globals is legal at main
// scope only).
var int SPARK_LEN = 8
var array<bool> g_sparkContActive = array.new_bool(0)
var array<bool> g_sparkRevActive  = array.new_bool(0)
var array<int>  g_sparkContDir    = array.new_int(0)
var array<int>  g_sparkRevDir     = array.new_int(0)

// Reads g_marketState (Module 17.5) + g_alignLast / g_alignBars (main-scope
// alignment tracker) for cell content.
f_renderBannerCentre(table _tbl, VerdictState _cont, VerdictState _rev) =>
    string _ct_state = g_marketState
    int _ct_contDir = _cont.dir
    int _ct_revDir = _rev.dir
    // 30) across all market states. Centre reads as architectural moulding
    // / hinge separating the bull/bear/amber engine sides. State-direction
    // difference between cont/rev state bgcolor and flat-grey centre serves
    // hairline fallback path).
    color _ct_tint = color.new(COL_BG_BRAND, 30)
    color _ct_stateCol = f_stateColor(_ct_state, _ct_contDir, _ct_revDir)

    // Row 1: MARKET STATE label
    table.cell(_tbl, 3, 1, "MARKET STATE",
         bgcolor = _ct_tint, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 3, 1, 4, 1)

    // Row 2: State name (one of 6)
    table.cell(_tbl, 3, 2, _ct_state,
         bgcolor = _ct_tint, text_color = _ct_stateCol, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttBannerCentre(_ct_state)
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 3, 2, 4, 2)

    // Row 3: Subline (direction + dominance descriptor)
    table.cell(_tbl, 3, 3, f_subLine(_ct_state, _ct_contDir, _ct_revDir),
         bgcolor = _ct_tint, text_color = _ct_stateCol, text_size = g_dashTiny, text_halign = text.align_center
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 3, 3, 4, 3)

    // voice library fallback. f_whyState identifies top-2 contributing
    // score components from the primary engine; when no component meets
    // the >=10 point material threshold (early bars / DUAL STAND-DOWN /
    // f_renderModifierStrip + footer logic: single-engine case picks the
    bool _ct_conflict = g_showConflictFlag
    bool _ct_single = (g_showContinuation != g_showReversal) and not _ct_conflict
    bool _ct_primaryIsCont = _ct_single ? g_showContinuation : g_primaryEngine == 1
    VerdictState _ct_primary = _ct_primaryIsCont ? _cont : _rev
    string _ct_why = f_whyState(_ct_primary)
    string _ct_voiceTxt = str.length(_ct_why) > 0 ? _ct_why : f_bannerVoice(_ct_state, _ct_contDir, _ct_revDir)
    table.cell(_tbl, 3, 4, _ct_voiceTxt,
         bgcolor = _ct_tint, text_color = COL_TX_PRIMARY, text_size = g_dashTiny, text_halign = text.align_center
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 3, 4, 4, 4)

    // Row 5: Alignment indicator + bar count (read main-scope tracker globals)
    string _ct_align = g_alignLast + " . " + str.tostring(g_alignBars) + " bars"
    table.cell(_tbl, 3, 5, _ct_align,
         bgcolor = _ct_tint, text_color = COL_TX_SECONDARY, text_size = g_dashTiny, text_halign = text.align_center
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 3, 5, 4, 5)

    // Renders last-20-bars engine activity history as 4-state glyph stream
    // g_sparkRevActive drive the per-bar glyph selection; g_sparkContDir +
    // Per-bar glyph encoding:
    //   (cont active, rev active) -> glyph
    //   (true,  true)  -> GLYPH_SPARK_BOTH (◆ U+25C6)
    //   (true,  false) -> GLYPH_DIR_BULL   (▲ U+25B2, dual-role with MSD direction)
    //   (false, true)  -> GLYPH_DIR_BEAR   (▼ U+25BC, dual-role with MSD direction)
    // Below 8 entries (warmup window), renders progressively from the right.
    // Empty buffers render a blank cell without collapsing the banner row.
    // Cell tint matches centre block (COL_BG_BRAND @ 30 transparency,
    // matches f_centreTint hinge effect). Text colour COL_TX_SECONDARY
    // for all 4 glyphs uniformly -- 4-state encoding carries its own
    // Four-state sparkline legend: ▲ cont-only, ▼ rev-only, ◆ both, · neither.
    string _ct_spark = ""
    int _ct_sparkSize = array.size(g_sparkContActive)
    if _ct_sparkSize > 0
        for _ct_i = 0 to _ct_sparkSize - 1
            bool _ct_cAct = array.get(g_sparkContActive, _ct_i)
            bool _ct_rAct = array.get(g_sparkRevActive,  _ct_i)
            _ct_spark := _ct_spark + (_ct_cAct and _ct_rAct ? GLYPH_SPARK_BOTH :
                                      _ct_cAct              ? GLYPH_DIR_BULL   :
                                      _ct_rAct              ? GLYPH_DIR_BEAR   :
                                                              GLYPH_SPARK_NONE)
    table.cell(_tbl, 3, 6, _ct_spark,
         bgcolor = _ct_tint, text_color = COL_TX_SECONDARY, text_size = g_dashTiny, text_halign = text.align_center
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 3, 6, 4, 6)

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 19.1.E END -- score-mode banner helpers
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Renders DUAL STAND-DOWN with dim state text while preserving row layout.
f_renderVerdictFlat(table _tbl, VerdictState _cont, VerdictState _rev) =>
    f_renderBannerSide(_tbl, true, _cont, true, false)
    f_renderBannerCentre(_tbl, _cont, _rev)
    f_renderBannerSide(_tbl, false, _rev, true, false)

// Renders opposing ready engines as a DIVERGENT SIGNALS state with amber
// centre text and conflict warning markers.
f_renderVerdictConflict(table _tbl, VerdictState _cont, VerdictState _rev) =>
    f_renderBannerSide(_tbl, true, _cont, false, true)
    f_renderBannerCentre(_tbl, _cont, _rev)
    f_renderBannerSide(_tbl, false, _rev, false, true)

//   - g_marketState == "REVERSAL EMERGING"  -> cont side dimmed
//   - g_marketState == "DUAL STAND-DOWN"    -> both sides dimmed
//   - all other states (DUAL ACTIVITY / DIVERGENT / RIPENING)        -> both full intensity
// _primaryIsCont retained in signature for caller-side compatibility but
// no longer used internally (dominance is now state-driven via the
f_renderVerdictDual(table _tbl, VerdictState _cont, VerdictState _rev, bool _primaryIsCont) =>
    bool _du_dimCont = g_marketState == VOCAB_STATE_REV_EMERG or g_marketState == VOCAB_STATE_DUAL_DOWN
    bool _du_dimRev = g_marketState == VOCAB_STATE_CONT_PHASE or g_marketState == VOCAB_STATE_DUAL_DOWN
    f_renderBannerSide(_tbl, true, _cont, _du_dimCont, false)
    f_renderBannerCentre(_tbl, _cont, _rev)
    f_renderBannerSide(_tbl, false, _rev, _du_dimRev, false)

// dominance table. Centre always renders the market state (typically
// single-engine configurations).
f_renderVerdictSingle(table _tbl, VerdictState _cont, VerdictState _rev, bool _primaryIsCont) =>
    bool _sn_dimCont = not _primaryIsCont
    bool _sn_dimRev = _primaryIsCont
    f_renderBannerSide(_tbl, true, _cont, _sn_dimCont, false)
    f_renderBannerCentre(_tbl, _cont, _rev)
    f_renderBannerSide(_tbl, false, _rev, _sn_dimRev, false)

// modifier strip row 6 render.
// conflict states, AMBER in CONFLICT.
// Renders single full-width cell (cols 0-7 merged) carrying modifier
// flags from primary engine. Caller pre-resolves _primaryIsCont and
// _isConflict from outer-scope state, and is responsible for the
// modifier strip block with a single call.
f_renderModifierStrip(table _tbl, VerdictState _cont, VerdictState _rev, bool _primaryIsCont, bool _isConflict) =>
    // Left cell: warning flags from primary engine's modifier state.
    // Negative effects use U+2212 MINUS SIGN ([- LIQ] etc.); positive use
    // U+002B PLUS ([+ SWEEP] etc.); context labels unprefixed ([NY OVERLAP]);
    // warnings use !! prefix ([!! CONFLICT]). Strings consumed from VOCAB_MOD_*
    // the bracket+prefix system carries warning semantics now (-- LIQ for
    // negative effect / !! CONFLICT for warning). The CONFLICT-state-only
    // ⚠ glyph reappears via f_modifierStripText helper when CONFLICT mode
    string _vb_modL = ""
    if _primaryIsCont
        if _cont.liquidityDowngraded
            _vb_modL := _vb_modL + VOCAB_MOD_LIQ_DOWN + "  "
        if _cont.sweepBoosted
            _vb_modL := _vb_modL + VOCAB_MOD_SWEEP_BOOST + "  "
        if _cont.nyOverlapBoosted
            _vb_modL := _vb_modL + VOCAB_MOD_NY_OVERLAP + "  "
        if _cont.lunchCapped
            _vb_modL := _vb_modL + VOCAB_MOD_LUNCH_CAP + "  "
        if _cont.consolidationCapped
            _vb_modL := _vb_modL + VOCAB_MOD_CONSOL_CAP + "  "
        if _cont.validationConflicted
            _vb_modL := _vb_modL + VOCAB_MOD_VAL_CONFLICT + "  "
    else
        if _rev.bbRetestBoosted
            _vb_modL := _vb_modL + VOCAB_MOD_BB_RETEST + "  "
        if _rev.nyOverlapBoosted
            _vb_modL := _vb_modL + VOCAB_MOD_NY_OVERLAP + "  "
        if _rev.lunchCapped
            _vb_modL := _vb_modL + VOCAB_MOD_LUNCH_CAP + "  "
        if _rev.consolidationCapped
            _vb_modL := _vb_modL + VOCAB_MOD_CONSOL_CAP + "  "
    // (empty cells communicate "nothing active" without dim-text noise).

    // CONFLICT state retains amber override.
    color _mod_bg = _isConflict ? color.new(COL_AMBER, 80) : COL_BG_MODIFIER_PURPLE

    // Empty-strip case renders an empty cell with background colour only.
    table.cell(_tbl, 0, 7, _vb_modL,
         bgcolor = _mod_bg, text_color = COL_AMBER, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_left,
         tooltip = f_ttModifierStrip(_cont, _rev)
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 0, 7, 7, 7)

// machine. Renders only when CW state is ARMED (1) or CONFIRMING (2);
// caller responsible for the `i_showPendingStrip and _vb_cwPending`
// gate. No UDT params; all state from primitive globals (g_cwState,
// g_cwDir, g_cwStartBar, g_sweepPoolPrice, bar_index) +
f_renderPendingStrip(table _tbl) =>
    int _vb_cwAge = bar_index - g_cwStartBar
    int _vb_cwRemain = i_confirmationWindowBars - _vb_cwAge
    string _vb_cwDirStr = g_cwDir == 1 ? "bull" : g_cwDir == -1 ? "bear" : ""
    string _vb_cwStateStr = g_cwState == 1 ? "awaiting close confirmation" : "awaiting follow-through"
    string _vb_cwL = "⌛ PENDING  |  " + _vb_cwDirStr + " sweep at " + str.tostring(g_sweepPoolPrice, format.mintick) + "  |  " + _vb_cwStateStr
    string _vb_cwR = str.tostring(_vb_cwRemain) + " of " + str.tostring(i_confirmationWindowBars) + " bars remaining"

    // tiny -> small per Tier 2 body discipline. Both cells (left content +
    // right countdown) migrate together to preserve in-row visual weight.
    table.cell(_tbl, 0, 8, _vb_cwL,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_PENDING, text_color = COL_TX_PENDING, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_left
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 0, 8, 2, 8)
    table.cell(_tbl, 3, 8, _vb_cwR,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_PENDING, text_color = COL_TX_PENDING_STRONG, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_right
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 3, 8, 7, 8)

f_clearPendingStrip(table _tbl) =>
    table.cell(_tbl, 0, 8, "",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_BASE, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashTiny, text_halign = text.align_left
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 0, 8, 2, 8)
    table.cell(_tbl, 3, 8, "",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_BASE, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashTiny, text_halign = text.align_right
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 3, 8, 7, 8)

// MSD body row render (one of 7 rows, MSD index 0-6 -> table rows 13-19
// original 8-col schema (TF / SWING H/L / DIR/STRUCT / OB↑ / OB↓ /
// split into a single-column FVG cell and inserts a single-column BB
// cell at col 5, producing the lifecycle-ordered layout: TF / SWING H/L
// COL_BG_ROW_ALT/COL_BG_ROW otherwise -- delegated to f_msdRowBg
// compose helpers. SWING H/L tier-coloured via dedicated COL_TIER_*
// discount). OB and FVG split into above-/below-price cells; each side
// (PRISTINE/TESTED/INVALIDATED/NONE). No UDT params; arrays + primitive
f_renderMsdRow(table _tbl, int _row, string _tfLabel) =>
    int _msd_tableRow = 13 + _row

    [_msd_phase, _msd_dir, _msd_phaseStr] = f_classifyTfPhase(_row)

    // TRN = bull green / bear coral, PUL = amber, CON = dim slate.
    string _msd_phaseGlyph = _msd_phase == PHASE_TRENDING ? (_msd_dir == 1 ? "▲" : "▼") : _msd_phase == PHASE_PULLING ? "↔" : "◦"
    color  _msd_phaseCol   = _msd_phase == PHASE_TRENDING ? (_msd_dir == 1 ? COL_BULL : COL_BEAR) : _msd_phase == PHASE_PULLING ? COL_AMBER : COL_NEUTRAL
    string _msd_phaseTxt   = _msd_phaseGlyph + " " + _msd_phaseStr

    // Context row detection: row's TF string matches i_tfContext.
    // Applied as cell bgcolor across all 8 columns in this row. Bg
    // computation delegated to f_msdRowBg compose helper.
    bool _msd_isCtx = _tfLabel == i_tfContext
    color _msd_rowBg = f_msdRowBg(_row, _msd_isCtx)

    // Col 0 -- TF label (brighter on context row, dim elsewhere).
    color _msd_tfCol = _msd_isCtx ? COL_DS_AMBER : COL_TX_PRIMARY
    // label column 0 bumps further size.small -> size.normal -- TF labels
    // are row identifiers (header-class) not body data; matching col-0
    // COL_BG_LEFT_ANCHOR -- col 0 becomes a continuous slate-tinted left
    // stripe across all 8 rows (TF header + 7 body). Context-row visual
    // Context signal: cols 1-7 still receive _msd_rowBg (which tints
    // COL_BG_ROW_CTX teal on the context row), while col 0 retains its
    // amber TF-label text colour on the context row -- the colour cue
    // shifts from cell-bg to row-extent + label-text rather than
    // disappearing.
    table.cell(_tbl, 0, _msd_tableRow, _tfLabel,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_LEFT_ANCHOR, text_color = _msd_tfCol, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center
         )

    // Consumes g_msdRangeHigh/Low + g_msdReclaimLow/High populated by the
    // per-timeframe reclaim state machine. g_msdRangePct drives PREM (>66%) /
    // EQ (33-66%) / DISC (<33%)
    // text colour via COL_TIER_WEAK / COL_AMBER / COL_TIER_STRONG. For
    // sweep states the percentile naturally falls outside [0, 100], mapping
    // to TIER_WEAK / TIER_STRONG for above / below sweeps respectively --
    // visual reinforcement of the bar's directional state.
    float _msd_pct       = array.get(g_msdRangePct,   _row)
    float _msd_rangeHigh = array.get(g_msdRangeHigh,  _row)
    float _msd_rangeLow  = array.get(g_msdRangeLow,   _row)
    bool  _msd_reclaimLo = array.get(g_msdReclaimLow,  _row)
    bool  _msd_reclaimHi = array.get(g_msdReclaimHigh, _row)
    string _msd_swingBar = f_msdSwingPositionBar(_msd_rangeHigh, _msd_rangeLow, close, _msd_reclaimLo, _msd_reclaimHi)
    string _msd_rangeWidth = (na(_msd_rangeHigh) or na(_msd_rangeLow)) ? "" : "  " + f_fmtDistance(_msd_rangeHigh - _msd_rangeLow)
    string _msd_swingTxt = _msd_swingBar + _msd_rangeWidth
    string _msd_pdLabel = na(_msd_pct) ? "" : _msd_pct > 60.0 ? " [PRM]" : _msd_pct < 40.0 ? " [DSC]" : " [EQ]"
    string _msd_oteLabel = na(_msd_pct) ? "" : ((_msd_pct >= 62.0 and _msd_pct <= 79.0) or (_msd_pct >= 21.0 and _msd_pct <= 38.0)) ? " [OTE]" : ""
    string _msd_swpLabel = na(_msd_pct) ? "" : (_msd_pct >= (100.0 - i_swingProxPct) or _msd_pct <= i_swingProxPct) ? " [SWP]" : ""
    if i_pdLabelEnabled
        _msd_swingTxt := _msd_swingTxt + _msd_pdLabel + _msd_oteLabel + _msd_swpLabel
    color  _msd_swingCol = COL_TX_MUTED
    if not na(_msd_pct)
        if _msd_pct > 66.0
            _msd_swingCol := COL_TIER_WEAK
        else if _msd_pct < 33.0
            _msd_swingCol := COL_TIER_STRONG
        else
            _msd_swingCol := COL_AMBER
    table.cell(_tbl, 1, _msd_tableRow, _msd_swingTxt,
         bgcolor = _msd_rowBg, text_color = _msd_swingCol, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttMsdSwing(_tfLabel, _msd_pct)
         )

    // textual chart of last 8 swing pivots normalised to recent range,
    // plus trailing bias arrow (↑ HH-HL / ↓ LL-LH / → MIX). Bias source:
    // g_msdStructPattern[row] from Module 4. Pivot data: per-TF buffer
    // pairs from Module 3 (g_msdSwingHigh1..7 / g_msdSwingLow1..7).
    // Per-TF buffer dispatch is unavoidable because each TF has its own
    // buffer global -- Pine v6 cannot dynamically index globals via row
    // number. 7-way switch on _row matches the OB / FVG dispatch pattern
    // already used in Module 3 (lines 1145+).
    // Cell text colour reflects structural bias (g_msdStructPattern):
    // bar tier colour (which keys off range percentile g_msdRangePct).
    int _msd_struct = array.get(g_msdStructPattern, _row)
    string _msd_sparkTxt = ""
    if _row == 0
        _msd_sparkTxt := f_msdStructureSparkline(g_msdSwingHigh1, g_msdSwingLow1, _msd_struct)
    else if _row == 1
        _msd_sparkTxt := f_msdStructureSparkline(g_msdSwingHigh2, g_msdSwingLow2, _msd_struct)
    else if _row == 2
        _msd_sparkTxt := f_msdStructureSparkline(g_msdSwingHigh3, g_msdSwingLow3, _msd_struct)
    else if _row == 3
        _msd_sparkTxt := f_msdStructureSparkline(g_msdSwingHigh4, g_msdSwingLow4, _msd_struct)
    else if _row == 4
        _msd_sparkTxt := f_msdStructureSparkline(g_msdSwingHigh5, g_msdSwingLow5, _msd_struct)
    else if _row == 5
        _msd_sparkTxt := f_msdStructureSparkline(g_msdSwingHigh6, g_msdSwingLow6, _msd_struct)
    else
        _msd_sparkTxt := f_msdStructureSparkline(g_msdSwingHigh7, g_msdSwingLow7, _msd_struct)
    color _msd_dirCol = _msd_struct == 1 ? COL_BULL : _msd_struct == -1 ? COL_BEAR : COL_NEUTRAL
    table.cell(_tbl, 2, _msd_tableRow, _msd_sparkTxt,
         bgcolor = _msd_rowBg, text_color = _msd_dirCol, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttMsdStructure(_tfLabel, _msd_struct)
         )

    // Col 3 -- OB↑ above-price Order Block (resistance). 4-state mitigation
    // /NONE). BEAR colour (resistance above current close).
    // migrated from glyph idiom (●/○/[T]) to bracket+text idiom ([UF]/[PF]/
    // FILLED) via STR_ZONE_* constants. NONE renders as em dash STR_ZONE_NONE
    // emits PRISTINE / NONE so [PF] / FILLED labels won't fire visually yet,
    // but display mapping is pre-wired so 7.6.4 only touches the populator.
    float _msd_obA = array.get(g_msdObAbovePrice, _row)
    int   _msd_obAs = array.get(g_msdObAboveState, _row)
    string _msd_obAboveTxt = STR_ZONE_NONE
    color  _msd_obAboveCol = COL_TX_MUTED
    if _msd_obAs != OB_STATE_NONE and not na(_msd_obA)
        string _msd_obALabel = _msd_obAs == OB_STATE_INVALIDATED ? STR_ZONE_INVALID : _msd_obAs == OB_STATE_TESTED ? STR_ZONE_TESTED : STR_ZONE_PRISTINE
        _msd_obAboveTxt := _msd_obALabel + " " + f_fmtDistance(_msd_obA - close)
        _msd_obAboveCol := COL_BEAR
    table.cell(_tbl, 3, _msd_tableRow, _msd_obAboveTxt,
         bgcolor = _msd_rowBg, text_color = _msd_obAboveCol, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttMsdOB(_tfLabel, false, _msd_obAs, _msd_obA - close)
         )

    // Col 4 -- OB↓ below-price Order Block (support). Same 4-state contract
    // as OB↑ + token edits 6 + 2 applied identically. BULL colour (support
    // below current close).
    float _msd_obB = array.get(g_msdObBelowPrice, _row)
    int   _msd_obBs = array.get(g_msdObBelowState, _row)
    string _msd_obBelowTxt = STR_ZONE_NONE
    color  _msd_obBelowCol = COL_TX_MUTED
    if _msd_obBs != OB_STATE_NONE and not na(_msd_obB)
        string _msd_obBLabel = _msd_obBs == OB_STATE_INVALIDATED ? STR_ZONE_INVALID : _msd_obBs == OB_STATE_TESTED ? STR_ZONE_TESTED : STR_ZONE_PRISTINE
        _msd_obBelowTxt := _msd_obBLabel + " " + f_fmtDistance(close - _msd_obB)
        _msd_obBelowCol := COL_BULL
    table.cell(_tbl, 4, _msd_tableRow, _msd_obBelowTxt,
         bgcolor = _msd_rowBg, text_color = _msd_obBelowCol, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttMsdOB(_tfLabel, true, _msd_obBs, close - _msd_obB)
         )

    // display vocabulary:
    //   BB_STATE_PRISTINE    -> [UF]   (active untested)
    //   BB_STATE_TESTED      -> [PF]   (tested with rejection)
    //   BB_STATE_INVALIDATED -> FILLED (50-bar post-expiry display)
    //   BB_STATE_NONE        -> em dash STR_ZONE_NONE
    // ▼ bear BB above price (resistance). Both-direction tiebreak applies
    // nearer wins by absolute pip distance from close; equidistant case
    // prefers bias-aligned BB (bear bias -> bear BB above; bull or zero
    // bias -> bull BB below). Inline rendering pattern mirrors OB cousin
    // blocks above per D4 style refinement (locals -> active flags ->
    // useAbove resolution -> label + glyph + pips composition -> table.cell).
    float _msd_bbA  = array.get(g_msdBbAbovePrice, _row)
    int   _msd_bbAs = array.get(g_msdBbAboveState, _row)
    float _msd_bbB  = array.get(g_msdBbBelowPrice, _row)
    int   _msd_bbBs = array.get(g_msdBbBelowState, _row)
    string _msd_bbTxt = STR_ZONE_NONE
    color  _msd_bbCol = COL_TX_MUTED
    string _msd_bbTooltip = "No active Breaker Block on this timeframe. BB retest becomes relevant only when a fresh failed-OB-in-reversed-role is present."
    bool _msd_bbAboveActive = _msd_bbAs != BB_STATE_NONE and not na(_msd_bbA)
    bool _msd_bbBelowActive = _msd_bbBs != BB_STATE_NONE and not na(_msd_bbB)
    if _msd_bbAboveActive or _msd_bbBelowActive
        bool _msd_bbUseAbove = false
        if _msd_bbAboveActive and not _msd_bbBelowActive
            _msd_bbUseAbove := true
        else if _msd_bbBelowActive and not _msd_bbAboveActive
            _msd_bbUseAbove := false
        else
            // Both active -- nearer wins; equidistant -> bias-aligned (bear
            float _msd_bbDistA = _msd_bbA - close
            float _msd_bbDistB = close - _msd_bbB
            if _msd_bbDistA < _msd_bbDistB
                _msd_bbUseAbove := true
            else if _msd_bbDistA > _msd_bbDistB
                _msd_bbUseAbove := false
            else
                _msd_bbUseAbove := g_contextBiasDir == -1
        int _msd_bbState = _msd_bbUseAbove ? _msd_bbAs : _msd_bbBs
        string _msd_bbLabel = _msd_bbState == BB_STATE_INVALIDATED ? STR_ZONE_INVALID : _msd_bbState == BB_STATE_TESTED ? STR_ZONE_TESTED : STR_ZONE_PRISTINE
        string _msd_bbGlyph = _msd_bbUseAbove ? GLYPH_DIR_BEAR : GLYPH_DIR_BULL
        _msd_bbTxt := _msd_bbGlyph + " " + _msd_bbLabel + " " + f_fmtDistance(_msd_bbUseAbove ? _msd_bbA - close : close - _msd_bbB)
        _msd_bbCol := _msd_bbUseAbove ? COL_BEAR : COL_BULL
        _msd_bbTooltip := f_ttMsdBB(_tfLabel, not _msd_bbUseAbove, _msd_bbState, _msd_bbUseAbove ? _msd_bbA - close : close - _msd_bbB)
    table.cell(_tbl, 5, _msd_tableRow, _msd_bbTxt,
         bgcolor = _msd_rowBg, text_color = _msd_bbCol, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = _msd_bbTooltip
         )

    // Col 6 -- FVG Fair Value Gap (collapsed from prior FVG↑ + FVG↓ split
    // case prefers bias-aligned gap (bear bias -> bear gap above; bull or
    // bullish FVG below (support gap), ▼ bearish FVG above (resistance gap).
    // Inline rendering pattern mirrors BB cousin block above per D4 style
    float _msd_fvgA  = array.get(g_msdFvgAbovePrice, _row)
    int   _msd_fvgAs = array.get(g_msdFvgAboveState, _row)
    float _msd_fvgB  = array.get(g_msdFvgBelowPrice, _row)
    int   _msd_fvgBs = array.get(g_msdFvgBelowState, _row)
    string _msd_fvgTxt = STR_ZONE_NONE
    color  _msd_fvgCol = COL_TX_MUTED
    string _msd_fvgTooltip = "No active FVG on this timeframe. FVG context appears when a fresh imbalance sits near current price."
    bool _msd_fvgAboveActive = _msd_fvgAs != FVG_STATE_NONE and not na(_msd_fvgA)
    bool _msd_fvgBelowActive = _msd_fvgBs != FVG_STATE_NONE and not na(_msd_fvgB)
    if _msd_fvgAboveActive or _msd_fvgBelowActive
        bool _msd_fvgUseAbove = false
        if _msd_fvgAboveActive and not _msd_fvgBelowActive
            _msd_fvgUseAbove := true
        else if _msd_fvgBelowActive and not _msd_fvgAboveActive
            _msd_fvgUseAbove := false
        else
            float _msd_fvgDistA = _msd_fvgA - close
            float _msd_fvgDistB = close - _msd_fvgB
            if _msd_fvgDistA < _msd_fvgDistB
                _msd_fvgUseAbove := true
            else if _msd_fvgDistA > _msd_fvgDistB
                _msd_fvgUseAbove := false
            else
                _msd_fvgUseAbove := g_contextBiasDir == -1
        int _msd_fvgState = _msd_fvgUseAbove ? _msd_fvgAs : _msd_fvgBs
        string _msd_fvgLabel = _msd_fvgState == FVG_STATE_INVALIDATED ? STR_ZONE_INVALID : _msd_fvgState == FVG_STATE_TESTED ? STR_ZONE_TESTED : STR_ZONE_PRISTINE

        // Variant D BPR annotation: chart-TF row only. Walks g_fvgs PARENT
        // records reading the inBpr flag (NOT a re-scan of g_bprs ranges --
        // that approach would lose BPR-involvement when a BPR mitigates or
        // RANGE overlap between the displayed MSD FVG price-with-tolerance
        // window and the parent FVG's range, not single-price-in-range.
        // Chart-TF detection via timeframe.in_seconds(_tfLabel) ==
        // timeframe.in_seconds() -- robust against TF-string aliasing
        // Cost bound: chart-TF row only, <= i_fvgMaxZones (default 5, cap
        // 50) iterations per bar, walk short-circuits on first hit.
        bool _msd_fvgInBpr = false
        if timeframe.in_seconds(_tfLabel) == timeframe.in_seconds() and array.size(g_fvgs) > 0
            float _msd_fvgPx  = _msd_fvgUseAbove ? _msd_fvgA : _msd_fvgB
            float _msd_fvgTol = na(g_atr14) ? 0.0 : g_atr14 * i_fvgProximityPct
            float _msd_fvgTop = _msd_fvgPx + _msd_fvgTol
            float _msd_fvgBot = _msd_fvgPx - _msd_fvgTol
            for _fi = 0 to array.size(g_fvgs) - 1
                _fvg = array.get(g_fvgs, _fi)
                if _fvg.inBpr and _fvg.state != ZS_MITIGATED and _fvg.state != ZS_EXPIRED
                    // Range overlap test: two intervals [a1,a2] and [b1,b2]
                    // overlap iff min(a2,b2) > max(a1,b1).
                    if math.min(_msd_fvgTop, _fvg.priceTop) > math.max(_msd_fvgBot, _fvg.priceBottom)
                        _msd_fvgInBpr := true
                        break
        if _msd_fvgInBpr
            // each STR_ZONE_* token to its BPR-annotated form.
            _msd_fvgLabel := _msd_fvgState == FVG_STATE_INVALIDATED ? STR_ZONE_INVALID + "·BPR" : _msd_fvgState == FVG_STATE_TESTED ? "[PF·BPR]" : "[UF·BPR]"
        string _msd_fvgGlyph = _msd_fvgUseAbove ? GLYPH_DIR_BEAR : GLYPH_DIR_BULL
        _msd_fvgTxt := _msd_fvgGlyph + " " + _msd_fvgLabel + " " + f_fmtDistance(_msd_fvgUseAbove ? _msd_fvgA - close : close - _msd_fvgB)
        _msd_fvgCol := _msd_fvgInBpr ? BPR_COLOR : (_msd_fvgUseAbove ? COL_BEAR : COL_BULL)
        _msd_fvgTooltip := f_ttMsdFVG(_tfLabel, not _msd_fvgUseAbove, _msd_fvgState, _msd_fvgUseAbove ? _msd_fvgA - close : close - _msd_fvgB, _msd_fvgInBpr)
    else
        // Option α / R-a: proxy reports no FVG above or below close, but a
        // band. Walk g_fvgs against close ± (g_atr14 × i_fvgProximityPct).
        // If any active flagged FVG overlaps, surface a standalone ·BPR
        // tag in BPR purple (no glyph, no state, no distance — just the
        // annotation; full cell would require synthesising data from a
        // pipeline the user doesn't expect to drive MSD cell contents).
        // bp confluence factor remains primary BPR surface (unconditional).
        if timeframe.in_seconds(_tfLabel) == timeframe.in_seconds() and array.size(g_fvgs) > 0 and not na(g_atr14)
            float _msd_fvgTol = g_atr14 * i_fvgProximityPct
            float _msd_fvgTop = close + _msd_fvgTol
            float _msd_fvgBot = close - _msd_fvgTol
            bool _msd_fvgInBprBlank = false
            for _fi = 0 to array.size(g_fvgs) - 1
                _fvg = array.get(g_fvgs, _fi)
                if _fvg.inBpr and _fvg.state != ZS_MITIGATED and _fvg.state != ZS_EXPIRED
                    if math.min(_msd_fvgTop, _fvg.priceTop) > math.max(_msd_fvgBot, _fvg.priceBottom)
                        _msd_fvgInBprBlank := true
                        break
            if _msd_fvgInBprBlank
                _msd_fvgTxt := "·BPR"
                _msd_fvgCol := BPR_COLOR
                _msd_fvgTooltip := "BPR overlap is active even though the MSD proxy has no direct FVG cell value. BPR marks overlapping imbalance zones and feeds confluence through the Zone pillar."
    table.cell(_tbl, 6, _msd_tableRow, _msd_fvgTxt,
         bgcolor = _msd_rowBg, text_color = _msd_fvgCol, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = _msd_fvgTooltip
         )

    table.cell(_tbl, 7, _msd_tableRow, _msd_phaseTxt,
         bgcolor = _msd_rowBg, text_color = _msd_phaseCol, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttMsdTrend(_tfLabel, array.get(g_msdPhaseDirection, _row))
         )

// trajectory display. Defensive na() guard handles g_impState[N] returning
// na on bars 0-2 (insufficient history); na propagation through int
// comparisons would otherwise produce literal "na" strings in the rendered
// load window. Returns "STRG"/"SLID"/"HLLW"/"ABSD"/"WEAK" for the 5
// classifier states; falls through to "WEAK" for unknown ints.
f_impAbbrev(int _state) =>
    na(_state) ? "--" : _state == IMP_STRONG ? "STRG" : _state == IMP_SOLID ? "SLID" : _state == IMP_HOLLOW ? "HLLW" : _state == IMP_ABSORBED ? "ABSD" : "WEAK"

// SOLID (displacement only), ABSORBED (volume without displacement),
// WEAK (neither). Renders direction arrow + 3-glyph state-tier sparkline
// + volume ratio + plain-language summary on the right. No UDT params;
// primitive globals (g_impState, g_impDirection, g_volRatio) only.
// g_impState -- STRONG=ascending peak / SOLID=rising / ABSORBED=descending
// Row index reflects the 4-row confluence layout.
f_renderImpRow(table _tbl) =>
    string _imp_dirStr = g_impDirection == 1 ? "↑" : g_impDirection == -1 ? "↓" : "--"
    color  _imp_col = g_impState == IMP_STRONG ? (g_impDirection == 1 ? COL_BULL : COL_BEAR) : g_impState == IMP_SOLID ? COL_NEUTRAL : g_impState == IMP_ABSORBED ? COL_AMBER : COL_TX_DIM
    string _imp_midTxt = f_impAbbrev(g_impState[3]) + " → " + f_impAbbrev(g_impState[2]) + " → " + f_impAbbrev(g_impState[1]) + " → NOW: " + f_impAbbrev(g_impState) + " " + _imp_dirStr + "  |  " + f_fmtVolPct(g_volRatio) + " volume"

    if i_showDashboard and i_showDetailRows and barstate.islast
        table.cell(_tbl, 0, 29, "IMP",
             bgcolor = COL_BG_LEFT_ANCHOR, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
             tooltip = f_ttMctxCell(TT_MCTX_IMP)
             )
        table.cell(_tbl, 1, 29, _imp_midTxt,
             bgcolor = COL_BG_ROW_CTX, text_color = _imp_col, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_left,
             tooltip = f_ttMctxCell(TT_MCTX_IMP)
             )
        table.merge_cells(_tbl, 1, 29, 3, 29)
        table.cell(_tbl, 4, 29, g_impState == IMP_STRONG or g_impState == IMP_SOLID ? "momentum present" : g_impState == IMP_ABSORBED ? "momentum absorbed" : "no impulse",
             bgcolor = color.new(COL_BG_ROW_CTX, 50), text_color = COL_TX_SECONDARY, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_right,
             tooltip = f_ttMctxCell(TT_MCTX_IMP)
             )
        table.merge_cells(_tbl, 4, 29, 7, 29)

// -- LIQ detail row 28 render.
// liquidity side (BSL/SSL/balanced). Quality scalar from Module 7
// LIQUIDITY shown when sweep ACTIVE. No UDT params; primitive globals
// shifts 27 -> 28 per CONFLUENCE 4-row layout cascade.
f_renderLiqRow(table _tbl) =>
    string _liq_sweepStr = g_sweepState == 1 ? "SWEEP ACTIVE" : g_sweepState == 2 ? "SWEEP AWAITING" : "no active sweep"
    string _liq_statusStr = g_liqStatus == 1 ? "BSL dominant" : g_liqStatus == -1 ? "SSL dominant" : "liquidity balanced"
    color  _liq_col = g_sweepState > 0 ? COL_AMBER : COL_TX_SECONDARY
    string _liq_midTxt = _liq_sweepStr + (g_sweepState == 1 ? "  |  Q:" + str.tostring(g_sweepQuality, "0") : "")

    table.cell(_tbl, 0, 30, "LIQ",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_LEFT_ANCHOR, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttMctxCell(TT_MCTX_LIQ)
         )
    table.cell(_tbl, 1, 30, _liq_midTxt,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_ROW, text_color = _liq_col, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_left,
         tooltip = f_ttMctxCell(TT_MCTX_LIQ)
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 1, 30, 3, 30)
    table.cell(_tbl, 4, 30, _liq_statusStr,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_ROW, text_color = COL_TX_SECONDARY, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_right,
         tooltip = f_ttMctxCell(TT_MCTX_LIQ)
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 4, 30, 7, 30)

// Liquidity magnet row. Right cell shows nearest-magnet identifier
// ("VWAP nearest" / "PDH nearest" / "PDL nearest") computed from current close.
// Missing magnets are treated as infinitely distant via sentinel value.
f_renderMagRow(table _tbl) =>
    // Distance strings (sign + arrow + % via f_fmtDistance). NA -> "PDH --"
    // sentinel; near-zero (< 0.05% of close) -> "AT" indicator.
    string _mag_pdhStr  = na(g_pdh)  ? "PDH --"  : math.abs(close - g_pdh)  < close * 0.0005 ? "PDH AT"  : "PDH "  + (close >= g_pdh  ? "↑" : "↓") + f_fmtDistance(math.abs(close - g_pdh))
    string _mag_vwapStr = na(g_vwap) ? "VWAP --" : math.abs(close - g_vwap) < close * 0.0005 ? "VWAP AT" : "VWAP " + (close >= g_vwap ? "↑" : "↓") + f_fmtDistance(math.abs(close - g_vwap))
    string _mag_pdlStr  = na(g_pdl)  ? "PDL --"  : math.abs(close - g_pdl)  < close * 0.0005 ? "PDL AT"  : "PDL "  + (close >= g_pdl  ? "↑" : "↓") + f_fmtDistance(math.abs(close - g_pdl))
    string _mag_midTxt  = _mag_pdhStr + "  ·  " + _mag_vwapStr + "  ·  " + _mag_pdlStr

    // Nearest-magnet identification. NA magnets treated as infinitely
    // distant (1e15 sentinel); resolved magnet wins. All-NA edge case
    // (extremely rare; pre-market with no prior session) -> "session pending".
    float _mag_distPdh  = na(g_pdh)  ? 1e15 : math.abs(close - g_pdh)
    float _mag_distVwap = na(g_vwap) ? 1e15 : math.abs(close - g_vwap)
    float _mag_distPdl  = na(g_pdl)  ? 1e15 : math.abs(close - g_pdl)
    float _mag_distMin  = math.min(_mag_distPdh, math.min(_mag_distVwap, _mag_distPdl))
    string _mag_nearestTxt = (_mag_distMin == 1e15           ? "session pending"  :
                              _mag_distMin == _mag_distVwap  ? "VWAP nearest"     :
                              _mag_distMin == _mag_distPdh   ? "PDH nearest"      :
                                                               "PDL nearest")

    // Row anchor (col 0) -- matches IMP/LIQ/BIAS left-anchor pattern.
    table.cell(_tbl, 0, 28, "MAG",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_LEFT_ANCHOR, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttMctxCell(TT_MCTX_MAG)
         )
    // Body cell (cols 1-3 merged) -- distance values, left-aligned.
    table.cell(_tbl, 1, 28, _mag_midTxt,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_ROW, text_color = COL_TX_SECONDARY, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_left,
         tooltip = f_ttMctxCell(TT_MCTX_MAG)
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 1, 28, 3, 28)
    // Right note (cols 4-7 merged) -- nearest-magnet identifier.
    table.cell(_tbl, 4, 28, _mag_nearestTxt,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_ROW, text_color = COL_TX_SECONDARY, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_right,
         tooltip = f_ttMctxCell(TT_MCTX_MAG)
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 4, 28, 7, 28)

// BIAS row with net-label classification (full alignment / leaning /
// scattered / mixed). Helper renders the shared mixed-direction glyph.
// Aggregation runs once per render call inside barstate.islast gate.
// Layout matches IMP/LIQ structural pattern with a col-0 "BIAS" label.
f_renderBiasRow(table _tbl) =>
    int _bias_bullCount  = 0
    int _bias_bearCount  = 0
    int _bias_mixedCount = 0
    for _bias_i = 0 to 6
        int _bias_d = array.get(g_msdPhaseDirection, _bias_i)
        if _bias_d == 1
            _bias_bullCount += 1
        else if _bias_d == -1
            _bias_bearCount += 1
        else
            _bias_mixedCount += 1

    string _bias_countsTxt = "BULL " + str.tostring(_bias_bullCount, "0") + " . BEAR " + str.tostring(_bias_bearCount, "0") + " . MIX " + str.tostring(_bias_mixedCount, "0")

    string _bias_netTxt = f_biasNetLabel(_bias_bullCount, _bias_bearCount, _bias_mixedCount)

    // (mixed-heavy wins, then full/leaning, then scattered, then tied).
    int _bias_maxDir = math.max(_bias_bullCount, _bias_bearCount)
    color _bias_dirCol = _bias_bullCount > _bias_bearCount ? COL_BULL : COL_BEAR
    color _bias_netCol = (_bias_mixedCount >= 3                     ? COL_TX_DIM       :
                          _bias_maxDir == 7                         ? _bias_dirCol     :
                          _bias_maxDir >= 5                         ? _bias_dirCol     :
                          _bias_maxDir >= 3                         ? COL_TX_SECONDARY :
                          _bias_bullCount == _bias_bearCount        ? COL_AMBER        :
                                                                      COL_TX_SECONDARY)

    table.cell(_tbl, 0, 31, "BIAS",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_LEFT_ANCHOR, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttMctxCell(TT_MCTX_BIAS)
         )
    table.cell(_tbl, 1, 31, _bias_countsTxt,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_ROW_CTX, text_color = COL_TX_PRIMARY, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_left,
         tooltip = f_ttMctxCell(TT_MCTX_BIAS)
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 1, 31, 3, 31)
    table.cell(_tbl, 4, 31, _bias_netTxt,
         bgcolor = color.new(COL_BG_ROW_CTX, 50), text_color = _bias_netCol, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_right,
         tooltip = f_ttMctxCell(TT_MCTX_BIAS)
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 4, 31, 7, 31)

// -- desktop footer row 30 render.
// current verdict level + UTC time right. Verdict summary derived from
// primary engine UDT (CONFLICT shortcut suppresses normal levelStr).
// Helper recomputes primary-engine resolution locally because the
// verdict cascade's _vb_primaryIsCont local is out of scope at the
// g_showConflictFlag, g_showContinuation, g_showReversal, g_primaryEngine
// CONFLUENCE 4-row layout cascade.
f_renderFooter(table _tbl, VerdictState _cont, VerdictState _rev) =>
    string _ft_leftTxt = "IID Dual v1.0  |  Community Edition"
    // Recompute primary-engine selection locally (not accessible from verdict block scope).
    bool _ft_conflict = g_showConflictFlag
    bool _ft_single   = (g_showContinuation != g_showReversal) and not _ft_conflict
    bool _ft_primaryIsCont = _ft_single ? g_showContinuation : g_primaryEngine == 1
    // Verdict summary from primary engine.
    // use new tier vocabulary via TIER_* constants (Module 16); only the
    string _ft_verdictSummary = _ft_conflict ? "CONFLICT" : (g_showContinuation or g_showReversal) ? (_ft_primaryIsCont ? _cont.levelStr : _rev.levelStr) : TIER_INACTIVE
    // UTC time as HH:mm format.
    string _ft_timeTxt = str.format_time(timenow, "HH:mm", "UTC") + " UTC"
    string _ft_rightTxt = "VERDICT " + _ft_verdictSummary + "  |  " + _ft_timeTxt

    table.cell(_tbl, 0, 32, _ft_leftTxt,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_HEADER, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashTiny, text_halign = text.align_left
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 0, 32, 3, 32)
    table.cell(_tbl, 4, 32, _ft_rightTxt,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_HEADER, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashTiny, text_halign = text.align_right
         )
    table.merge_cells(_tbl, 4, 32, 7, 32)

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 19.5 POSITION STRING -> position.*
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Maps i_dashPosition string to the Pine position.* constant.

f_positionFromString(string _disp_posStr) =>
    string _disp_result = position.top_right
    if _disp_posStr == "top_left"
        _disp_result := position.top_left
    else if _disp_posStr == "top_center"
        _disp_result := position.top_center
    else if _disp_posStr == "top_right"
        _disp_result := position.top_right
    else if _disp_posStr == "middle_left"
        _disp_result := position.middle_left
    else if _disp_posStr == "middle_right"
        _disp_result := position.middle_right
    else if _disp_posStr == "bottom_left"
        _disp_result := position.bottom_left
    else if _disp_posStr == "bottom_center"
        _disp_result := position.bottom_center
    else if _disp_posStr == "bottom_right"
        _disp_result := position.bottom_right
    _disp_result

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 19.6 DELIVERY STATE RENDERING
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

f_deliveryStateDisplay(int _disp_dsRaw) =>
    string _disp_text = "LOADING"
    color _disp_col   = COL_TX_MUTED
    if _disp_dsRaw == DS_EXPANSION
        _disp_text := "STRUCTURAL PHASE: >> EXPANSION"
        _disp_col  := COL_DS_BULL
    else if _disp_dsRaw == DS_CONTINUATION
        _disp_text := "STRUCTURAL PHASE: > CONTINUATION"
        _disp_col  := COL_DS_BULL
    else if _disp_dsRaw == DS_CONSOLIDATION
        _disp_text := "STRUCTURAL PHASE: = CONSOLIDATION"
        _disp_col  := COL_DS_DIM
    else if _disp_dsRaw == DS_PULLBACK
        _disp_text := "STRUCTURAL PHASE: < PULLBACK"
        _disp_col  := COL_DS_AMBER
    else if _disp_dsRaw == DS_REVERSAL
        _disp_text := "STRUCTURAL PHASE: << REVERSAL"
        _disp_col  := COL_DS_BEAR
    [_disp_text, _disp_col]

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 19.8 TABLE DECLARATION
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Single var table. Created once under barstate.isfirst when i_showDashboard
// table.delete destroys var table references).
// 33 rows provides generous headroom across brand, verdict, modifier, pending,
// grid headers, 7 TFs, VWAP, confluence, context, detail rows, and footer.
// Dynamic position arg in table.new at var-decl is permitted in Pine v6;
// render on barstate.islast only.
// Desktop table uses the user-selected dashboard position.
var table g_dashboard = table.new(
     position     = f_positionFromString(i_dashPosition),
     columns      = 8,
     rows         = 33,
     bgcolor      = color.new(COL_BG_BASE, i_dashOpacity),
     border_width = 0,
     border_color = color.new(COL_BG_HEADER, 50),
     frame_width  = 0)

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 19.9 BRAND STRIP (row 0)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Left cell: product identity + instrument ticker. Always rendered when
// dashboard is on.
// Right cell: chart-timeframe Delivery State indicator.
// Desktop width split: brand label left, delivery state right. Columns 0-3
// are reserved for the left cell and columns 4-7 for the right cell.

if i_showDashboard and barstate.islast
    // Brand strip: two-cell split via merge_cells (Solo / Precision pattern).
    // Left cell spans cols 0-3 carrying product identity + instrument.
    // Right cell spans cols 4-6 carrying Delivery State indicator.
    string _disp_brandTxt = "IID INSTITUTIONAL INTELLIGENCE  |  DUAL  |  " + syminfo.ticker
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 0, 0, _disp_brandTxt,
         bgcolor     = COL_BG_BRAND,
         text_color  = COL_TX_PRIMARY,
         text_size   = i_dashSize,
         text_halign = text.align_left
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 0, 0, 3, 0)

    // Right cell: chart-timeframe Delivery State indicator.
    [_disp_dsText, _disp_dsCol] = f_deliveryStateDisplay(g_chartTfPhase)
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 4, 0, _disp_dsText,
         bgcolor     = COL_BG_BRAND,
         text_color  = _disp_dsCol,
         text_size   = i_dashSize,
         text_halign = text.align_right,
         tooltip     = f_ttDeliveryState(g_chartTfPhase)
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 4, 0, 7, 0)

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
//   - Verdict banner: three-state cascade (single / dual / CONFLICT)
//   - Condition ring: simplified format "<circles> N/5 conditions met"
//   - Pending strip: live wiring from g_cwState / g_cwDir / g_sweepPoolPrice
// Unicode glyphs: this render block consumes glyphs from the 19.1.A GLYPH
// shared glyph constants.
// Row 1 = verdict banner top line  (or first engine's top line in dual mode)
// Row 2 = verdict banner ring      (or first engine's ring)
// Row 3 = second engine's top line (dual mode only)
// Row 4 = second engine's ring     (dual mode only)
// Row 6 = modifier strip
// Row 7 = pending strip            (conditional on CW state)

// ---- Banner routing precedence cascade ---------------------------------
// 1. g_showConflictFlag TRUE  -> CONFLICT banner, modifier suppressed
// 2. both cont & rev active   -> DUAL banner with engine labels
// 3. cont XOR rev active      -> SINGLE banner for the active engine
// 4. neither active           -> NO_TRADE banner (flat grey)
// Routing globals from Module 18: g_showContinuation, g_showReversal,
// g_showConflictFlag, g_primaryEngine, g_dominantEngine, g_bothEnginesActive.

//   (a) the 3-bar history buffers stay current when the dashboard is
//       hidden (i_showDashboard = false), and
//   (b) drift mutation happens AFTER engine UDT reassignment and BEFORE
//       banner render -- the assignment cascade is engine compute (lines
//       ~4640 / ~4880) -> drift mutation here -> banner render (line
//       ~7250+).
// Mutation must happen at main scope; Pine v6 prohibits scalar global
// mutation from inside function bodies. UDT field mutation on var globals
// at main scope is legal (same pattern as g_validation.dir := ... in
// Module 5 main scope).

// --- A.2 drift computation (BEFORE pushing current score to history) ---
// Buffer cap = 3. When buffer has >= 3 entries, element 0 is the score
// from 3 bars ago; drift3 = current - oldest. Below 3 entries (warmup),
// drift3 stays at the engine's default 0.0 -- f_driftStr renders flat
// arrow under threshold 0.5.
if array.size(g_contPctHist) >= 3
    g_verdictCont.drift3 := g_verdictCont.pct - array.get(g_contPctHist, 0)
if array.size(g_revPctHist) >= 3
    g_verdictRev.drift3 := g_verdictRev.pct - array.get(g_revPctHist, 0)

array.push(g_contPctHist, g_verdictCont.pct)
if array.size(g_contPctHist) > 3
    array.shift(g_contPctHist)
array.push(g_revPctHist, g_verdictRev.pct)
if array.size(g_revPctHist) > 3
    array.shift(g_revPctHist)

// Pushes current bar's per-engine active flag + direction into the four
// parallel ring buffers. Active threshold = level >= VL_READY (BUILDING
// NOT count as active for sparkline purposes -- distinct from the EARLY-
// tier threshold used by counter-engine label. Sparkline
// reads as "engine had meaningful presence on this bar" rather than
// "engine had any heartbeat". Buffer cap = SPARK_LEN (8); FIFO-shift on
// overflow. Consumed by f_renderBannerCentre row 6 sparkline render via
// 4-state glyph encoding (▲ cont-only / ▼ rev-only / ◆ both / · neither).
array.push(g_sparkContActive, g_verdictCont.level >= VL_READY)
if array.size(g_sparkContActive) > SPARK_LEN
    array.shift(g_sparkContActive)
array.push(g_sparkRevActive, g_verdictRev.level >= VL_READY)
if array.size(g_sparkRevActive) > SPARK_LEN
    array.shift(g_sparkRevActive)
array.push(g_sparkContDir, g_verdictCont.dir)
if array.size(g_sparkContDir) > SPARK_LEN
    array.shift(g_sparkContDir)
array.push(g_sparkRevDir, g_verdictRev.dir)
if array.size(g_sparkRevDir) > SPARK_LEN
    array.shift(g_sparkRevDir)

// --- A.1 alignment classification + SHIFTING window detection ---
// Step 1: compute raw classification (one of STRONG / DIVERGENT /
// Step 2: if 3-bar history buffer has >= 3 entries, compare oldest
//         against current raw -- mismatch fires SHIFTING override.
// Step 3: push current raw to buffer, FIFO shift if over cap.
// Step 4: existing bar-count tracker on the FINAL classification.
string _rawAlignThisBar = f_computeAlignment(g_verdictCont, g_verdictRev)
string _alignThisBar = _rawAlignThisBar
if array.size(g_alignRawHist) >= 3
    string _alignPrev3 = array.get(g_alignRawHist, 0)
    if _alignPrev3 != _rawAlignThisBar
        _alignThisBar := "SHIFTING"

array.push(g_alignRawHist, _rawAlignThisBar)
if array.size(g_alignRawHist) > 3
    array.shift(g_alignRawHist)

if _alignThisBar != g_alignLast
    g_alignBars := 0
    g_alignLast := _alignThisBar
else
    g_alignBars := g_alignBars + 1

if i_showDashboard and i_showVerdictBanner and barstate.islast
    // --- Determine active render state ---
    bool _vb_conflict = g_showConflictFlag
    bool _vb_dual     = g_showContinuation and g_showReversal and not _vb_conflict
    bool _vb_single   = (g_showContinuation != g_showReversal) and not _vb_conflict
    bool _vb_flat     = not (_vb_conflict or _vb_dual or _vb_single)

    // Primary engine resolves which engine's state drives modifier strip
    // and trade levels. Tie -> Continuation wins (by g_primaryEngine default).
    bool _vb_primaryIsCont = _vb_single ? g_showContinuation : g_primaryEngine == 1

    // --- State 3: CONFLICT banner ---
    if _vb_conflict
        f_renderVerdictConflict(g_dashboard, g_verdictCont, g_verdictRev)

    // --- State 2: DUAL banner (both engines, Show Both mode) ---
    if _vb_dual
        f_renderVerdictDual(g_dashboard, g_verdictCont, g_verdictRev, _vb_primaryIsCont)

    // --- State 1: SINGLE banner (one engine active) ---
    if _vb_single
        f_renderVerdictSingle(g_dashboard, g_verdictCont, g_verdictRev, _vb_primaryIsCont)

    // --- State 4: NO_TRADE banner (both engines inactive) ---
    // centre panel can render DUAL STAND-DOWN state name + dim engine
    if _vb_flat
        f_renderVerdictFlat(g_dashboard, g_verdictCont, g_verdictRev)

    // --- Modifier strip (row 6) -- suppressed on CONFLICT and NO_TRADE ---
    if i_showModifierStrip and (_vb_single or _vb_dual)
        f_renderModifierStrip(g_dashboard, g_verdictCont, g_verdictRev, _vb_primaryIsCont, _vb_conflict)

    // --- Confirmation Window pending strip (row 7) ---
    // Wires real content from Module 7 Confirmation Window state machine:
    //   g_cwState    : 1 = CW_ARMED (sweep fired, awaiting close confirmation)
    //                  2 = CW_CONFIRMING (close confirmation seen, awaiting follow-through)
    //   g_cwDir      : +1 bull sweep / -1 bear sweep
    //   g_cwStartBar : bar_index where sweep fired
    //   g_sweepPoolPrice : price of the swept pool
    // Only renders when state is ARMED or CONFIRMING.
    bool _vb_cwPending = (g_cwState == 1 or g_cwState == 2) and not na(g_cwStartBar)
    if i_showPendingStrip and _vb_cwPending
        f_renderPendingStrip(g_dashboard)
    else
        f_clearPendingStrip(g_dashboard)

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Intelligence briefing occupies rows 9-11 with header, card row, and spacer.
//   STATE card     -- cols 0-2 merged (3 cols, 37.5% width)
//   OBSERVED card  -- cols 3-5 merged (3 cols, 37.5% width)
//   TRACKING card  -- cols 6-7 merged (2 cols, 25% width)
// Each card = single cell with multi-line content (\n separators) per
// solid bgcolor + one text_color". Pine v6 cell auto-grows row height for
// multi-line text.
//   Line 1: Card label (STATE / OBSERVED / TRACKING)
//   Line 2: State name + direction glyph (STATE) / event narrative
//           (OBSERVED) / pending narrative (TRACKING)
//   Line 3: Meta line (condition count / "validated event" / bars remaining)
// Briefing tint rule:
//   STATE + OBSERVED match (reinforce dominant direction):
//     DUAL ACTIVITY       -> bull tint or bear tint by cont direction
//     DIVERGENT SIGNALS   -> AMBER + warning glyph
//     CONDITIONS RIPENING -> dim base / no tint
//     DUAL STAND-DOWN     -> dim grey
//   TRACKING always AMBER tinted (forward-looking framing always amber).
// STATE card content is composed inline; OBSERVED and TRACKING reuse the
// shared f_observedText / f_trackingText wording helpers.

if i_showDashboard and i_showBriefing and barstate.islast
    // Recompute primary-engine selection locally (verdict-block-scope
    // locals not accessible here).
    bool _ib_conflict = g_showConflictFlag
    bool _ib_single = (g_showContinuation != g_showReversal) and not _ib_conflict
    bool _ib_primaryIsCont = _ib_single ? g_showContinuation : g_primaryEngine == 1
    int _ib_level = _ib_primaryIsCont ? g_verdictCont.level : g_verdictRev.level
    int _ib_dir = _ib_primaryIsCont ? g_verdictCont.dir : g_verdictRev.dir
    int _ib_contDir = g_verdictCont.dir
    int _ib_revDir = g_verdictRev.dir
    string _ib_state = g_marketState

    // 80% transparency keeps tinted card visible against COL_BG_BASE
    // dashboard backdrop while maintaining sufficient contrast for primary
    // text. Direction-aware for trend/dual states; AMBER for transition
    // ripening/standdown.
    color _ib_cardBg = COL_BG_ROW
    color _ib_cardTx = COL_TX_PRIMARY
    if _ib_state == VOCAB_STATE_DUAL_DOWN
        _ib_cardTx := COL_TX_DIM
    else if _ib_state == VOCAB_STATE_DIVERGENT or _ib_state == VOCAB_STATE_REV_EMERG
        _ib_cardBg := color.new(COL_AMBER, 75)
    else if _ib_state == VOCAB_STATE_CONT_PHASE or _ib_state == VOCAB_STATE_DUAL_ACT
        _ib_cardBg := _ib_contDir == DIR_BULL ? color.new(COL_BULL, 75) : color.new(COL_BEAR, 75)
    else if _ib_state == VOCAB_STATE_COND_RIPE
        _ib_cardTx := COL_TX_SECONDARY
    // CONDITIONS RIPENING + default: COL_BG_ROW backdrop preserved

    // ("Cont X% . Rev Y%") for active states, "Both inactive" for DUAL
    // _ib_conds + _ib_stateGlyph + _ib_stateMeta composition.
    string _ib_stateTxt = f_stateCardDominance(_ib_state, _ib_contDir, _ib_revDir, g_verdictCont.pct, g_verdictRev.pct)

    // --- OBSERVED card content compose ---
    string _ib_obsContent = f_observedText(_ib_level, _ib_dir, na, na, na)
    string _ib_obsMeta = "validated event"
    string _ib_obsTxt = "OBSERVED\n" + _ib_obsContent + "\n" + _ib_obsMeta

    // --- TRACKING card content compose ---
    // Pending event plumbing from Module 7 Confirmation Window state
    // machine. With-pending narrative variant selected when CW is ARMED
    // (state 1) or CONFIRMING (state 2); otherwise without-pending fall
    // through.
    bool _ib_pending = (g_cwState == 1 or g_cwState == 2) and not na(g_cwStartBar)
    string _ib_trkEvent = _ib_pending ? "sweep at " + str.tostring(g_sweepPoolPrice, format.mintick) : na
    string _ib_trkCountdown = _ib_pending ? str.tostring(i_confirmationWindowBars - (bar_index - g_cwStartBar)) + "m" : na
    string _ib_trkKillzone = g_kzActive == KZ_NONE ? na : g_kzName
    string _ib_trkContent = f_trackingText(_ib_pending, _ib_trkEvent, _ib_trkCountdown, _ib_trkKillzone, _ib_level, _ib_dir)
    string _ib_trkMeta = _ib_pending ? str.tostring(i_confirmationWindowBars - (bar_index - g_cwStartBar)) + " of " + str.tostring(i_confirmationWindowBars) + " bars remaining" : "no pending events"
    string _ib_trkTxt = "TRACKING\n" + _ib_trkContent + "\n" + _ib_trkMeta

    // --- Row 8: INTELLIGENCE BRIEFING section header ---
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 0, 9, "INTELLIGENCE BRIEFING",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_BAND, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = TT_BRIEF_HEAD
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 0, 9, 7, 9)

    // --- Row 9: STATE card (cols 0-2) ---
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 0, 10, _ib_stateTxt,
         bgcolor = _ib_cardBg, text_color = _ib_cardTx, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttBannerCentre(g_marketState)
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 0, 10, 2, 10)

    table.cell(g_dashboard, 3, 10, _ib_obsTxt,
         bgcolor = _ib_cardBg, text_color = _ib_cardTx, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttBriefObs(g_validation.fired and not g_validation.stale)
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 3, 10, 5, 10)

    table.cell(g_dashboard, 6, 10, _ib_trkTxt,
         bgcolor = color.new(COL_AMBER, 80), text_color = COL_TX_PRIMARY, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttBriefTrk(_ib_trkTxt != STR_TRACKING_EMPTY)
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 6, 10, 7, 10)

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
//   - MSD header row (row 11)
//   - Context row amber highlight: applied when row TF matches i_tfContext
//   - VWAP row (row 19): real data from g_vwap / g_vwapPrev
//   - CONF row (row 20): real data from g_confFactors[15] + counters
//   - SWING H/L column (needs per-TF swing pivot detection in Module 4)
//   - STRUCTURE column (needs per-TF close history + per-TF swing pattern)
//   - ORDER BLOCK column (needs .tf field on Zone / BbRecord UDTs)
//   - FVG column (needs .tf field on Zone UDT)
// Unicode glyphs: this render block consumes the shared GLYPH constants.
// g_vwap / g_vwapPrev (for VWAP row), g_confFactors / g_confMet / g_confEnabled
// / g_confPercent / g_confTierStr (for CONF row).

// --- MSD 8-col header row (row 12) ---

if i_showDashboard and i_showMsdGrid and barstate.islast
    // TF header carries explicit width anchoring; remaining headers use
    // normal text size for scanability across the MSD grid.
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 0, 12, "TF",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_LEFT_ANCHOR, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         width = 1.4,
         tooltip = TT_COL_TF
         )
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 1, 12, "SWING H/L",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_BAND, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = TT_COL_SWING_HL
         )
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 2, 12, "STRUCTURE",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_BAND, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = TT_COL_STRUCTURE
         )
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 3, 12, "OB↑",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_BAND, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = TT_COL_OB_UP
         )
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 4, 12, "OB↓",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_BAND, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = TT_COL_OB_DN
         )
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 5, 12, "BB",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_BAND, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = TT_COL_BB
         )
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 6, 12, "FVG",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_BAND, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = TT_COL_FVG
         )
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 7, 12, "TREND",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_BAND, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = TT_COL_TREND
         )

// --- MSD body rows (rows 12-18) ---
// Each row corresponds to MSD index 0-6 (W/D/4H/1H/15M/5M/1M by default).
// Context row (matching i_tfContext) gets amber bg; all others use alternating
// row bg from COL_BG_ROW / COL_BG_ROW_ALT.

if i_showDashboard and i_showMsdGrid and barstate.islast
    // MSD row labels from user-configurable inputs.
    var array<string> _msd_tfLabels = array.from(i_msdTf1, i_msdTf2, i_msdTf3, i_msdTf4, i_msdTf5, i_msdTf6, i_msdTf7)
    var array<bool> _msdRowShows = array.from(i_msdRow1Show, i_msdRow2Show, i_msdRow3Show, i_msdRow4Show, i_msdRow5Show, i_msdRow6Show, i_msdRow7Show)

    // Render each visible structure row.

    for _msd_row = 0 to 6
        // f_msdPatternStr and f_msdRowBg compose helpers consumed inside helper body.
        if array.get(_msdRowShows, _msd_row)
            string _msd_tfLabel = array.get(_msd_tfLabels, _msd_row)
            f_renderMsdRow(g_dashboard, _msd_row, _msd_tfLabel)

// --- VWAP row (row 16) ---
// Label cell left, mid content, right content. Real data from Module 11
// (g_vwap, g_vwapPrev).

if i_showDashboard and i_showVwapRow and barstate.islast
    // Direction arrow vs prior VWAP.
    string _vwap_dirArrow = not na(g_vwapPrev) and not na(g_vwap) ? (g_vwap > g_vwapPrev ? "up" : g_vwap < g_vwapPrev ? "dn" : "--") : "--"
    // Distance from price to VWAP as % of close.
    string _vwap_distTxt = not na(g_vwap) ? f_fmtDistance(close - g_vwap) + " " + (close > g_vwap ? "above" : close < g_vwap ? "below" : "at") : "--"
    string _vwap_anchorLabel = i_vwapAnchor == "Session" ? "S" : i_vwapAnchor == "Daily" ? "D" : i_vwapAnchor == "Weekly" ? "W" : "M"
    string _vwap_midTxt = not na(g_vwap) ? _vwap_anchorLabel + ": " + str.tostring(g_vwap, format.mintick) + " " + _vwap_dirArrow + "  |  " + _vwap_distTxt : "VWAP --"
    // Prior-period anchor for right cell.
    string _vwap_rightTxt = not na(g_vwapPrev) ? "P" + _vwap_anchorLabel + ": " + str.tostring(g_vwapPrev, format.mintick) : "--"

    table.cell(g_dashboard, 0, 20, "VWAP",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_LEFT_ANCHOR, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = TT_VWAP_LABEL
         )
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 1, 20, _vwap_midTxt,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_ROW, text_color = COL_BULL, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_left
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 1, 20, 4, 20)
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 5, 20, _vwap_rightTxt,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_ROW, text_color = COL_BULL, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_right
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 5, 20, 7, 20)

// Confluence category buckets:
//   STRONG (>=67%) -> COL_TIER_STRONG  (bright green)
//   STANDARD (34-66%) -> COL_AMBER     (amber)
//   WEAK (<34%) -> COL_TIER_WEAK       (bright red)
//   ZONE      = ob (idx 0), fvg (1), bb (10), bpr (11), swp (12), ote (13), p/d (14)
//   REFERENCE = vwap (2), ema (5), pdh (8), pvw (9)
//   TIMING    = kz (3), bos (4), sw (6), vol (7)
// summary+bar / col 7 empty. Bar visually centres over REFERENCE column
// (cols 2-4) of factor breakdown row 22.

if i_showDashboard and i_showConfRow and barstate.islast
    int _conf_zoneCount = (array.get(g_confFactors, 0)  ? 1 : 0) + (array.get(g_confFactors, 1)  ? 1 : 0) + (array.get(g_confFactors, 10) ? 1 : 0) + (array.get(g_confFactors, 11) ? 1 : 0) + (array.get(g_confFactors, 12) ? 1 : 0) + (array.get(g_confFactors, 13) ? 1 : 0) + (array.get(g_confFactors, 14) ? 1 : 0)
    int _conf_refCount  = (array.get(g_confFactors, 2)  ? 1 : 0) + (array.get(g_confFactors, 5)  ? 1 : 0) + (array.get(g_confFactors, 8)  ? 1 : 0) + (array.get(g_confFactors, 9)  ? 1 : 0)
