    int _conf_timeCount = (array.get(g_confFactors, 3)  ? 1 : 0) + (array.get(g_confFactors, 4)  ? 1 : 0) + (array.get(g_confFactors, 6)  ? 1 : 0) + (array.get(g_confFactors, 7)  ? 1 : 0)

    color _conf_tierCol = g_confTier == CONF_TIER_STRONG ? COL_TIER_STRONG : g_confTier == CONF_TIER_STANDARD ? COL_AMBER : COL_TIER_WEAK
    color _conf_tierTint = color.new(_conf_tierCol, 90)

    // Summary text plus reversed-gradient confluence bar, centred via merged cell.
    // fired factors as structural-led / level-based / mixed using the 5/4/3
    // factor split. Suppressed (no suffix) when no factors fired -- avoids
    // "0/12 WEAK ░░░░  mixed" noise on blank state.
    string _conf_categoryStr = g_confMet > 0 ? "  " + f_confluenceCategory() : ""
    string _conf_summaryTxt = str.tostring(g_confMet) + "/" + str.tostring(g_confEnabled) + "  " + g_confTierStr + "  " + f_confBar(g_confMet, g_confEnabled) + _conf_categoryStr

    // Per-category factor strings -- bracket on met factors via f_factorBracket
    // (idx 0,1,10,11,12,13,14);
    // REFERENCE = vwap/ema/pdh/pvw (idx 2,5,8,9); TIMING = kz/bos/sw/vol
    // (idx 3,4,6,7).
    string _conf_zoneStr = _conf_zoneCount == 0 ? "inactive" : f_factorBracket(0) + f_factorBracket(1) + f_factorBracket(10) + f_factorBracket(11) + f_factorBracket(12) + f_factorBracket(13) + f_factorBracket(14)
    string _conf_refStr  = _conf_refCount  == 0 ? "inactive" : f_factorBracket(2) + f_factorBracket(5) + f_factorBracket(8) + f_factorBracket(9)
    string _conf_timeStr = _conf_timeCount == 0 ? "inactive" : f_factorBracket(3) + f_factorBracket(4) + f_factorBracket(6) + f_factorBracket(7)

    table.cell(g_dashboard, 0, 21, "CONFLUENCE",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_BAND, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = TT_CONF_HEAD
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 0, 21, 7, 21)

    table.cell(g_dashboard, 0, 22, "",
         bgcolor = _conf_tierTint, text_size = g_dashTiny)
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 1, 22, _conf_summaryTxt,
         bgcolor = _conf_tierTint, text_color = _conf_tierCol, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttConfSummary()
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 1, 22, 6, 22)
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 7, 22, "",
         bgcolor = _conf_tierTint, text_size = g_dashTiny)

    table.cell(g_dashboard, 0, 23, "ZONE . " + str.tostring(_conf_zoneCount) + "/7",
         bgcolor = _conf_tierTint, text_color = _conf_zoneCount > 0 ? COL_TIER_STRONG : COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = "Zone factors hit near active OB, FVG, BPR, BB, swing extremes, OTE, or clear premium/discount territory."
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 0, 23, 1, 23)
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 2, 23, "REFERENCE . " + str.tostring(_conf_refCount) + "/4",
         bgcolor = _conf_tierTint, text_color = _conf_refCount > 0 ? COL_AMBER : COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = "Reference factors activate near higher-timeframe anchors such as previous-day high/low, VWAP, weekly open, or midnight open."
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 2, 23, 4, 23)
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 5, 23, "TIMING . " + str.tostring(_conf_timeCount) + "/4",
         bgcolor = _conf_tierTint, text_color = _conf_timeCount > 0 ? COL_BLUE : COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = "Timing factors activate during major killzones and while recent BOS, CHoCH, or MSS events are still fresh."
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 5, 23, 7, 23)

    // size.small. Counter cells (row 22) stay size.normal; factor lines are
    // tier-2 supporting detail under the counters.
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 0, 24, _conf_zoneStr,
         bgcolor = _conf_tierTint, text_color = COL_TX_PRIMARY, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 0, 24, 1, 24)
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 2, 24, _conf_refStr,
         bgcolor = _conf_tierTint, text_color = COL_TX_PRIMARY, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 2, 24, 4, 24)
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 5, 24, _conf_timeStr,
         bgcolor = _conf_tierTint, text_color = COL_TX_PRIMARY, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 5, 24, 7, 24)

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Single section-band header introducing the chart-TF zone (5-cell detail
// BG_BAND tint matches the briefing, structure, and confluence section headers.

if i_showDashboard and i_showDetailStrip and barstate.islast
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 0, 25, "CHART TIMEFRAME INTELLIGENCE",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_BAND, text_color = COL_TX_DIM, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = TT_CTI_HEAD
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 0, 25, 7, 25)

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Single-row strip rendering 5 cells: VOLUME / SWING / VOLATILITY / SESSION
// / KILLZONE.
// SWING uses a 9-segment position bar; VOLATILITY uses ATR delta from
// g_atr14 / g_atrBaseline.

if i_showDashboard and i_showDetailStrip and barstate.islast
    // --- Compose strip parameters ---
    float _det_swingPct = na
    if not na(g_swingHigh) and not na(g_swingLow) and g_swingHigh > g_swingLow
        _det_swingPct := (close - g_swingLow) / (g_swingHigh - g_swingLow) * 100.0

    // Compact session labels leave room for the progress bar.
    string _det_sessShort = g_kzActive == KZ_ASIAN ? "ASIA" : g_kzActive == KZ_LONDON ? "LDN" : g_kzActive == KZ_NY_AM ? "NY" : g_kzActive == KZ_NY_PM ? "NY-PM" : g_kzActive == KZ_LUNCH ? "LUNCH" : "OFF"

    // _kzDurationMin computation (NY_PM = 120, all others = 180, KZ_NONE
    // = 0). When KZ_NONE the progress is undefined and the helper
    // fall-through renders "OFF-SESSION".
    int _det_kzDuration = (g_kzActive == KZ_NY_PM ? 120 :
                           g_kzActive == KZ_NONE ? 0 :
                           180)
    float _det_sessPct = (_det_kzDuration > 0 and not na(g_kzCountdown)) ? math.max(0.0, math.min(100.0, (_det_kzDuration - g_kzCountdown) * 100.0 / _det_kzDuration)) : na

    string _det_killzoneStr = g_kzActive == KZ_NONE ? "KZ NONE" : "KZ " + g_kzName + "  " + str.tostring(g_kzCountdown) + "m"

    // Text-only fallback array via f_chartTfStrip (used per-cell when underlying data is na).
    string _det_sessionStrLong  = g_kzActive == KZ_ASIAN ? "ASIAN" : g_kzActive == KZ_LONDON ? "LONDON" : g_kzActive == KZ_NY_AM ? "NEW YORK" : "OFF-SESSION"
    array<string> _det_cells = f_chartTfStrip(g_volRatio, _det_swingPct, na, _det_sessionStrLong, g_kzActive == KZ_NONE ? na : _det_killzoneStr)

    // --- Compose cell text strings: visual bars where data available, text fallback otherwise ---
    string _det_volTxt = not na(g_volRatio) ? "VOL  " + f_volumeBar(g_volRatio) + "  " + f_fmtVolPct(g_volRatio) : array.get(_det_cells, 0)
    string _det_pdLabel = na(_det_swingPct) ? "" : _det_swingPct > 60.0 ? " [PRM]" : _det_swingPct < 40.0 ? " [DSC]" : " [EQ]"
    string _det_oteLabel = na(_det_swingPct) ? "" : ((_det_swingPct >= 62.0 and _det_swingPct <= 79.0) or (_det_swingPct >= 21.0 and _det_swingPct <= 38.0)) ? " [OTE]" : ""
    string _det_swpLabel = na(_det_swingPct) ? "" : (_det_swingPct >= (100.0 - i_swingProxPct) or _det_swingPct <= i_swingProxPct) ? " [SWP]" : ""
    string _det_swgTxt = not na(_det_swingPct) ? "SWING  " + f_chartTfSwingBar(_det_swingPct, false) + "  " + f_fmtDistance(g_swingHigh - g_swingLow) + (i_pdLabelEnabled ? _det_pdLabel + _det_oteLabel + _det_swpLabel : "") : array.get(_det_cells, 1)
    string _det_vltTxt = "VLT  " + f_volatilityBar(g_atrDelta) + "  " + (g_atrDelta >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(g_atrDelta * 100, "0") + "%"
    string _det_sessTxt = not na(_det_sessPct) ? f_sessionProgressBar(_det_sessShort, _det_sessPct) : array.get(_det_cells, 3)
    string _det_kzTxt = _det_killzoneStr  // KZ always text-only

    // --- Per-cell tier colours ---
    color _det_volCol  = na(g_volRatio) ? COL_TX_MUTED : g_volRatio > 1.5 ? COL_BULL : g_volRatio > 0.8 ? COL_NEUTRAL : COL_AMBER
    color _det_swgCol  = na(_det_swingPct) ? COL_TX_MUTED : _det_swingPct > 66.0 ? COL_BEAR : _det_swingPct < 33.0 ? COL_BULL : COL_AMBER
    // Volatility colour mapped from same buckets as f_volatilityState.
    string _det_vltState = f_volatilityState(g_atrDelta)
    color _det_vltCol = (_det_vltState == "compression" ? COL_TX_MUTED :
                         _det_vltState == "cooling"     ? COL_NEUTRAL :
                         _det_vltState == "normal"      ? COL_NEUTRAL :
                         _det_vltState == "elevated"    ? COL_AMBER :
                                                          COL_BEAR)
    color _det_sessCol = g_kzActive == KZ_NONE ? COL_TX_DIM : COL_AMBER
    color _det_kzCol   = g_kzActive == KZ_NONE ? COL_TX_DIM : COL_AMBER

    // VOLUME cell (col 0).
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 0, 26, _det_volTxt,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_ROW, text_color = _det_volCol, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttCtiCell(TT_CTI_VOL)
         )

    // SWING cell (cols 1-2 merged).
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 1, 26, _det_swgTxt,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_ROW, text_color = _det_swgCol, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttCtiCell(TT_CTI_SWING)
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 1, 26, 2, 26)

    table.cell(g_dashboard, 3, 26, _det_vltTxt,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_ROW, text_color = _det_vltCol, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttCtiCell(TT_CTI_VLT)
         )

    // SESSION cell (cols 4-5 merged).
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 4, 26, _det_sessTxt,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_ROW, text_color = _det_sessCol, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttCtiCell(TT_CTI_MID_LUNCH)
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 4, 26, 5, 26)

    table.cell(g_dashboard, 6, 26, _det_kzTxt,
         bgcolor = COL_BG_ROW, text_color = _det_kzCol, text_size = i_dashSize, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = f_ttCtiCell(TT_CTI_KZ)
         )

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// PDH / VWAP / PDL distances from close. Single full-width merged cell
// cell). Sign convention: close minus level -- close above = positive, close
// below = negative. Data sources g_pdh / g_vwap / g_pdl declared in Module 11

if i_showDashboard and i_showDetailStrip and barstate.islast
    // MARKET CONTEXT section banner.
    table.cell(g_dashboard, 0, 27, "MARKET CONTEXT",
         bgcolor = COL_BG_BAND, text_color = COL_TX_SECONDARY, text_size = g_dashNormal, text_halign = text.align_center,
         tooltip = TT_MCTX_HEAD
         )
    table.merge_cells(g_dashboard, 0, 27, 7, 27)

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Four rows showing liquidity magnets, impulse quality, liquidity state, and
// aggregate bias under the row 27 "MARKET CONTEXT" section banner. All four
// helpers share the same left-anchor/body/right-note layout.
// i_showDetailRows controls all four rows as a group.

if i_showDashboard and i_showDetailRows and barstate.islast
    // --- Row 28: MAG (Liquidity Magnets) -- gated with IMP/LIQ/BIAS as unified group ---
    f_renderMagRow(g_dashboard)

f_renderImpRow(g_dashboard)

if i_showDashboard and i_showDetailRows and barstate.islast
    // --- Row 30: LIQ -- real ---
    f_renderLiqRow(g_dashboard)
    // --- Row 31: BIAS (aggregate) -- real, via f_biasNetLabel ---
    f_renderBiasRow(g_dashboard)

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

if i_showDashboard and i_showFooter and barstate.islast
    f_renderFooter(g_dashboard, g_verdictCont, g_verdictRev)


// --- Module 19 DISPLAY end ---
// Desktop dashboard remains the sole dashboard render surface.
// Dashboard position now follows i_dashPosition unconditionally.
// Delivery-state display text is desktop-only.
// Exposes: g_dashboard, colour tokens, display strings, and shared glyphs.

//#endregion MODULE 19

//#region MODULE 20 -- ALERTS
// ============================================================================
// MODULE 20 -- ALERTS
// ============================================================================
// Alerts fire on confirmed bars with separate cooldowns for engine, sweep,
// phase, swing-proximity, and OTE-zone events.

f_alertCooldownEngine() =>
    int _m = timeframe.isminutes ? timeframe.multiplier : 0
    timeframe.isminutes ? (_m <= 1 ? 8 : _m <= 5 ? 4 : _m <= 15 ? 3 : _m <= 60 ? 2 : 1) : 1

f_alertCooldownSweep() =>
    int _m = timeframe.isminutes ? timeframe.multiplier : 0
    timeframe.isminutes ? (_m <= 1 ? 5 : _m <= 5 ? 3 : _m <= 15 ? 2 : 1) : 1

f_alertCooldownPhase() =>
    int _m = timeframe.isminutes ? timeframe.multiplier : 0
    timeframe.isminutes ? (_m <= 1 ? 10 : _m <= 5 ? 6 : _m <= 15 ? 4 : _m <= 30 ? 3 : _m <= 60 ? 2 : 1) : 1

f_alertCanFire(int _lastBar, int _cooldownBars) =>
    na(_lastBar) or bar_index - _lastBar >= _cooldownBars

var int g_alertContCnfLastBar = na
var int g_alertRevCnfLastBar = na
var int g_alertSweepReclaimLastBar = na
var int g_alertPhaseExpansionLastBar = na
var int g_alertPhasePullbackLastBar = na
var int g_alertPhaseConsolidationLastBar = na
var int g_alertPhaseContinuationLastBar = na
var int g_alertPhaseReversalLastBar = na
var int g_alertSwingNearHighLastBar = na
var int g_alertSwingNearLowLastBar = na
var int g_alertOteDiscountLastBar = na
var int g_alertOtePremiumLastBar = na

_alertEngineCooldown = f_alertCooldownEngine()
_alertSweepCooldown = f_alertCooldownSweep()
_alertPhaseCooldown = f_alertCooldownPhase()

_contCnfEntry = barstate.isconfirmed and i_enableContinuation and g_verdictCont.level == VL_EXECUTE and (g_verdictCont[1]).level != VL_EXECUTE
_revCnfEntry  = barstate.isconfirmed and i_enableReversal    and g_verdictRev.level  == VL_EXECUTE and (g_verdictRev[1]).level  != VL_EXECUTE
_sweepReclaimEntry = barstate.isconfirmed and g_cwState == CW_CONFIRMED and g_cwState[1] != CW_CONFIRMED

_phaseExpansionEntry = barstate.isconfirmed and g_chartTfPhase == DS_EXPANSION and g_chartTfPhase[1] != DS_EXPANSION
_phasePullbackEntry = barstate.isconfirmed and g_chartTfPhase == DS_PULLBACK and g_chartTfPhase[1] != DS_PULLBACK
_phaseConsolidationEntry = barstate.isconfirmed and g_chartTfPhase == DS_CONSOLIDATION and g_chartTfPhase[1] != DS_CONSOLIDATION
_phaseContinuationEntry = barstate.isconfirmed and g_chartTfPhase == DS_CONTINUATION and g_chartTfPhase[1] != DS_CONTINUATION
_phaseReversalEntry = barstate.isconfirmed and g_chartTfPhase == DS_REVERSAL and g_chartTfPhase[1] != DS_REVERSAL

float _alertSwingPct = na
if not na(g_swingHigh) and not na(g_swingLow) and g_swingHigh > g_swingLow
    _alertSwingPct := (close - g_swingLow) / (g_swingHigh - g_swingLow) * 100.0

bool _swingJustChanged = g_newSwingHighFired or g_newSwingLowFired
_swingNearHighNow = not na(_alertSwingPct) and _alertSwingPct >= (100 - i_swingProxPct)
_swingNearHighEntry = barstate.isconfirmed and _swingNearHighNow and not _swingNearHighNow[1] and not _swingJustChanged
_swingNearLowNow = not na(_alertSwingPct) and _alertSwingPct <= i_swingProxPct
_swingNearLowEntry = barstate.isconfirmed and _swingNearLowNow and not _swingNearLowNow[1] and not _swingJustChanged
_oteDiscountNow = not na(_alertSwingPct) and _alertSwingPct >= 21.0 and _alertSwingPct <= 38.0
_oteDiscountEntry = barstate.isconfirmed and _oteDiscountNow and not _oteDiscountNow[1] and not _swingJustChanged
_otePremiumNow = not na(_alertSwingPct) and _alertSwingPct >= 62.0 and _alertSwingPct <= 79.0
_otePremiumEntry = barstate.isconfirmed and _otePremiumNow and not _otePremiumNow[1] and not _swingJustChanged

_contCnfFired = _contCnfEntry and f_alertCanFire(g_alertContCnfLastBar, _alertEngineCooldown)
_revCnfFired = _revCnfEntry and f_alertCanFire(g_alertRevCnfLastBar, _alertEngineCooldown)
_sweepReclaimFired = _sweepReclaimEntry and f_alertCanFire(g_alertSweepReclaimLastBar, _alertSweepCooldown)

_phaseExpansionFired = _phaseExpansionEntry and f_alertCanFire(g_alertPhaseExpansionLastBar, _alertPhaseCooldown)
_phasePullbackFired = _phasePullbackEntry and f_alertCanFire(g_alertPhasePullbackLastBar, _alertPhaseCooldown)
_phaseConsolidationFired = _phaseConsolidationEntry and f_alertCanFire(g_alertPhaseConsolidationLastBar, _alertPhaseCooldown)
_phaseContinuationFired = _phaseContinuationEntry and f_alertCanFire(g_alertPhaseContinuationLastBar, _alertPhaseCooldown)
_phaseReversalFired = _phaseReversalEntry and f_alertCanFire(g_alertPhaseReversalLastBar, _alertPhaseCooldown)
_swingNearHighFired = _swingNearHighEntry and f_alertCanFire(g_alertSwingNearHighLastBar, _alertPhaseCooldown)
_swingNearLowFired = _swingNearLowEntry and f_alertCanFire(g_alertSwingNearLowLastBar, _alertPhaseCooldown)
_oteDiscountFired = _oteDiscountEntry and f_alertCanFire(g_alertOteDiscountLastBar, _alertPhaseCooldown)
_otePremiumFired = _otePremiumEntry and f_alertCanFire(g_alertOtePremiumLastBar, _alertPhaseCooldown)

if _contCnfFired
    g_alertContCnfLastBar := bar_index
if _revCnfFired
    g_alertRevCnfLastBar := bar_index
if _sweepReclaimFired
    g_alertSweepReclaimLastBar := bar_index
if _phaseExpansionFired
    g_alertPhaseExpansionLastBar := bar_index
if _phasePullbackFired
    g_alertPhasePullbackLastBar := bar_index
if _phaseConsolidationFired
    g_alertPhaseConsolidationLastBar := bar_index
if _phaseContinuationFired
    g_alertPhaseContinuationLastBar := bar_index
if _phaseReversalFired
    g_alertPhaseReversalLastBar := bar_index
if _swingNearHighFired
    g_alertSwingNearHighLastBar := bar_index
if _swingNearLowFired
    g_alertSwingNearLowLastBar := bar_index
if _oteDiscountFired
    g_alertOteDiscountLastBar := bar_index
if _otePremiumFired
    g_alertOtePremiumLastBar := bar_index

alertcondition(_contCnfFired,
     title   = "IID Dual: Continuation confirmed",
     message = "Continuation confirmed on {{ticker}} {{interval}}.")

alertcondition(_revCnfFired,
     title   = "IID Dual: Reversal confirmed",
     message = "Reversal confirmed on {{ticker}} {{interval}}.")

alertcondition(_sweepReclaimFired,
     title   = "IID Dual: Sweep reclaim confirmed",
     message = "Sweep reclaim confirmed on {{ticker}} {{interval}}.")

alertcondition(_phaseExpansionFired,
     title   = "IID Dual: Expansion phase",
     message = "Structural phase is now Expansion on {{ticker}} {{interval}}.")

alertcondition(_phasePullbackFired,
     title   = "IID Dual: Pullback phase",
     message = "Structural phase is now Pullback on {{ticker}} {{interval}}.")

alertcondition(_phaseConsolidationFired,
     title   = "IID Dual: Consolidation phase",
     message = "Structural phase is now Consolidation on {{ticker}} {{interval}}.")

alertcondition(_phaseContinuationFired,
     title   = "IID Dual: Continuation phase",
     message = "Structural phase is now Continuation on {{ticker}} {{interval}}.")

alertcondition(_phaseReversalFired,
     title   = "IID Dual: Reversal phase",
     message = "Structural phase is now Reversal on {{ticker}} {{interval}}.")

alertcondition(_swingNearHighFired,
     title   = "IID Dual: Swing near high",
     message = "Price entered swing-high proximity on {{ticker}} {{interval}}.")

alertcondition(_swingNearLowFired,
     title   = "IID Dual: Swing near low",
     message = "Price entered swing-low proximity on {{ticker}} {{interval}}.")

alertcondition(_oteDiscountFired,
     title   = "IID Dual: OTE discount zone",
     message = "Price entered the OTE discount zone (21-38%) on {{ticker}} {{interval}}.")

alertcondition(_otePremiumFired,
     title   = "IID Dual: OTE premium zone",
     message = "Price entered the OTE premium zone (62-79%) on {{ticker}} {{interval}}.")
